function void buildHolos(){
    # Prism (holo 1) is bottom-docked to BaseEnt's bottom: its center sits so the
    # prism's lower edge rests flush on the base's bottom and can't drop below it.
    holoCreate(1, BaseEnt:toWorld(vec(BaseEnt:boxSize():x()/2 + -4, 0, -BaseEnt:boxSize():z()/2 + 0.4/2)), vec(0.4,0.01,0.4), BaseEnt:toWorld(ang(0,90,90)), vec(255), "prism")
    holoParent(1, BaseEnt)

    holoCreate(3, vec(), vec(0.0), ang(), vec(0,0,255), "sphere")
    holoCreate(4, vec(), vec(0.0), ang(), vec(0,255,0), "sphere")
    holoCreate(6, vec(), vec(0), ang(), vec(255,0,255), "sphere")
    holoCreate(7, vec(), vec(0.0), ang(), vec(255,255,0), "sphere")

    holoCreate(10001, entity():toWorld(vec(0,0,entity():boxSize():z()-0.4)), vec(0.2,0.2,0.05), entity():toWorld(ang()), vec(255,0,0), "models/holograms/hq_cylinder.mdl")
    holoCreate(10002, entity():toWorld(vec(0,0,entity():boxSize():z()-0.4)), vec(0.2,0.2,0.05), entity():toWorld(ang()), vec(0,255,0), "models/holograms/hq_cylinder.mdl")
    holoParent(10001, entity())
    holoParent(10002, entity())
    holoAlpha(10001, 0)
    holoAlpha(10002, 0)
    holoDisableShading(10001,1)
    holoDisableShading(10002,1)

    holoCreate(500, BaseEnt:toWorld(vec(0,0,0)), vec(0), BaseEnt:toWorld(ang()), vec(), "models/holograms/hq_cylinder.mdl")
    holoDisableShading(500,1)
    holoParent(500, BaseEnt)




    holoCreate(8500, BaseEnt:toWorld(vec(Frint_back,0,Up_Down_turn_plate)), vec(0), BaseEnt:toWorld(ang()), vec(255), "cube")
    holoParent(8500, BaseEnt)

    # entity parents to. Lets you offset the actual gun prop from the pivot via
    # Gun_Offset (z = up/down) without touching the pivot or any gun children.

    # --- PRIMARY COG (drives the servo) ---
    holoCreate(999888, holoEntity(8600):toWorld(vec(0,0,holoEntity(8600):boxSize():z()-28.0)), vec(0.4,0.4,0.1), holoEntity(8600):toWorld(ang(0,0,0)), vec(255), "models/gears/gear_24.mdl")

    holoParent(9998878, holoEntity(8500))
    holoMaterial(9998878, "models/props_canal/canal_bridge_railing_01a")
    
}

function vector calculateBoltIntercept(ShooterPos:vector, TargetPos:vector, TgtVel:vector, Speed:number){
    local RelativePos = TargetPos - ShooterPos
    local Distance = RelativePos:length()

    if(Speed <= 0){
        #InterceptTime = 0 keep in case!
        return TargetPos
    }

    if(Distance < 1){
       # InterceptTime = 0 same for this keep in case!
        return TargetPos
    }

    if(TgtVel:length() < 1){
        #InterceptTime = Distance / Speed #keep in case 
        return TargetPos
    }

    local PredictedPos = TargetPos
    local PrevTime = Distance / Speed

    for(I = 1, 4){
        I = I

        local PredictDistance = (PredictedPos - ShooterPos):length()
        local TimeToTarget = PredictDistance / Speed

        if(TimeToTarget <= 0){
            TimeToTarget = PrevTime
        }

        if(TimeToTarget > 10){
            TimeToTarget = 10
        }

        # Velocity-only prediction.
        # Do NOT use TgtAccel here because acceleration noise
        # causes the bolt aim point to jitter.

        local NewPredictedPos = TargetPos + TgtVel * TimeToTarget

        if(abs(TimeToTarget - PrevTime) < 0.001){
            PredictedPos = NewPredictedPos
            PrevTime = TimeToTarget
            break
        }

        PredictedPos = NewPredictedPos
        PrevTime = TimeToTarget
    }

      #InterceptTime = PrevTime Keep tiul we fix 

    if(!BoltInterceptInitialized){
        BoltLastInterceptPos = PredictedPos
        BoltInterceptInitialized = 1
        return PredictedPos
    }

    local InterceptChange = (PredictedPos - BoltLastInterceptPos):length()

    if(InterceptChange < 4){
        return BoltLastInterceptPos
    }

    local BoltSmooth = 0.25

    BoltLastInterceptPos = BoltLastInterceptPos
        + (PredictedPos - BoltLastInterceptPos) * BoltSmooth

    return BoltLastInterceptPos
}


function vector calculateHitscanIntercept(TargetPos:vector, TgtVel:vector, TgtAccel:vector){
    if(TgtVel:length() < HitscanMinSpeed){
        return TargetPos
    }

    local ShootingPoint = Gun:pos()
    if(holoEntity(4580):isValid()){
        ShootingPoint = holoEntity(4580):pos()
    }
    local Distance = (TargetPos - ShootingPoint):length()

    # Hitscan lead is for turret tracking delay, so lead mostly the component
    # moving across the sight line. Leading radial motion causes overshoot.
    local DesiredAim = (TargetPos - ShootingPoint):normalized()
    local LateralVel = TgtVel - DesiredAim * TgtVel:dot(DesiredAim)
    local PredictTime = HitscanPrediction

    # Ramp lead down for CLOSE targets to avoid aiming way ahead of nearby flyers.
    # A near target (e.g. a scanner) shouldn't be led by a full prediction time - the
    # closer it is, the less lead it needs before it's already on top of you.
    if(HitscanMinLeadDist > 0 && Distance < HitscanMinLeadDist){
        PredictTime = PredictTime * (Distance / HitscanMinLeadDist)
    }

    # Smooth, continuous turn-lag compensation instead of a hard 15 cliff.
    if(HitscanTurnComp > 0){
        local CurrentAim = GunHoloForward
        local AngleDiff = acos(clamp(CurrentAim:dot(DesiredAim), -1, 1)) * 180 / _PI
        PredictTime = PredictTime + (AngleDiff / 180) * HitscanTurnComp
    }

    # Optional: real bullet travel time on top, if your gun isn't truly instant.
    if(HitscanBulletSpeed > 0){
        PredictTime = PredictTime + Distance / HitscanBulletSpeed
    }

    # Keep prediction bounded during sudden target direction changes.
    PredictTime = clamp(PredictTime, 0, 0.5)
    local PredictedPos = TargetPos + LateralVel * PredictTime

    if(HitscanAccelComp > 0 && TgtAccel:length() > 2){
        local LateralAccel = TgtAccel - DesiredAim * TgtAccel:dot(DesiredAim)
        PredictedPos = PredictedPos + LateralAccel * PredictTime^2 * HitscanAccelComp
    }

   # InterceptTime = PredictTime #keep til fixed

    return PredictedPos
}


function number canFire(){
    if(!TurretActive_Real || !Power_Available || Battery_Fire_Paused || Retargeting){ return 0 }
    if(Battery_Percent <= Fire_Pause_Threshold){ return 0 }
    if(!Target:isValid() || !Target:isAlive()){ return 0 }
    return 1
}

function number canScan(){
    # Ask the chip whether another find query fits THIS tick's budget (sphere costs 1).
    # Previous gating used findCount(), which counts finds ALREADY executed this tick -
    # so when nothing else ran a find yet (battery/mag already seated) it returned 0 and
    # starved the scan; only a chip reset cleared it. findCanQuery() is the correct check.
    return findCanQuery() ? 1 : 0
}

function void displayAmmoError(Message:string) {
    if(curtime() > Last_Warning_Time + 3) {
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[AMMO ERROR] " + Message)
        Gun:soundPlay(10, 0.8, "@weapons/pistol/pistol_empty.wav")
        Last_Warning_Time = curtime()
    }
}

function void idleSearchBeepTick(){
    if(!IdleSearchBeep_Enabled){ return }
    if(!TurretActive_Real || !Power_Available || HasValidTarget || Target:isValid()){
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep") }
        return
    }
    # Still searching  play the beep
    BaseEnt:emitSound(IdleSearchBeep_Sound, IdleSearchBeep_Level, IdleSearchBeep_Pitch, IdleSearchBeep_Volume)
}

function void displayRates(){
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "========== LAMBDA TIMER RATES ==========")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "aim   : " + Aim_Rate + " s  (" + round(1 / max(Aim_Rate, 0.001)) + " Hz)")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "scan  : " + Scan_Rate + " s  (" + round(1 / max(Scan_Rate, 0.001)) + " Hz)")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "los   : " + LOS_Rate + " s")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "power : " + Power_Rate + " s")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "status: " + Status_Rate + " s")
    printColor(vec(200,200,200), "Commands: !setaimrate <n>, !setscanrate <n>")
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "========================================")
}

function void displayTargetList(){
    printColor(vec(255,150,0), "========== TARGET LIST STATUS ==========")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "TARGET PLAYERS (" + TargetPlayers:count() + "):")
    if(TargetPlayers:count() > 0){
        foreach(PlayerName:string, Value:number = TargetPlayers){ if(Value){}
            printColor(vec(255,100,100), "   " + PlayerName)
        }
    }else{
        printColor(vec(100,255,100), "  (No specific players targeted)")
    }

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "list_npcs")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "list_npcs") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "list_npcs", List_delay / 1000, function(){
        print("")
        printColor(vec(255,255,0), "TARGET NPCs (" + TargetNPCs:count() + "):")
        if(TargetNPCs:count() > 0){
            foreach(NPCType:string, Value:number = TargetNPCs){ if(Value){}
                printColor(vec(255,100,100), "   " + NPCType)
            }
        }else{
            printColor(vec(100,255,100), "  (No NPCs targeted)")
        }

        timer(TimerPrefix + "list_vehicles", List_delay / 1000, function(){
            print("")
            printColor(vec(255,255,0), "TARGET VEHICLES (" + TargetVehicles:count() + "):")
            if(TargetVehicles:count() > 0){
                foreach(VehicleType:string, Value:number = TargetVehicles){ if(Value){}
                    printColor(vec(255,100,100), "   " + VehicleType)
                }
            }else{
                printColor(vec(100,255,100), "  (No vehicles targeted)")
            }

            timer(TimerPrefix + "list_custom", List_delay / 1000, function(){
                print("")
                printColor(vec(255,255,0), "CUSTOM TARGETS (" + TargetCustom:count() + "):")
                if(TargetCustom:count() > 0){
                    foreach(CustomType:string, Value:number = TargetCustom){ if(Value){}
                        printColor(vec(255,100,100), "   " + CustomType)
                    }
                }else{
                    printColor(vec(100,255,100), "  (No custom targets)")
                }
                printColor(vec(255,150,0), "=======================================")
                printColor(vec(200,200,200), "Commands: !targets, !queue, !rates")
            })
        })
    })
}

function void displayTargetingSettings(){

}

function void displayWhitelist(){
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "========== WHITELIST STATUS ==========")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "PROTECTED PLAYERS (" + WhitelistPlayers:count() + "):")
    if(WhitelistPlayers:count() > 0){
        foreach(PlayerName:string, Value:number = WhitelistPlayers){ if(Value){}
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "   " + PlayerName)
        }
    }else{
        printColor(vec(255,100,100), "  (No players whitelisted)")
    }
    print("")
    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "PROTECTED NPCs")
    if(WhitelistNPCs:count() > 0){
        foreach(NPCType:string, Value:number = WhitelistNPCs){ if(Value){}
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "   " + NPCType)
        }
    }else{
        printColor(vec(255,100,100), "  (No NPCs whitelisted)")
    }
}

function void dropFromQueue(Ent:entity){
    for(I = TargetQueueSize, 1, -1){
        if(TargetQueue[I, entity] == Ent){
            TargetQueue:removeEntity(I)
            TargetQueueScore:removeNumber(I)
            TargetQueueSize--
        }
    }
    TargetCount = TargetQueueSize
}

function void ejectBrass() {
    if(!EnableBrass) { return }
    local EjectHolo = holoEntity(BrassEjectHolo)
    local EjectPos = EjectHolo:pos()
    local EjectAng = EjectHolo:angles()
    local Effect = effect()
    Effect:setOrigin(EjectPos)
    Effect:setAngles(EjectAng)
    Effect:setNormal(EjectHolo:right())
    Effect:play("ShellEject")
}

# Keep the muzzle flash origin (holo 4581) synced to the active gun mode's
# per-mode position/angle arrays every tick, so tuning them live takes effect.
function void syncMuzzleFlash(){
    if(!holoEntity(4581):isValid()){ return }
    local Mode = MachineGunMode + 1
    holoPos(4581, holoEntity(20):toWorld(MuzzlePosA[Mode, vector]*Scale))
    holoAng(4581, holoEntity(20):toWorld(MuzzleAngA[Mode, angle]))
    holoEntity(4581):propDraw(Debug_Shoot)
}

# Dedicated bolt muzzle (holo 4583). Its local transform is set once at
# creation, after it is parented to holo 20. This makes vec(0,0,0) mean the
# exact origin of holo 20, with no world-to-local parenting ambiguity.
# Do not update it every tick: the parent already supplies the gun's aim.
function void syncBoltMuzzle(){
    if(!holoEntity(4583):isValid()){ return }
    holoEntity(4583):propDraw(Debug_Shoot)
}

function void doMuzzleFlashEffect(){
    if(!holoEntity(4581):isValid()){ return }
    local Mode = MachineGunMode + 1
    local EffName = MuzzleEffNameA[Mode, string]
    local EffScale = MuzzleEffScaleA[Mode, number]
    local Effect = effect()
    Effect:setOrigin(holoEntity(4581):pos())
    Effect:setAngles(holoEntity(4581):angles())
    Effect:setNormal(holoEntity(4581):forward())
    Effect:setScale(EffScale)
    if(Gun:isValid()){ Effect:setEntity(Gun) }
    Effect:play(EffName)
}

function void pulseMuzzleLight(){
    if(!MuzzleFlash_Enabled || !holoEntity(4581):isValid()){ return }
    local FlashPos = holoEntity(4581):pos()
    lightCreate(LightBase + 1, FlashPos, MuzzleLightColor, MuzzleLightDistance, MuzzleLightBrightness)
    lightParent(LightBase + 1, holoEntity(4581))
    lightToggle(LightBase + 1, 1)
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "muzzle_light_off")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "muzzle_light_off") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "muzzle_light_off", max(min(PewPewDelay * 0.45, 0.08), 0.015), function(){
        lightToggle(LightBase + 1, 0)
    })
}



function void fireStop(){
    Fire = 0
    Fire_Pending = 0
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "muzzle_light_off")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "muzzle_light_off") }
    lightToggle(LightBase + 1, 0)
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "fire_on")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "fire_on") }
    if(timerExists("Fire_Bolt")){ stoptimer("Fire_Bolt") }
    BoltChainActive = 0
}

function void fireStopDelayed(){
    if(!Fire && !Fire_Pending){ return }
    Fire_Pending = 0
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "fire_on")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "fire_on") }
    if(timerExists("Fire_Bolt")){ stoptimer("Fire_Bolt") }
    BoltChainActive = 0
    if(Delay_off <= 0){
        Fire = 0
        return
    }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "fire_off")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "fire_off") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "fire_off", Delay_off, function(){
        Fire = 0
    })
}

function vector getAimPoint(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid()){ return vec() }
    # Always aim at the physics mass center, including tiny NPCs such as birds
    # and headcrabs. Fall back to the entity origin if no center is available.
    local Center = Ent:massCenter()
    return Center:length() > 0 ? Center : Ent:pos()
}

function entity getAmmoEntity() {
    if(MagEnt:isValid()) { return MagEnt }
    if(MagEntIndex > 0) {
        local E = entity(MagEntIndex)
        if(E:isValid()) {
            MagEnt = E
            return E
        }
    }
    if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        MagEnt = Ammo_Prop:entity()
        MagEntIndex = MagEnt:id()
        return MagEnt
    }
    return noentity()
}

function number getDistanceToReloadProp() {
    if(Reload_Prop:entity():isValid() && Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        local Distance = Ammo_Prop:entity():pos():distance(Reload_Prop:entity():pos())
        if(Debug_Mode & ~Reload_Button & Reload_Button) {
            local DistanceFeet = floor(Distance / 16)
            printColor(vec(0,255,255), "[DEBUG] Distance between props: " + Distance + " units (" + DistanceFeet + " feet). Max allowed: " + Reload_Distance_Feet + " feet (" + Max_Reload_Distance + " units)")
        }
        return Distance
    }
    return 99999
}

function number getReloadAmmoFromProp() {
    if(Reload_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        local Size = Reload_Prop:entity():boxSize()
        return floor((Size:x() * Size:y() * Size:z()) / 1)
    }
    return 0
}

function number isOwnProjectile(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid()){ return 0 }
    if(Ent == LastBoltEnt){ return 1 }
    local Cls = Ent:getClass():lower()
    if(Cls == "crossbow_bolt" || Cls == "antcore_bolt" || Cls == "hunter_flechette" || Cls == "rpg_missile" || Cls == "grenade_ar2"){ return 1 }
    return 0
}

function number hasLineOfSight(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid() || !Gun:isValid()){ return 0 }
    if(isOwnProjectile(Ent)){ return 0 }
    rangerFilter(array(holoEntity(8600), holoEntity(8500), BaseEnt, entity(), Shooter, Prop, Gun, Lamp, LastBoltEnt, holoEntity(99999285), holoEntity(999992851)))
    local Muzzle = Gun:pos()
    local AimPt = getAimPoint(Ent)
    local WallCheck = rangerOffset(Muzzle, AimPt)
    if(!WallCheck:hit()){ return 1 }
    local HitEnt = WallCheck:entity()
    # Ignore own bolts crossing the LOS ray so lock doesn't drop every shot
    if(isOwnProjectile(HitEnt)){ return 1 }
    return (HitEnt == Ent) ? 1 : 0
}




function void hideAmmoDockMarker() {
    if(holoEntity(5601):isValid()) {
        holoAlpha(5601, 0)
        holoScale(5601, vec(1.0, 1.25, 1.0))
    }
    if(holoEntity(5602):isValid()) {
        holoAlpha(5602, 0)
    }
    Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
}

function void showBatteryDockMarker() {
    if(holoEntity(8887):isValid()) {
        holoAlpha(8887, 255)
    }
}

function void hideBatteryDockMarker() {
    if(holoEntity(8887):isValid()) {
        holoAlpha(8887, 0)
    }
}


# Actual left/right half-angle the turret really sweeps to (LEFT_RIGHT_FOV is the
# total L/R spread, margin trims the sweep ends). This is the REAL L/R aim envelope
# - used everywhere a target is acquired, retained, or drawn. Up/down uses UP_FOV /
# DOWN_FOV (own limits each side).
function number turretLRRange(){
    return (LEFT_RIGHT_FOV - Sweep_Margin) / 2
}

function number isInTurretRange(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid() || !BaseEnt:isValid()){
        return 0
    }
    local WorldDir = Ent:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()
    if(WorldDir:length() < 1){ return 1 }
    local LD = BaseEnt:toLocalAxis(WorldDir:normalized())
    if(LD:length() < 0.001){ return 0 }
    LD = LD:normalized()

    # Pure local cone around base forward. Works for floor, wall, ceiling, any angle.
    local Yaw   = atan2(LD:y(), LD:x()) * 180 / _PI
    local Horiz = sqrt(LD:x()*LD:x() + LD:y()*LD:y())
    local Pitch = atan2(-LD:z(), max(Horiz, 0.0001)) * 180 / _PI

    while(Yaw > 180){ Yaw -= 360 }
    while(Yaw < -180){ Yaw += 360 }
    while(Pitch > 180){ Pitch -= 360 }
    while(Pitch < -180){ Pitch += 360 }

   # return (abs(Yaw) <= 90) && (abs(Pitch) <= 85)
return (abs(Yaw) <= turretLRRange()) && (Pitch >= -UP_FOV) && (Pitch <= DOWN_FOV)
}



function number isWhitelisted(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid()){
        return 0
    }
    if(Ent:isPlayer()){
        local RawName = Ent:name()
        local CleanName = RawName:trim():lower()
        if(WhitelistPlayers:exists(CleanName)){
            return 1
        }
        foreach(WhitelistedName:string, Value:number = WhitelistPlayers){ if(Value){}
            if(CleanName:find(WhitelistedName) >= 1){
                return 1
            }
        }
        return 0
    }
    local EntType = Ent:getClass():lower()
    if(WhitelistNPCs:exists(EntType)){
        return 1
    }
    foreach(Key:string, Value:number = WhitelistNPCs){ if(Value){} if(Value){}
        if(EntType:find(Key) >= 1){
            return 1
        }
    }
    return 0
}

function number isSharpNPC(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid()){
        return 0
    }
    local EntType = Ent:getClass():lower()
    if(SharpNPCs:exists(EntType)){
        return 1
    }
    foreach(Key:string, Value:number = SharpNPCs){ if(Value){}
        if(EntType:find(Key) >= 1){
            return 1
        }
    }
    return 0
}

function void logAutoReloadState(Message:string) {
    if(Debug_Mode) {
        printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[AUTO-RELOAD DEBUG] " + Message + " | Ammo: " + Ammo_Count + " | Triggered: " + Auto_Reload_Triggered + " | Paused: " + Auto_Reload_Paused)
    }
}

function void makeParticleSimple(Mat:string, Col:vector, StartAlpha:number, StartSize:number, Duration:number, StartPos:vector, Velocity:vector){
    particleSetMaterial(Mat)
    particleSetColor(Col)
    particleSetPitch(0)
    particleSetStartAlpha(StartAlpha)
    particleSetEndAlpha(0)
    particleSetStartSize(StartSize)
    particleSetEndSize(0)
    particleSetDuration(Duration)
    particleBounce(0)
    particleGravity(vec())
    particleCollision(0)
    particleSetPosition(StartPos)
    particleSetVelocity(Velocity)
    particleCreate()
} 

# ---- Multi-turret ownership helper ----
# Returns 1 if Ent is parented to something that is NOT one of this turret's own
# entities (chip, Gun, or any of its build holos). Prevents two turrets from
# fighting over the same prop (ammo box, gun, battery, EGP, charger...).
function number isOwnedElsewhere(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid()){ return 0 }
    local Par = Ent:parent()
    if(!Par:isValid()){ return 0 }
    if(Par == entity()){ return 0 }
    if(Gun:isValid() && Par == Gun){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(20):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(20)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8500):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8500)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8600):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8600)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8601):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8601)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8888):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8888)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(9001):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(9001)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(9501):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(9501)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(999888):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(999888)){ return 0 }
    return 1
}

# For wired mount props (Gun/Battery/EGP/Charger/Prop): only block if the prop is
# already parented to ANOTHER turret's build (a hologram that isn't one of ours).
# A prop welded to a regular base/spawn is the normal pre-assembled mount and is
# still grabbed, matching the original behaviour.
function number isMountedByOtherTurret(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid()){ return 0 }
    local Par = Ent:parent()
    if(!Par:isValid()){ return 0 }
    if(Par == entity()){ return 0 }
    if(Gun:isValid() && Par == Gun){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(20):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(20)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8500):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8500)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8600):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8600)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8601):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8601)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(8888):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(8888)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(9001):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(9001)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(9501):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(9501)){ return 0 }
    if(holoEntity(999888):isValid() && Par == holoEntity(999888)){ return 0 }
    # Parented to a hologram we don't own = some other chip's build = leave it alone
    if(Par:getClass() == "gmod_wire_hologram"){ return 1 }
    return 0
}

# ---- Battery seating (mirrors lamp / ammo box) ----
# Holo 8888 is ONLY a position/angle reference. The physical battery parents to a
# STABLE entity (BaseEnt / chip) so it is never deleted when holograms are
# destroyed on E2 reset / AdvDupe2 paste.
function void repositionBatteryOnly(P:entity) {
    if(!P:isValid()) { return }
    if(!holoEntity(8888):isValid()) { return }

    # Parent to a STABLE entity, never the ephemeral holo.
    local Par = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt : entity()
    if(!Par:isValid()) { return }

    P:propFreeze(1)
    P:deparent()
    P:setPos(holoEntity(8888):pos())
    P:setAng(holoEntity(8888):angles())
    P:propNotSolid(0)
    local BatteryIndex = clamp(BatteryProfile, 1, max(BatteryModels:count(), 1))
    if(BatteryModels:count() > 0) { P:setModel(BatteryModels[BatteryIndex, string]) }
    P:setModelScale(BatteryProfileScales:count() >= BatteryIndex ? BatteryProfileScales[BatteryIndex, vector] : Battery_Scale_Main)
    P:setPhysScale(BatteryProfilePhysScales:count() >= BatteryIndex ? BatteryProfilePhysScales[BatteryIndex, number] : Battery_Scale)
    if(BatteryProfileAngles:count() >= BatteryIndex) { P:setAng(holoEntity(8888):toWorld(BatteryProfileAngles[BatteryIndex, angle])) }
    P:parentTo(Par)          # stable parent
    P:propNotSolid(1)
    P:noCollideAll(1)

    BatteryEnt = P
    # Keep frozen while seated (same reason lamp/mag stay frozen).
}

function void ejectBattery(Reason:string) {
    local E = BatteryEnt
    if(!E:isValid()) { return }

    Battery_EjectedEnt = E
    E:deparent()
    E:propFreeze(0)
    E:propNotSolid(0)

    local Dock = holoEntity(8888)
    local Out = Dock:isValid() ? (Dock:right() + Dock:up() * 0.8 + Dock:forward() * 0.15) : vec(0, 1, 0.8)
    Out = Out:normalized()
    E:setPos(Dock:isValid() ? Dock:pos() + Out * 4 : E:pos())
    E:applyForce(Out * Battery_Eject_Speed * E:mass() + vec(0, 0, Battery_Eject_Up) * E:mass())
    BatteryEnt = noentity()

    printColor(vec(255, 180, 0), "[BATTERY] Battery ejected (" + Reason + ").")
    hint("Battery ejected - insert a charged battery.", 5)
    if(BaseEnt:isValid()) { BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/button1.wav", 70, 90, 0.8) }

    local Ejected = E
    timer(TimerPrefix + "battery_eject_cleanup", Battery_Eject_Delay, function(){
        if(Ejected:isValid()){
            Ejected:propDelete()
            Ejected:admPropDelete()
        }
        if(Battery_EjectedEnt == Ejected){ Battery_EjectedEnt = noentity() }
    })
}

# Same idea as recoverAmmoBox(), for the battery. Resolves BatteryEnt - the
# entity everything else (power gate, color feedback, holo parenting) should
# use - from whichever source is available: the wired Battery input if
# present, otherwise a nearby unclaimed battery prop found by proximity, same
# as the ammo box requires zero explicit wiring to be picked up.
# True when the seated battery is missing, parented wrong, or not on the mount holo.
function number batteryNeedsRepos(){
    if(!BatteryEnt:isValid()){ return 1 }
    if(isMountedByOtherTurret(BatteryEnt)){ return 1 }
    local Par = BatteryEnt:parent()
    local WantPar = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt : entity()
    # Must parent to BaseEnt (or the chip) - not floating, not on a dead holo
    if(!Par:isValid() || (WantPar:isValid() && Par != WantPar && Par != entity())){
        return 1
    }
    return 0
}

function void recoverBattery() {
    # Do not auto-grab or seat a battery until the gun is wired. Assembly order
    # is gun first, then battery - otherwise the battery snaps onto the mount
    # while the turret is still being built.
    if(!Gun:isValid()){
        return
    }

    # Wired input always wins as the resolved entity
    if(Battery:isValid() && Battery != Battery_EjectedEnt && !isMountedByOtherTurret(Battery)) {
        BatteryEnt = Battery
    }

    # Already have a good seated battery - nothing to do
    if(BatteryEnt:isValid() && !batteryNeedsRepos()){ hideBatteryDockMarker() return }
    # Have a battery but it drifted / lost parent / survived a chip reset:
    # snap it back onto holo 8888 (parent stays BaseEnt/chip, not the holo).
    if(BatteryEnt:isValid() && !isMountedByOtherTurret(BatteryEnt)) {
        if(holoEntity(8888):isValid()) {
            repositionBatteryOnly(BatteryEnt)
        }
        hideBatteryDockMarker()
        return
    }
    
    
    # No usable BatteryEnt yet - search nearby (unwired / auto-find path)
     showBatteryDockMarker()
    BatteryEnt = noentity()
    local SearchFrom = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:pos() : entity():pos()
    if(findCanQuery()) {
        findClearBlackList()
        findClearWhiteList()
        findExcludeClass("player")
        findExcludeClass("gmod_wire_hologram")
        findExcludeEntity(entity())
        if(Gun:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Gun) }
        if(Prop:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Prop) }
        if(BaseEnt:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(BaseEnt) }
        findInSphere(SearchFrom, Battery_Dock_Radius)
        findSortByDistance(SearchFrom)
        local Arr = findToArray()
        local Batt = noentity()

        # pass 1: real Wiremod battery entities take priority
        for(I = 1, Arr:count()) {
            local C = Arr[I, entity]
            if(!C:isValid()) { continue }
            if(C == Battery_EjectedEnt) { continue }
            if(isMountedByOtherTurret(C)) { continue }
            if(C:getClass() == "gmod_wire_battery") { Batt = C break }
        }
        # pass 2: fall back to anything modeled like a battery prop
        if(!Batt:isValid()) {
            for(I = 1, Arr:count()) {
                local C = Arr[I, entity]
                if(!C:isValid()) { continue }
                if(C == Battery_EjectedEnt) { continue }
                if(isMountedByOtherTurret(C)) { continue }
                local M = C:model():lower()
                if(M:find("battery") || M:find("power") || M:find("car_battery")) { Batt = C break }
            }
        }

             if(Batt:isValid()) {
            BatteryEnt = Batt
            hideBatteryDockMarker()
            printColor(vec(0,255,200), "[BATTERY] Auto-found nearby battery: " + Batt:model())
            if(holoEntity(8888):isValid()) {
                repositionBatteryOnly(Batt)
            }
        }
    }

    # Keep retrying after dupe/spawn until the battery shows up (quiet, namespaced)
    if(!BatteryEnt:isValid() && !timerExists(TimerPrefix + "retry_battery_recovery")) {
        timer(TimerPrefix + "retry_battery_recovery", 0.4, function(){ recoverBattery() })
    }
}

function void parentBattery(){
    if(!Gun:isValid()){
        # Silent - gun not wired yet, battery stays put until gun is linked
        return
    }
    recoverBattery()
    local Batt = BatteryEnt
    if(!Batt:isValid()){
        # Quiet unless the gun has been up for a bit (avoids spam during boot)
        if(Setup_Done){
            printColor(vec(255,100,100), "[BATTERY] parentBattery: Battery entity invalid")
        }
        return
    }
    if(!holoEntity(8888):isValid()){
        printColor(vec(255,100,100), "[BATTERY] parentBattery: Holo 8888 invalid - buildHolos not run yet?")
        return
    }
    if(isMountedByOtherTurret(Batt)){
        printColor(vec(255,150,0), "[BATTERY] parentBattery: Battery owned by other turret")
        return
    }
    # Do not re-parent a battery that is already seated. This function is called
    # by several wiring/recovery paths, so it must be edge-triggered like ammo.
    if(!batteryNeedsRepos()){ return }
    printColor(vec(0,255,200), "[BATTERY] Parenting battery: " + Batt:model())
    repositionBatteryOnly(Batt)

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "seatParent2")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent2") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent2", 0.05, function(){
        local Ent = BatteryEnt
        if(Ent:isValid() && holoEntity(8888):isValid()){
            if(batteryNeedsRepos()){
                repositionBatteryOnly(Ent)
            }
        }else{
            if(Setup_Done){
                printColor(vec(255,100,100), "[BATTERY] Battery became invalid during parenting")
            }
        }
    })
}

function void parentCharger(){
    if(!RechargeStation:isValid() || !holoEntity(9501):isValid()){ return }
    if(isMountedByOtherTurret(RechargeStation)){ return }
    RechargeStation:setPos(holoEntity(9501):pos())
    RechargeStation:setAng(holoEntity(9501):angles())
    RechargeStation:propNotSolid(0)
    RechargeStation:propFreeze(1)

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "Reparent_")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "Reparent_") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "Reparent_", 0.1, function(){
        if(RechargeStation:isValid()){
            RechargeStation:parentTo(holoEntity(9501))
            RechargeStation:propFreeze(0)
            hint("Ammo Prop Parented", 3)
            RechargeStation:setModelScale(vec(0.4,0.4,0.6))
            RechargeStation:setPhysScale(0.5)
            RechargeStation:noCollideAll(1)
            BaseEnt:soundPlay(0, 1, "buttons/bell1.wav")
        }
    })
}

function void disableGunAssemblyCollision(){
    if(Gun:isValid()){ Gun:noCollideAll(1) }
    if(MagEnt:isValid()){ MagEnt:propNotSolid(1) MagEnt:noCollideAll(1) }
    if(BatteryEnt:isValid()){ BatteryEnt:propNotSolid(1) BatteryEnt:noCollideAll(1) }
    if(Entity:isValid()){ Entity:noCollideAll(1) }
    if(Prop:isValid()){ Prop:noCollideAll(1) }
    #if(holoEntity(999992851):isValid()){ holoEntity(999992851):noCollideAll(1) }

}


function number angDiffAbs(A:number, B:number){
    local D = A - B
    while(D > 180){ D -= 360 }
    while(D < -180){ D += 360 }
    return abs(D)
}




function void parentGun(){
    if(!Gun:isValid() || !holoEntity(8601):isValid()){ return }
    if(isMountedByOtherTurret(Gun)){ return }
    Gun:propFreeze(1)
    Gun:deparent()
    Gun:setPos(holoEntity(8601):pos())
    Gun:setAng(holoEntity(8601):angles())
    Gun:propNotSolid(0)
    Gun:noCollideAll(1)

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "seatParentGun")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "seatParentGun") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "seatParentGun", 0.01, function(){
        if(!Gun:isValid() || !holoEntity(8601):isValid()){ return }
        Gun:setPos(holoEntity(8601):pos())
        Gun:setAng(holoEntity(8601):angles())
        Gun:parentTo(holoEntity(8601))
        # Keep FROZEN while seated - propFreeze(0) made the gun overlap the
        # mount and the engine deleted it (same bug as mag/battery/lamp).
        Gun:propFreeze(1)
        Gun:noCollideAll(1)
        hint("Gun parented", 3)
        BaseEnt:soundPlay(0, 1, "buttons/bell1.wav")
        printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[GUN] Gun prop parented to pivot (frozen/seated)")
    })
}

function void parentProp(){
    if(!Prop:isValid() || !holoEntity(999888):isValid()){ return }
    if(isMountedByOtherTurret(Prop)){ return }
    Prop:setPos(holoEntity(999888):pos())
    Prop:setAng(holoEntity(999888):angles())
    Prop:propFreeze(1)

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "seatParent")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent", 0.005, function(){
        if(Prop:isValid()){
            Prop:parentTo(holoEntity(999888))
            hint("Parenting done", 5)
            BaseEnt:soundPlay(0, 1, "buttons/bell1.wav")
            Prop:propFreeze(0)
        }
    })
}

# The magazine is a real PHYSICAL prop, so it MUST parent to the Gun entity
function entity gunModelHolo(){
    if(MachineGunMode == 1){ return holoEntity(40) }
    return holoEntity(20)
}

# Reference entity for the ammo box POSITION. Prefers the ACTIVE gun-model
# hologram (holo 40 machine gun in mode 1, holo 20 minigun in mode 0) so the
# MagPos0/1 + MagAng0/1 values are offsets from the VISIBLE gun model. Falls
# back to Gun. (The box still PARENTS to Gun for reset safety.)
function entity magRefHolo(){
    if(gunModelHolo():isValid()){ return gunModelHolo() }
    return Gun
}



function number magNeedsRepos(){
    if(!MagSeated || !MagEnt:isValid() || !Gun:isValid()){ return 0 }
    local AmmoIndex = clamp(AmmoProfile, 1, max(AmmoModels:count(), 1))
    if(AmmoModels:count() >= AmmoIndex && MagEnt:model() != AmmoModels[AmmoIndex, string]){ return 1 }
    if(MagEnt:parent() != Gun){ return 1 }
    local Ref = magRefHolo()
    if(!Ref:isValid()){ Ref = Gun }
    if(MagEnt:pos():distance(Ref:toWorld(MaG_PoS)) > 25){ return 1 }
    local CurA = MagEnt:angles()
    local ProfileAng = AmmoProfileAngles:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfileAngles[AmmoIndex, angle] : ang()
    local WantA = Ref:toWorld(Mag_Ang + ProfileAng)
    if(angDiffAbs(CurA:pitch(), WantA:pitch()) > 2){ return 1 }
    if(angDiffAbs(CurA:yaw(), WantA:yaw()) > 2){ return 1 }
    if(angDiffAbs(CurA:roll(), WantA:roll()) > 2){ return 1 }
    return 0
}

#[
# Returns 1 if the seated ammo box is out of place versus the current per-mode
# config - checks parent, POSITION and ANGLE. Comparing angle too is what lets a
# MagAng0/1 change actually reposition the box (previously only position was
# checked, so pure-angle edits did nothing).
function number magNeedsRepos(){
    if(!MagSeated || !MagEnt:isValid() || !Gun:isValid()){ return 0 }
    local AmmoIndex = clamp(AmmoProfile, 1, max(AmmoModels:count(), 1))
    if(AmmoModels:count() >= AmmoIndex && MagEnt:model() != AmmoModels[AmmoIndex, string]){ return 1 }
    if(MagEnt:parent() != Gun){ return 1 }
    local Ref = magRefHolo()
    if(!Ref:isValid()){ Ref = Gun }
    if(MagEnt:pos():distance(Ref:toWorld(MaG_PoS)) > 25){ return 1 }
    local CurA = MagEnt:angles()
    local ProfileAng = AmmoProfileAngles:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfileAngles[AmmoIndex, angle] : ang()
    local WantA = Ref:toWorld(Mag_Ang + ProfileAng)
    if(abs(CurA:pitch() - WantA:pitch()) > 2){ return 1 }
    if(abs(CurA:yaw() - WantA:yaw()) > 2){ return 1 }
    if(abs(CurA:roll() - WantA:roll()) > 2){ return 1 }
    return 0
}
]#

function void updateLightBaseParent() {
    if(!holoEntity(1050):isValid()) { return }
    # Parent to a STABLE entity (Gun / gun-model holo / chip), NEVER the ammo
    # box prop. Parented to the box, deleting the box deletes the hologram with
    # it and it never comes back. With the stable parent it always exists and
    # just follows the box's base position/angle instead.
    local P = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : (gunModelHolo():isValid() ? gunModelHolo() : entity())
    if(!P:isValid()){ return }
    local LBPos = MachineGunMode == 1 ? AmmoBase_Pos1 : AmmoBase_Pos0
    local LBAng = MachineGunMode == 1 ? AmmoBase_Ang1 : AmmoBase_Ang0
    # Position/angle reference: the seated box if there is one (so the holder
    # sits exactly on the box's base), else the gun model so it stays put.
    local Ref = MagEnt
    if(!(MagSeated && MagEnt:isValid())){
        Ref = gunModelHolo():isValid() ? gunModelHolo() : P
    }
    holoParent(1050, P)
    holoPos(1050, Ref:toWorld(LBPos))
    holoAng(1050, Ref:toWorld(LBAng))
    holoAlpha(1050, 255)
}

# per-mode offsets MaG_PoS/Mag_Ang (MagPos0/1, MagAng0/1).
function void repositionMagOnly(P:entity) {
    if(!P:isValid()) { return }
    local Parent = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : entity()
    if(!Parent:isValid()) { return }
    local Ref = magRefHolo()
    if(!Ref:isValid()){ Ref = Parent }
    P:propFreeze(1)
    P:deparent()
    P:setPos(Ref:toWorld(MaG_PoS))
    local AmmoIndex = clamp(AmmoProfile, 1, max(AmmoModels:count(), 1))
    local ProfileAng = AmmoProfileAngles:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfileAngles[AmmoIndex, angle] : ang()
    P:setAng(Ref:toWorld(Mag_Ang + ProfileAng))
    P:propNotSolid(0)
    if(AmmoModels:count() > 0) { P:setModel(AmmoModels[AmmoIndex, string]) }
    P:setModelScale(AmmoProfileScales:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfileScales[AmmoIndex, vector] : Mag_Scale)
    P:setPhysScale(AmmoProfilePhysScales:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfilePhysScales[AmmoIndex, number] : Mag_phys_scale)
    if(AmmoProfileAngles:count() >= AmmoIndex) { P:setAng(Ref:toWorld(Mag_Ang + AmmoProfileAngles[AmmoIndex, angle])) }
    P:parentTo(Parent)
    P:propNotSolid(1)
    # Keep the mag frozen while seated - propFreeze(0) here made the box
    # overlap the gun and the engine deleted it instantly. Unfreeze happens
    # only in ejectAmmoBox when the mag is flung off.
    MagEnt = P
    MagEntIndex = P:id()
    MagSeated = 1
    # Reparent after dupe/reset must restore capacity  was leaving Ammo_Count at 0
    if(Max_Ammo <= 0) { Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
    if(Ammo_Count <= 0) {
        Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo
        printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[AMMO] Mag re-parented + ammo restored: " + Ammo_Count + "/" + Max_Ammo)
    }
    # (removed the spammy "Mag re-parented to Gun" print that fired every recovery tick)
    # Keep the light-base holder (1050) riding with the box so it always sits with it.
    updateLightBaseParent()
}

function entity getLampEntity() {
    if(LampCache:isValid()) { return LampCache }
    if(LampIndex > 0) {
        local E = entity(LampIndex)
        if(E:isValid()) {
            LampCache = E
            return E
        }
    }
    if(Lamp:isValid()) {
        LampCache = Lamp
        LampIndex = LampCache:id()
        return LampCache
    }
    return noentity()
}

function void repositionLampOnly(P:entity) {
    if(!P:isValid()) { return }
    if(!holoEntity(42):isValid()) { return }
    # Parent the lamp to the STABLE prop (Gun), NOT the ephemeral mount holo.
    # Holos are destroyed on every E2 reset, which deletes/strands the lamp -
    # the ammo box avoids this by parenting to the Gun prop. Fall back to the
    # gun hub holo (20) or the chip itself if Gun isn't wired yet.
    local Par = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : (holoEntity(20):isValid() ? holoEntity(20) : entity())
    if(!Par:isValid()) { return }
    P:propFreeze(1)
    P:deparent()
    # Drop it exactly on the mount cube (holo 42) in world space, then parent to Par.
    P:setPos(holoEntity(42):pos())
    P:setAng(holoEntity(42):angles())
    P:propNotSolid(0)
    P:setModelScale(LampScale)
    P:setPhysScale(LampPhysScale)
    P:parentTo(Par)
    # Keep the lamp FROZEN while seated - propFreeze(0) made the lamp overlap
    # the mount and the engine deleted it instantly. Unfreeze is never needed
    # because the light model + sprite handle the visible glow.
    LampCache = P
    LampEnt = P
    LampIndex = P:id()
    LampParented = 1
    P:setAlpha(0)
    LampWirelink = P:wirelink()
}

function void seatLampProp(P:entity) {
    if(!P:isValid()) {
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[LAMP] Tried to seat invalid prop")
        return
    }
    if(LampParented) { return }
    if(!holoEntity(42):isValid()) {
        # Mount holo not ready yet - caller retries via the recover timer.
        return
    }
    LampParented = 1
    LampCache = P
    LampEnt = P
    LampIndex = P:id()

    local Par = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : (holoEntity(20):isValid() ? holoEntity(20) : entity())

    P:propFreeze(1)
    P:deparent()
    P:setPos(holoEntity(42):pos())
    P:setAng(holoEntity(42):angles())
    P:propNotSolid(0)
    P:setModelScale(LampScale)
    P:setPhysScale(LampPhysScale)

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "seat_lamp")) { stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "seat_lamp") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "seat_lamp", 0.05, function() {
        if(!P:isValid()) {
            LampParented = 0
            LampCache = noentity()
            LampEnt = noentity()
            LampIndex = 0
            return
        }
        if(holoEntity(42):isValid() && Par:isValid()) {
            P:setPos(holoEntity(42):pos())
            P:setAng(holoEntity(42):angles())
            P:parentTo(Par)
        }
        # Keep the lamp FROZEN while seated - propFreeze(0) makes the engine delete it.
        LampParented = 1
        P:setAlpha(0)
        LampWirelink = P:wirelink()
        printColor(vec(0,255,255), "[LAMP] Lamp seated on Gun! (index " + LampIndex + ")")
        hint("Lamp seated", 3)
        entity():soundPlay(0,1,"buttons/bell1.wav")
    })
}

function void recoverLamp() {
    local Par = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : (holoEntity(20):isValid() ? holoEntity(20) : entity())
    if(LampParented && LampCache:isValid() && Par:isValid()) {
        # Reseat if the lamp drifted or lost its Gun parent (mirrors the ammo box).
        if(LampCache:parent() != Par || LampCache:pos():distance(holoEntity(42):isValid() ? holoEntity(42):pos() : LampCache:pos()) > 25) {
            repositionLampOnly(LampCache)
        }
        return
    }
    local LE = getLampEntity()
    if(LE:isValid()) {
        LampCache = LE
        LampEnt = LE
        LampIndex = LE:id()
        if(holoEntity(42):isValid()) {
            repositionLampOnly(LE)
        } else {
            LampParented = 1
        }
        LampWirelink = LE:wirelink()
    } else {
        LampParented = 0
    }
}

function void resetTracking(){
    TargetVel = vec()
    TargetAccel = vec()
    PrevTargetVel = vec()
    PrevTargetPos = vec()
    CurrentTargetPos = vec()
    LastTargetUpdate = 0
   # InterceptTime = 0
    VelHistory = array()
    VelHistoryIndex = 1
}

function void servoSound(){
    CUR = holoEntity(8500):angles():yaw()
    VEL = abs(min((CUR - PREV), 1))
    PREV = CUR

    local GunPitch = holoEntity(8600):angles():pitch()
    GunPitchVel = abs(GunPitch - GunPrevPitch)
    GunPrevPitch = GunPitch
    local GunMoving = GunPitchVel > 0.02

    local SweepVolume = 0
    if(TurretActive_Real){
        if(Sweep_speed == 0){
            if(HasValidTarget && VEL > 0){
                SweepVolume = remap(VEL, 0, 0.5, 0.0, Turret_sound_vol)
            }
        }elseif(!Target:isValid() && VEL > 0){
            # Mode 1: mute while paused at either side  only hum while actually traveling
            if(Sweep_Mode == 1 && Sweep_Pausing){
                SweepVolume = 0
            }else{
                local YawDiff = holoEntity(8500):angles():yaw() - BaseEnt:angles():yaw()
                while(YawDiff > 180){ YawDiff -= 360 }
                while(YawDiff < -180){ YawDiff += 360 }
                SweepVolume = Turret_sound_vol * clamp(1 - (abs(YawDiff) / LEFT_RIGHT_FOV), 0, 1)
            }
        }
    }
    soundPitch(500, remap(VEL, 0, 1, TurretPitch_Min, TurretPitch_Max))
    soundVolume(500, SweepVolume)

    local GunVolume = 0
    if(TurretActive_Real && GunMoving){
        if(Sweep_speed != 0 || HasValidTarget){
            GunVolume = remap(GunPitchVel, 0, 0.5, 0.0, Turret_sound_vol)
        }
    }
    soundPitch(501, remap(GunPitchVel, 0, 1, GunPitch_Min, GunPitch_Max))
    soundVolume(501, GunVolume)
}

function void setAimRate(Rate:number){
    Aim_Rate = clamp(Rate, 0.01, 0.2)
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "aim")){ timerSetDelay(TimerPrefix + "aim", Aim_Rate) }
}

function void setScanRate(Rate:number){
    Scan_Rate = clamp(Rate, 0.05, 2)
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "scan")){ timerSetDelay(TimerPrefix + "scan", Scan_Rate) }
}

# True when this turret is the screen owner: the LOWEST live hive slot that has
# a wired EGP. Only ONE turret may draw/parent the shared EGP screen, otherwise
# when two or more are wired to the same screen they all egpClear()+redraw it
# every tick and fight over the same objects (glitching live readouts).
function number hiveAmIEGPOwner(){
    if(!EGP:isValid()){ return 0 }
    if(HiveSlot <= 0 || HiveSlots <= 0){ return 0 }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    for(I = 1, HiveSlot - 1){
        local Sig  = GT["s" + I + "_sig", number]
        local Hbt  = GT["s" + I + "_hb", number]
        local HasE = GT["s" + I + "_e", number]
        if(Sig != 0 && curtime() <= Hbt + 5 && HasE){ return 0 }
    }
    return 1
}

# This turret's sweep/aim offset, normalized to [-180,180]. Signed: positive to
# the right (+yaw), negative to the left. Broadcast to the hive so the shared
# screen can render EVERY turret's sweep readout (not just the owner's).
function number swingOffsetScreen(){
    local SweepBase = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:angles():yaw() : 0
    local SweepOff = Angle:yaw() - SweepBase
    while(SweepOff > 180){ SweepOff -= 360 }
    while(SweepOff < -180){ SweepOff += 360 }
    return SweepOff
}

# Touch cursor for the HUD screen (radio-style): tracks a player aiming at the
# EGP so the POWER button can be hit-tested during a playerUse event.
function void egpTouchCursor(){
    if(!EGP_ScreenCreated || !EGP:isValid() || !EGP_Screen:entity():isValid()){
        EGP_CursorVisible = 0
        return
    }
    if(!hiveAmIEGPOwner()){
        EGP_CursorVisible = 0
        return
    }
    local Ply = noentity()
    if(EGP_LastUser:isValid() && EGP_LastUser:shootPos():distance(EGP:pos()) < EGP_UseRange){
        Ply = EGP_LastUser
    }else{
        foreach(_:number, P:entity = players()){
            if(!P:isValid()){ continue }
            if(P:shootPos():distance(EGP:pos()) > EGP_UseRange){ continue }
            local C = EGP_Screen:egpCursor(P)
            if(C:x() >= 0 && C:x() <= 512 && C:y() >= 0 && C:y() <= 512){
                Ply = P
                break
            }
        }
    }
    if(!Ply:isValid()){
        if(EGP_CursorVisible){
            EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorIdx, 0)
            EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorHitIdx, 0)
            EGP_CursorVisible = 0
        }
        return
    }
    local Cursor = EGP_Screen:egpCursor(Ply)
    if(Cursor:x() < 0 || Cursor:x() > 512 || Cursor:y() < 0 || Cursor:y() > 512){
        if(EGP_CursorVisible){
            EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorIdx, 0)
            EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorHitIdx, 0)
            EGP_CursorVisible = 0
        }
        return
    }
    EGP_Screen:egpPos(EGP_CursorIdx, Cursor)
    EGP_Screen:egpPos(EGP_CursorHitIdx, Cursor)
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorIdx, 230)
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorHitIdx, 200)
    EGP_CursorVisible = 1
}

function void setupEGPScreen(){
    if(!EGP:isValid()){ return }
    EGP_Screen = EGP:wirelink()
    if(!hiveAmIEGPOwner()){ return }

    EGP_Screen:egpClear()
    # Single running object index (touchscreen style): every EGP object on the
    # screen is allocated from ONE continuous sequence starting after the clear,
    # so nothing gets dropped or collides as more objects are added.
    local EI = 0
    local W = 512
    local H = 512

    # Background (textured material box behind everything, picked by Theme_BG_Index)
    EI++
    local BgID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpBox(BgID, vec2(W/2, H/2), vec2(W, H))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(BgID, Theme_BG_Col)
    local ThemeBG = (Theme_BG_Index >= 1 && Theme_BG_Index <= Theme_Backgrounds:count()) ? Theme_Backgrounds[Theme_BG_Index, string] : ""
    if(Theme_Mode == 1){
        if(ThemeBG != ""){
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(BgID, vec(255))
            EGP_Screen:egpMaterial(BgID, ThemeBG)
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(BgID, Theme_BG_Col)
            EGP_Screen:egpMaterial(BgID, "")
        }
    }

    # Title bar
    EI++
    local PanelID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpBox(PanelID, vec2(W/2, 30), vec2(W, 60))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(PanelID, Theme_Panel_Col)
    if(Theme_Mode == 1){
        EI++
        local PanelOutlineID = EI
        EGP_Screen:egpBoxOutline(PanelOutlineID, vec2(W/2, 30), vec2(W, 60))
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(PanelOutlineID, Theme_Title_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(PanelOutlineID, 1)
    }
    EI++
    local TitleID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(TitleID, Theme_Title, vec2(225, 30))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(TitleID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(TitleID, Theme_Title_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(TitleID, Theme_Title_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(TitleID, Theme_Title_Font)

    # Table column headers: PWR | TURRET | AMMO | HEAT | DISTANCE | TARGET | RECHARGER | SWEEP | BATT | STATUS
    EGP_Head = array()
    EI++
    local HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["PWR", string], vec2(24, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["TURRET", string], vec2(52, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 0, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["AMMO", string], vec2(114, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["HEAT", string], vec2(165, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["DIST", string], vec2(216, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["TARGET", string], vec2(268, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["CHARGR", string], vec2(334, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["SWEEP", string], vec2(385, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["BATT", string], vec2(435, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)
    EI++
    HeadID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpText(HeadID, Theme_Headers["STATUS", string], vec2(482, 112))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(HeadID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(HeadID, Theme_Header_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(HeadID, Theme_Header_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(HeadID, Theme_Header_Font)
    EGP_Head:pushNumber(HeadID)

    # Roster table - each turret in its own row aligned to the column headers.
    # AMMO and HEAT stack label (top line) above value (bottom line); other columns hold values only.
    local RowMax = min(HiveSlots, 8)
    EGP_RowID = array()
    EGP_SlotPwr = array()
    EGP_SlotPwrIdx = array()
    for(I = 1, RowMax){
        local RY  = 142 + (I - 1) * 44
        local RY2 = RY # values share the turret name's baseline
        EI++
        local NName = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NName)
        EI++
        local NAVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NAVal)
        EI++
        local NHVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NHVal)
        EI++
        local NDVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NDVal)
        EI++
        local NTVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NTVal)
        EI++
        local NRVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NRVal)
        EI++
        local NBVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NBVal)
        EI++
        local NSVal = EI
        EGP_RowID:pushNumber(NSVal)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NName, "", vec2(52, RY))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NName, 0, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NName, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NName, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NName, vec(220, 220, 220))
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NAVal, "", vec2(114, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NAVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NAVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NAVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NAVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NHVal, "", vec2(165, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NHVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NHVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NHVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NHVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NDVal, "", vec2(216, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NDVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NDVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NDVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NDVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NTVal, "", vec2(268, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NTVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NTVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NTVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NTVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NRVal, "", vec2(334, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NRVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NRVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NRVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NRVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NBVal, "", vec2(435, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NBVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NBVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NBVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NBVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NSVal, "", vec2(482, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NSVal, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(NSVal, Theme_Value_Size)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NSVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NSVal, Theme_Value_Font)
        # Per-row power button (far left, next to the turret name)
        EI++
        local NPwr = EI
        EI++
        local NPwrT = EI
        EGP_Screen:egpBox(NPwr, vec2(24, RY), vec2(24, 30))
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NPwr, EGP_PowerOff_Fill)
        EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(NPwr, 0)
        EGP_Screen:egpTextLayout(NPwrT, "", vec2(24, RY), vec2(24, 24))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(NPwrT, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NPwrT, vec(255))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(NPwrT, 0)
        EGP_Screen:egpFont(NPwrT, EGP_Button_Font, 9)
        EGP_SlotPwr:pushNumber(NPwr)
        EGP_SlotPwrIdx:pushNumber(NPwrT)
    }

    # Per-row SWEEP value objects (built once). The owner renders each live
    # turret's broadcast sweep angle here, so every row shows its own readout.
    # SWEEP column header is at x=385; these are the per-row values.
    EGP_SweepID = array()
    for(I = 1, RowMax){
        EI++
        local SweepObj = EI
        EGP_SweepID:pushNumber(SweepObj)
        EGP_Screen:egpText(SweepObj, "", vec2(385, 142 + (I - 1) * 44))
        EGP_Screen:egpAlign(SweepObj, 1, 1)
        EGP_Screen:egpSize(SweepObj, 16)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(SweepObj, vec(220, 220, 220))
    }

    # HELP / backup boxes: pre-build one per possible roster row (up to RowMax).
    # Update packs LIVE turrets left-to-right in the SAME order as the roster
    # (EGP_RowSlot), so box 1 = row 1, box 2 = row 2, etc.
    EGP_HelpIdx = array()
    EGP_HelpBox = array()
    local BackupBoxY = 483


for(I = 1, RowMax){
    I = I   # suppress unused-variable warning
    # Placeholder positions; updateEGPScreen re-centers live ones each frame
    local CenterX = 256
    EI++
    local OutlineID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpBox(OutlineID, vec2(CenterX, BackupBoxY), vec2(40, 28))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(OutlineID, vec(0, 140, 255))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(OutlineID, 0)
    EI++
    local FillID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpBox(FillID, vec2(CenterX, BackupBoxY), vec2(36, 24))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(FillID, vec(10, 40, 70))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(FillID, 0)
    EGP_HelpBox:pushNumber(FillID)
    EI++
    local TextID = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpTextLayout(TextID, "", vec2(CenterX, BackupBoxY), vec2(36, 24))
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(TextID, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpSize(TextID, 9)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(TextID, vec(255, 170, 0))
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(TextID, "Trebuchet MS")
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(TextID, 0)
    EGP_HelpIdx:pushNumber(TextID)
}

    # Power button shows THIS turret's own state (remote source overrides wiring)
    EGP_PowerOn = ((WeaponSender_Enabled == 1) || (RemoteToggleEnabled == 1)) ? RemoteState : TurretActive

    # ---- Touch master POWER button (radio-style) ----
    EI++
    EGP_PowerOutlineIdx = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpBox(EGP_PowerOutlineIdx, EGP_Power_Pos, EGP_Power_Size + vec2(EGP_Outline_Thick * 2, EGP_Outline_Thick * 2))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_PowerOutlineIdx, EGP_Outline_Col)
    if(RemoteToggleEnabled != 1){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_PowerOutlineIdx, 0) }
    EI++
    EGP_PowerBtn = EGP_Screen:egpBox(EI, EGP_Power_Pos, EGP_Power_Size)
    EGP_PowerBtnIndex = EI
    EGP_PowerFill = EGP_PowerOn ? EGP_PowerOn_Fill : EGP_PowerOff_Fill
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_PowerBtnIndex, EGP_PowerFill)
    EI++
    EGP_PowerTextIdx = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpTextLayout(EGP_PowerTextIdx, "POWER", EGP_Power_Pos, EGP_Power_Size)
    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(EGP_PowerTextIdx, 1, 1)
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_PowerTextIdx, vec(255))
    EGP_Screen:egpFont(EGP_PowerTextIdx, EGP_Button_Font, EGP_Power_FontSize)
    # Hide the power button when the EGP remote is not the active control source
    if(RemoteToggleEnabled != 1){
        EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_PowerBtnIndex, 0)
        EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_PowerTextIdx, 0)
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_PowerTextIdx, vec(255, 0, 0))  # transparent red
    }

    # ---- Per-row power buttons are created in the roster loop above (dynamic index) ----

    # Touch cursor (hidden until a player aims at the screen)
    EI++
    EGP_CursorIdx = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpCircle(EGP_CursorIdx, vec2(256, 256), vec2(EGP_CursorSize, EGP_CursorSize))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_CursorIdx, EGP_Cursor_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorIdx, 0)
    EI++
    EGP_CursorHitIdx = EI
    EGP_Screen:egpBox(EGP_CursorHitIdx, vec2(256, 256), vec2(0, 0))
    EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_CursorHitIdx, EGP_Cursor_Col)
    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_CursorHitIdx, 0)
    EGP_CursorVisible = 0
    EGP_LastPressTime = 0

    EGP_ScreenCreated = 1
    EGP_LastUpdate = curtime()
    if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "egp_cursor")){ timer(TimerPrefix + "egp_cursor", EGP_CursorRate, 0, function(){ egpTouchCursor() }) }
    printColor(vec(0,255,200), "[EGP] Status display ready (built-in, no extra wires)")
}

function string shortenTargetName(TargetEnt:entity){
    if(!TargetEnt:isValid()){
        return "INVALID"
    }
    if(TargetEnt:isPlayer()){
        local PlayerName = TargetEnt:name()
        if(PlayerName:length() > 20){
            PlayerName = PlayerName:sub(1, 17) + "..."
        }
        return PlayerName
    }
    local Class = TargetEnt:getClass():lower()
    if(Class == ""){
        return "Unknown"
    }
    local CleanName = ""
    local FriendlyLookup = table()
    FriendlyLookup["npc_clawscanner"] = "Claw Scanner"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_cscanner"] = "City Scanner"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_manhack"] = "Manhack"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_headcrab"] = "Headcrab"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_headcrab_black"] = "Poison Headcrab"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_headcrab_fast"] = "Fast Headcrab"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_headcrab_poison"] = "Poison Headcrab"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_zombie"] = "Zombie"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_zombie_torso"] = "Zombie Torso"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_fastzombie"] = "Fast Zombie"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_fastzombie_torso"] = "Fast Zombie Torso"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_poisonzombie"] = "Poison Zombie"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_combine_s"] = "Combine Soldier"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_metropolice"] = "Metrocop"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_hunter"] = "Hunter"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_antlion"] = "Antlion"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_antlionguard"] = "Antlion Guard"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_antlion_worker"] = "Antlion Worker"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_strider"] = "Strider"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_gunship"] = "Gunship"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_helicopter"] = "Helicopter"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_rollermine"] = "Rollermine"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_turret_floor"] = "Floor Turret"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_turret_ceiling"] = "Ceiling Turret"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_barnacle"] = "Barnacle"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_bullseye"] = "Target"
    FriendlyLookup["vj_avp_hum_butch"] = "Butcher"
    FriendlyLookup["vj_avp_hum_archa"] = "Archer"
    FriendlyLookup["npc_vj_antlion"] = "Antlion"
    FriendlyLookup["prop_physics"] = "Prop"
    FriendlyLookup["prop_ragdoll"] = "Ragdoll"
    if(FriendlyLookup:exists(Class)){
        return FriendlyLookup[Class, string]
    }
    # Remove technical addon/entity prefixes before formatting generic NPC names.
    # The repeated pass handles classes such as npc_vj_avp_hum_archa.
    if(Class:left(4) == "npc_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 4)
    }
    if(Class:left(11) == "vj_avp_hum_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 11)
    }elseif(Class:left(7) == "vj_avp_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 7)
    }elseif(Class:left(3) == "vj_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 3)
    }
    if(Class:left(7) == "weapon_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 7)
    }
    if(Class:left(5) == "prop_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 5)
    }
    if(Class:left(6) == "monster_"){
        Class = Class:right(Class:length() - 6)
    }
    local Words = Class:explode("_")
    local CleanWords = array()
    for(I = 1, Words:count()){
        local W = Words[I, string]
        if(W != ""){
            CleanWords:pushString(W:left(1):upper() + W:right(W:length() - 1):lower())
        }
    }
    CleanName = CleanWords:concat(" ")
    if(CleanName == "" || CleanName:find("^[0-9]+$") > 0){
        CleanName = "Unknown"
    }
    if(CleanName:length() > 20){
        CleanName = CleanName:sub(1, 17) + "..."
    }
    return CleanName
}

function number shouldTriggerAutoReload() {
    if(!Auto_Reload_Enabled || Refilling) { return 0 }
    if(Ammo_Count <= Auto_Reload_Force_Below) { return 1 }
    if(Ammo_Count < Auto_Reload_Threshold) {
        if(Auto_Reload_With_Target || !Target:isValid()) { return 1 }
    }
    return 0
}

function vector smoothVelocity(NewVel:vector){
    VelHistory[VelHistoryIndex, vector] = NewVel
    local Newest = (VelHistoryIndex - 1 + VelSamples) % VelSamples + 1
    VelHistoryIndex = (VelHistoryIndex % VelSamples) + 1

    local SmoothVel = vec()
    local TotalWeight = 0
    local Weight = 2^(VelSamples - 1)

    local Idx = Newest
    for(I = 1, VelSamples){
        I = I
        local Sample = VelHistory[Idx, vector]
        if(Sample:length() >= 0){
            SmoothVel = SmoothVel + Sample * Weight
            TotalWeight = TotalWeight + Weight
        }
        Idx = (Idx - 2 + VelSamples) % VelSamples + 1
        Weight = Weight / 2
    }

    if(TotalWeight > 0){
        return SmoothVel / TotalWeight
    }
    return NewVel
}

function void stopAllGunSounds() {
    soundStop(2)
    soundStop(1)
    for(I = 1, 15) { soundStop(I) }
    soundStop("Click")
}

function void stopFiringSounds() {
    for(I = 1, 5) { soundStop(I) }
    soundStop(7)
    soundStop(10)
    soundStop(11)
    soundStop(12)
}

function number targetScore(Ent:entity){
    local ToBase = BaseEnt:pos() - Ent:pos()
    local Distance = ToBase:length()
    if(Priority_Mode == 0){
        return Distance
    }
    local Closing = Ent:vel():dot(ToBase:normalized())
    return Distance - max(Closing, 0) * Threat_Lead
}

function void updateEGPScreen(){
    if(!EGP_ScreenCreated || !EGP:isValid()){ return }
    if(!hiveAmIEGPOwner()){ return }
    if(curtime() - EGP_LastUpdate < EGP_UpdateInterval){ return }
    EGP_LastUpdate = curtime()

    local DesiredForDisplay = ((WeaponSender_Enabled == 1) || (RemoteToggleEnabled == 1)) ? RemoteState : TurretActive

    # Keep the POWER button color in sync (green when on, red when off, or
    # flash highlight while pressed)
    if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(EGP_PowerBtnIndex)){
        local ButtonCol = DesiredForDisplay ? EGP_PowerOn_Fill : EGP_PowerOff_Fill
        if(ButtonCol != EGP_PowerFill){
            EGP_PowerFill = ButtonCol
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(EGP_PowerBtnIndex, ButtonCol)
        }
    }

    # The single master POWER button is shown ONLY in single-switch mode
    # (MasterPower_Mode = 1), like the original screen. In multi-switch mode
    # (MasterPower_Mode = 0) the master is hidden and per-turret buttons are used.
    if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(EGP_PowerBtnIndex)){
        local MasterAlpha = (RemoteToggleEnabled == 1 && MasterPower_Mode == 1) ? 255 : 0
        EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_PowerBtnIndex, MasterAlpha)
if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(EGP_PowerTextIdx)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_PowerTextIdx, MasterAlpha) }
if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(EGP_PowerOutlineIdx)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(EGP_PowerOutlineIdx, MasterAlpha) }
    }

    # Roster - pack LIVE turrets to the top rows; EMPTY rows only after them.
    # Display row order is independent of hive slot index so a single turret
    # always appears on row 1 (not under a stale EMPTY slot above it).
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    local RowMax = min(HiveSlots, 8)
    EGP_RowSlot = array()
    local LiveSlots = array()
    for(S = 1, RowMax){
        local SigS = GT["s" + S + "_sig", number]
        local HbtS = GT["s" + S + "_hb", number]
        if(SigS != 0 && curtime() <= HbtS + 5){
            LiveSlots:pushNumber(S)
        }
    }
    local LiveN = LiveSlots:count()
    for(I = 1, RowMax){
        local RY  = 142 + (I - 1) * 44
        local RY2 = RY
        local RI = (I - 1) * 8
        local NName = EGP_RowID[RI + 1, number]
        local NAVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 2, number]
        local NHVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 3, number]
        local NDVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 4, number]
        local NTVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 5, number]
        local NRVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 6, number]
        local NBVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 7, number]
        local NSVal = EGP_RowID[RI + 8, number]
        local SweepVal = EGP_SweepID[I, number]

        if(I > LiveN){
            EGP_RowSlot[I, number] = 0
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NName, "EMPTY", vec2(52, RY))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NName, Theme_Dim_Col)
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NAVal, "", vec2(114, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NHVal, "", vec2(165, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NDVal, "", vec2(216, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NTVal, "", vec2(268, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NRVal, "", vec2(334, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NBVal, "", vec2(435, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(SweepVal, "", vec2(385, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NSVal, "", vec2(482, RY2))
            continue
        }

        local Slot = LiveSlots[I, number]
        EGP_RowSlot[I, number] = Slot
        local Name = GT["s" + Slot + "_name", string]
        if(Name == ""){ Name = "TURRET " + Slot }
        local On = GT["s" + Slot + "_on", number]
        local Batt = floor(GT["s" + Slot + "_batt", number])
        if(Slot == HiveSlot){
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NName, "> " + Name, vec2(52, RY))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NName, vec(0, 200, 255))
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NName, Name, vec2(52, RY))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NName, vec(220, 220, 220))
        }
        local ACur = GT["s" + Slot + "_acur", number]
        local AMax = GT["s" + Slot + "_amax", number]
        if(AMax > 0){
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NAVal, round(ACur) + "/" + round(AMax), vec2(114, RY2))
            if(ACur <= 0){
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(NAVal, vec(255, 80, 80))
            }elseif(ACur / AMax <= 0.25){
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(NAVal, vec(255, 200, 0))
            }else{
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(NAVal, vec(150, 255, 170))
            }
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NAVal, "--", vec2(114, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NAVal, Theme_Dim_Col)
        }
        local Hp = GT["s" + Slot + "_heat", number]
        if(Hp >= 0){
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NHVal, round(Hp) + "%", vec2(165, RY2))
            if(Hp >= 75){
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(NHVal, vec(255, 60, 60))
            }elseif(Hp >= 50){
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(NHVal, vec(255, 200, 0))
            }else{
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(NHVal, vec(120, 200, 255))
            }
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NHVal, "--", vec2(165, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NHVal, Theme_Dim_Col)
        }
        local TEnt = entity(GT["s" + Slot + "_tgt", number])
        local TDist = GT["s" + Slot + "_tdist", number]
        if(TEnt:isValid() && TEnt:isAlive() && TDist >= 0){
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NDVal, TDist + "FT", vec2(216, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NDVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NDVal, "--", vec2(216, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NDVal, Theme_Dim_Col)
        }
        if(TEnt:isValid() && TEnt:isAlive()){
            local TName = shortenTargetName(TEnt)
            if(TName:length() > 10){ TName = TName:sub(1, 7) + "..." }
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NTVal, TName, vec2(268, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NTVal, vec(255, 120, 120))
        }elseif(On){
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NTVal, "--", vec2(268, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NTVal, Theme_Value_Col)
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpText(NTVal, "--", vec2(268, RY2))
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NTVal, Theme_Dim_Col)
        }
        local Chg = GT["s" + Slot + "_chrg", number]
        local RechargeState = (Chg == 1 || Chg == 2 || Chg == 3) ? "CONN" : "--"
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NRVal, RechargeState, vec2(334, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(NRVal, Chg == 1 || Chg == 2 || Chg == 3 ? vec(0, 255, 100) : vec(150, 165, 185))
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NBVal, Batt + "%", vec2(435, RY2))
        if(Batt > 50){
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NBVal, vec(0, 255, 100))
        }elseif(Batt > 25){
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NBVal, vec(255, 200, 0))
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NBVal, vec(255, 60, 60))
        }
        local St = GT["s" + Slot + "_status", string]
        if(St == ""){ St = On ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE" }
        EGP_Screen:egpText(NSVal, St, vec2(482, RY2))
        if(On){
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NSVal, vec(0, 255, 100))
        }else{
            EGP_Screen:egpColor(NSVal, vec(255, 80, 80))
        }
        local SweepOff = GT["s" + Slot + "_sw", number]
        EGP_Screen:egpText(SweepVal, round(SweepOff):toString(), vec2(385, RY2))
        EGP_Screen:egpColor(SweepVal, SweepOff < -0.5 ? vec(120, 200, 255) : (SweepOff > 0.5 ? vec(0, 255, 100) : vec(220, 220, 220)))
    }

    # Per-row power buttons: only shown in multi-switch mode (MasterPower_Mode = 0).
    # In single-switch mode (MasterPower_Mode = 1, the original) they are fully hidden.
    if(MasterPower_Mode == 0){
        for(I = 1, RowMax){
            local Obj  = EGP_SlotPwr[I, number]
            local Txt  = EGP_SlotPwrIdx[I, number]
            local Slot = EGP_RowSlot[I, number]
            local RY   = 142 + (I - 1) * 44
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(Obj) && EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(Txt)){
                if(Slot > 0){
                    local SlotName = GT["s" + Slot + "_name", string]
                    if(SlotName == ""){ SlotName = "PWR" }
                    if(SlotName:length() > 4){ SlotName = SlotName:sub(1, 4) }
                    local SlotOn = GT["s" + Slot + "_on", number]
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(Obj, 255)
                    EGP_Screen:egpColor(Obj, SlotOn ? EGP_PowerOn_Fill : EGP_PowerOff_Fill)
                    EGP_Screen:egpPos(Obj, vec2(24, RY))
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(Txt, 255)
                    EGP_Screen:egpPos(Txt, vec2(24, RY))
                    EGP_Screen:egpTextLayout(Txt, SlotName, vec2(24, RY), vec2(24, 24))
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlign(Txt, 1, 1)
                }else{
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(Obj, 0)
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(Txt, 0)
                }
            }
        }
    }else{
        # Single master-switch mode: hide every per-row button and label
        for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
            local Obj = EGP_SlotPwr[I, number]
            local Txt = EGP_SlotPwrIdx[I, number]
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(Obj)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(Obj, 0) }
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(Txt)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(Txt, 0) }
        }
    }

    # HELP boxes: pack LIVE turrets left-to-right in the SAME order as the
    # roster (EGP_RowSlot). Box 1 = roster row 1, box 2 = row 2, etc.
    local BackupTable = gTable(HiveName)
    local LiveN2 = 0
    for(J = 1, RowMax){
        if(EGP_RowSlot[J, number] > 0){ LiveN2++ }
    }
    if(LiveN2 >= 2){
        local BoxCount2 = LiveN2
        local BackupGap2 = 16
        local BackupPad2 = 180
        local BackupAvailW2 = 512 - BackupPad2 * 2
        local BackupBoxW2 = (BackupAvailW2 - (BoxCount2 - 1) * BackupGap2) / BoxCount2
        local BackupBoxH2 = 24
        local BackupBlockW2 = BoxCount2 * BackupBoxW2 + (BoxCount2 - 1) * BackupGap2
        local BackupStartX2 = (512 - BackupBlockW2) / 2
        local BackupBoxY2 = 483
        for(I = 1, RowMax){
            local TextObj2 = EGP_HelpIdx[I, number]
            local FillObj2 = EGP_HelpBox[I, number]
            if(!EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(TextObj2) || !EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(FillObj2)){ continue }
            local OutlineObj2 = FillObj2 - 1
            local Slot = EGP_RowSlot[I, number]
            if(I <= LiveN2 && Slot > 0){
                local BoxCenterX2 = BackupStartX2 + BackupBoxW2 / 2 + (I - 1) * (BackupBoxW2 + BackupGap2)
                local IsBacking = BackupTable["s" + Slot + "_bk", number]
                local BackingName = IsBacking ? BackupTable["s" + Slot + "_bkn", string] : ""
                local TurretLabel = BackupTable["s" + Slot + "_name", string]
                if(TurretLabel == ""){ TurretLabel = "T" + Slot }
                if(TurretLabel:length() > 5){ TurretLabel = TurretLabel:sub(1, 5) }
                local StatusText = "NOT HELPING"
                local StatusCol = vec(150, 155, 165)
                if(IsBacking && BackingName != ""){
                    local HelpedName = BackingName
                    if(HelpedName:length() > 6){ HelpedName = HelpedName:sub(1, 6) }
                    StatusText = "HELP " + HelpedName:upper()
                    StatusCol = vec(255, 170, 0)
                }
                # Optional: prefix with this turret's short name so L->R matches roster
                # StatusText = TurretLabel + ": " + StatusText  (kept short for width)
                if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(FillObj2)){
                    EGP_Screen:egpBox(FillObj2, vec2(BoxCenterX2, BackupBoxY2), vec2(BackupBoxW2, BackupBoxH2))
                    EGP_Screen:egpColor(FillObj2, vec(10, 40, 70))
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(FillObj2, 255)
                }
                if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(OutlineObj2)){
                    EGP_Screen:egpBox(OutlineObj2, vec2(BoxCenterX2, BackupBoxY2), vec2(BackupBoxW2 + 4, BackupBoxH2 + 4))
                    EGP_Screen:egpColor(OutlineObj2, vec(0, 140, 255))
                    EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(OutlineObj2, 255)
                }
                EGP_Screen:egpTextLayout(TextObj2, " " + StatusText, vec2(BoxCenterX2, BackupBoxY2), vec2(BackupBoxW2, BackupBoxH2))
                EGP_Screen:egpAlign(TextObj2, 1, 1)
                EGP_Screen:egpSize(TextObj2, 9)
                EGP_Screen:egpColor(TextObj2, StatusCol)
                EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(TextObj2, 255)
            }else{
                if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(TextObj2)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(TextObj2, 0) }
                if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(FillObj2)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(FillObj2, 0) }
                if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(OutlineObj2)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(OutlineObj2, 0) }
            }
        }
    }else{
        # Hide all backup boxes when fewer than 2 live turrets
        for(I = 1, EGP_HelpBox:count()){
            local TextObj = EGP_HelpIdx[I, number]
            local FillObj = EGP_HelpBox[I, number]
            local OutlineObj = FillObj - 1
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(TextObj)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(TextObj, 0) }
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(FillObj)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(FillObj, 0) }
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(OutlineObj)){ EGP_Screen:egpAlpha(OutlineObj, 0) }
        }
    }
}

function void fireStart(){
    if(Fire == 1 || Fire_Pending){ return }
