    if(!canFire()){ return }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "fire_off")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "fire_off") }
    if(Delay <= 0){
        Fire = 1
        return
    }
    Fire_Pending = 1
    timer(TimerPrefix + "fire_on", Delay, function(){
        Fire_Pending = 0
        if(canFire()){
            Fire = 1
        }
    })
}

# Re-apply the target find filters. MUST run whenever the chip's find configuration may
# have been clobbered: recoverBattery(), recoverAmmoBox() and ammoDockScan() all call
# findClearWhiteList()/findClearBlackList() and install battery/mag-only filters, so the
# next findInSphere() in buildTargetQueue() would scan for the wrong classes and the
# turret silently stops acquiring targets until the E2 is reset (see buildTargetQueue).
function void applyTargetFindFilters(){
    if(TargetPlayers_enabled == 1){
        findIncludeClass("player")
    }else{
        findExcludeClass("player")
    }

    # Don't waste find results / ops on corpses & junk
    findExcludeClass("prop_ragdoll")
    findExcludeClass("class C_ClientRagdoll")
    findExcludeClass("gmod_wire_hologram")
    findExcludeClass("gmod_wire_expression2")
    findExcludeClass("crossbow_bolt")
    findExcludeClass("antcore_bolt")

    findIncludeClass("npc_stalker")
    findIncludeClass("npc_*")
    foreach(Key:string, Value:number = TargetNPCs){ if(Value){} if(Value){}
        findIncludeClass(Key)
    }
    foreach(Key:string, Value:number = TargetVehicles){ if(Value){} if(Value){}
        findIncludeClass(Key)
    }
    foreach(Key:string, Value:number = TargetCustom){ if(Value){} if(Value){}
        findIncludeClass(Key)
    }
}

function void setupWhitelist(){
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "=== SETTING UP WHITELIST ===")
    WhitelistPlayers = table()
    if(Owner:isValid()){
        local OwnerName = Owner:name():trim():lower()
        WhitelistPlayers[OwnerName, number] = 1
        printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Added owner: '" + OwnerName + "'")
    }

    local PlayersToAdd = array("sand", "Red Fox", "matt")
    for(I = 1, PlayersToAdd:count()){
        local PlayerName = PlayersToAdd[I, string]:trim():lower()
        WhitelistPlayers[PlayerName, number] = 1
        printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Added player: '" + PlayerName + "'")
    }

    WhitelistNPCs["npc_citizen", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_barney", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_kleiner", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_eli", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_alyx", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_dog", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_vortigaunt", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["zb_friendly_hunter", number] = 1
    WhitelistNPCs["npc_vj_avp_hum_", number] = 1


    TargetPlayers = table()
    TargetNPCs = table()
    TargetVehicles = table()
    TargetCustom = table()

    TargetNPCs["npc_combine_s", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_combine_*", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_metropolice", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_manhack", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_cscanner", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_clawscanner", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_hunter", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_combinegunship*", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_helicopter", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_zombie*", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_fastzombie", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_poisonzombie", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_headcrab", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_antlion", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["zb_*", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["drg_codzmp_*", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["npc_vj_*", number] = 1
    TargetNPCs["enemy*", number] = 1


    TargetVehicles["lvs_plane_*", number] = 1
    TargetVehicles["lvs_helicopter_", number] = 1
    TargetVehicles["lvs_tank_", number] = 1
    TargetVehicles["lvs_boat_", number] = 1
    TargetVehicles["prop_vehicle_", number] = 1

    TargetCustom["lvs_*", number] = 1
    TargetCustom["npc_turret_", number] = 1

    # Target find filters (player/corpses/junk + NPC/vehicle/custom classes).
    # Shared with buildTargetQueue() so a scan always runs against the right classes.
    applyTargetFindFilters()

    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Whitelist setup complete!")
    displayWhitelist()
}


function void powerTick(){
    local Dt = Power_Rate

    Battery_Percent = (Battery_Current / Battery_Capacity) * 100
    Battery_Voltage = Battery_MinVoltage + ((Battery_MaxVoltage - Battery_MinVoltage) * (Battery_Current / Battery_Capacity))

    recoverBattery()
    if(!BatteryEnt:isValid()){
        Power_Available = 0
        TurretActive_Real = 0
        fireStop()
        Target = noentity()
        HasValidTarget = 0
        Battery_Fire_Paused = 0
        Battery_Status = "NO BATTERY"
        Battery_Percent = 0
        Battery_Voltage = 0
        # During boot grace the battery/mag are still being recovered - do NOT
        # latch Battery_Shutdown or the turret stays dead until a manual reset
        # even after the battery snaps onto the mount.
        if(curtime() >= JustReset){
            Battery_Shutdown = 1
            if(curtime() - LastMissingWarnTime >= Missing_Warn_Interval){
                LastMissingWarnTime = curtime()
                printColor(vec(255,50,50), "[WARN] No battery wired! Turret offline.")
                BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/button10.wav", 80, 100, 0.7)
            }
        }
    }else{
        if(Battery_Shutdown){
            # After a fresh boot the simulated cell is full - accept normal start
            # threshold so we do not stay latched offline until a manual reset.
            local Need = (curtime() < JustReset + 2) ? Battery_MinStartThreshold : Battery_Startup_Threshold
            if(Battery_Percent >= Need){
                Battery_Shutdown = 0
                Battery_Fire_Paused = 0
                Power_Available = 1
                printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[BATTERY] Power restored (" + round(Battery_Percent) + "%)")
                BaseEnt:emitSound("@hl1/fvox/power_restored.wav", 80, 100, 1.0)
            }else{
                Power_Available = 0
            }
        }else{
            if(Battery_Percent <= Low_Cutoff_Percent && !Recharge_In_Range){
                Battery_Shutdown = 1
                Battery_Fire_Paused = 0
                Power_Available = 0
                TurretActive_Real = 0
                fireStop()
                Target = noentity()
                HasValidTarget = 0
                printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[BATTERY] Critical shutdown - no charger in range! (" + round(Battery_Percent) + "%)")
                BaseEnt:emitSound("@hl1/fvox/power_level_is_unsafe.wav", 80, 100, 1.0)
            }else{
                Power_Available = 1
            }
        }
    }

    if(Power_Available && !Battery_Shutdown){
        if(!Battery_Fire_Paused){
            if(Battery_Percent <= Fire_Pause_Threshold){
                Battery_Fire_Paused = 1
                fireStop()
                printColor(vec(255,150,0), "[BATTERY] " + round(Battery_Percent) + "% - pausing fire, charging...")
                BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/button10.wav", 70, 100, 0.7)
            }
        }else{
            local ResumeAt = HasValidTarget ? Fire_Resume_Threshold : 100
            if(Battery_Percent >= ResumeAt){
                Battery_Fire_Paused = 0
                printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[BATTERY] Charge recovered (" + round(Battery_Percent) + "%) - resuming fire")
                BaseEnt:emitSound("@hl1/fvox/power_restored.wav", 80, 100, 1.0)
            }
        }
    }

    # Control source priority: weapon sender > EGP remote > wired input
    # Power coming back must NOT arm the turret by itself. DesiredActive is only
    # whatever the user (or hive/EGP) explicitly set - default is always off.
    # NOTE: do not call applyTurretState from powerTick (defined later); just
    # mirror the desired bit. applyTurretState handles sounds/scan on explicit toggles.
    local RemoteControlOn = (WeaponSender_Enabled == 1) || (RemoteToggleEnabled == 1)
    local DesiredActive = RemoteControlOn ? RemoteState : TurretActive
    if(!Power_Available){
        if(TurretActive_Real){
            TurretActive_Real = 0
            fireStop()
            Target = noentity()
            HasValidTarget = 0
        }
    }elseif(DesiredActive){
        TurretActive_Real = 1
    }else{
        # Explicit default: stay/go offline. Battery insert must end here.
        if(TurretActive_Real){
            TurretActive_Real = 0
            fireStop()
            Target = noentity()
            HasValidTarget = 0
            AimDown = 1
            AimDownTimer = curtime() + TurnOff_Delay
            ReturnToCenter = 0
        }else{
            TurretActive_Real = 0
        }
    }

    if(Prev_Power_Available != Power_Available){
        if(!Power_Available){
            ejectBattery("power depleted")
            if(DesiredActive){
                printColor(vec(255,0,0), "POWER FAILURE - Turret forced offline! (" + round(Battery_Percent) + "%)")
                BaseEnt:emitSound("@hl1/fvox/power_level_is_unsafe.wav", 80, 100, 0.8)
                fireStop()
                Target = noentity()
                HasValidTarget = 0
                Battery_Fire_Paused = 0
                AimDown = 1
                AimDownTimer = curtime() + 1.0
            }
        }
        Prev_Power_Available = Power_Available
    }

    # ---- charger range ----
    local EffectiveRechargeRange = Recharge_Range > 0 ? Recharge_Range : Recharge_Range_Default
    local InRangeNow = (RechargeStation:isValid() && BaseEnt:isValid() && (RechargeStation:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length() <= EffectiveRechargeRange) ? 1 : 0
    if(InRangeNow != Recharge_In_Range){
        if(curtime() - LastRangeToggleTime >= 0.3){
            Recharge_In_Range = InRangeNow
            LastRangeToggleTime = curtime()
            Charger_in_range = InRangeNow
        }
    }

    # ---- charge decision ----
    if(Recharge_In_Range && !Fire && Battery_Percent < 100){
        if(Battery_Fire_Paused){
            local PauseChargeTarget = HasValidTarget ? Fire_Resume_Threshold : 100
            Battery_Charging = Battery_Percent < PauseChargeTarget ? 1 : 0
        }elseif(Battery_Shutdown){
            Battery_Charging = Battery_Percent < Battery_Startup_Threshold ? 1 : 0
        }elseif(Old_Battery_Mode == 1 && !HasValidTarget){
            Battery_Charging = 1
        }else{
            Battery_Charging = 0
        }
    }else{
        Battery_Charging = 0
    }

    local Drain  = (Fire == 1 && TurretActive_Real) ? Battery_DrainRate * Dt : 0
    local Charge = Battery_Charging ? Battery_ChargeRate * Dt : 0
    Battery_Current = clamp(Battery_Current + Charge - Drain, 0, Battery_Capacity)

    Battery_Percent = (Battery_Current / Battery_Capacity) * 100
    Battery_Voltage = Battery_MinVoltage + ((Battery_MaxVoltage - Battery_MinVoltage) * (Battery_Current / Battery_Capacity))
    Battery_Low_Warning = Battery_Percent <= Battery_LowThreshold

    # ---- charge sound, nested one-shot lambda ----
    if(Battery_Charging && Battery_Current < Battery_Capacity){
        if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "chargesound")){
            timer(TimerPrefix + "chargesound", 2, 0, function(){
                local Src = BatteryEnt:isValid() ? BatteryEnt : entity()
                Src:soundPlay("charging", 0, "@ambient/energy/electric_loop.wav")
                soundVolume("charging", 0.3)
            })
            local Src = BatteryEnt:isValid() ? BatteryEnt : entity()
            Src:soundPlay("charging", 0, "@ambient/energy/electric_loop.wav")
            soundVolume("charging", 0.3)
        }
    }else{
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "chargesound")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "chargesound") }
        soundStop("charging")
    }

    if(Battery_Percent <= Low_Warn_Threshold && curtime() - LastLowWarnTime >= LowWarnInterval){
        LastLowWarnTime = curtime()
        printColor(vec(255,150,0), "[BATTERY] LOW BATTERY - " + round(Battery_Percent) + "%")
        BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/bell1.wav", 80, 100, 0.5)
    }

    if(Battery_Percent <= Low_Cutoff_Percent){
        if(!Recharge_In_Range && curtime() - LastStationErrorTime >= StationErrorInterval){
            LastStationErrorTime = curtime()
            printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[CHARGER] Recharge station not in range (need <= " + round(EffectiveRechargeRange) + " units)")
            BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/button10.wav", 70, 100, 0.7)
        }
        fireStop()
    }
}

function number getAmmoFromProp() {
    if(getAmmoEntity():isValid()) { return floor(Max_rounds) }
    return 0
}

function number isReloadPropInRange() {
    local Distance = getDistanceToReloadProp()
    return Distance < (Max_Reload_Distance * 0.95)
}

function void seatAmmoProp(P:entity) {
    if(!P:isValid()) { 
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[AMMO] Tried to seat invalid prop")
        return 
    }
    if(MagSeated) { return }
    # Multi-turret: never steal a box another turret already claimed/parented
    if(isOwnedElsewhere(P)){
        printColor(vec(255,150,0), "[AMMO] Box is owned by another turret - leaving it alone")
        return
    }
    
    local Parent = noentity()
    if(Gun:isValid()) {
        Parent = Gun
    } elseif(holoEntity(20):isValid()) {
        Parent = holoEntity(20)
    } else {
        Parent = entity()
    }
    
    MagSeated = 1
    Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
    hideAmmoDockMarker()
    
    P:propFreeze(1)
    P:deparent()
    # Use the per-mode config position/angle (MagPos0/1 + MagAng0/1) relative to
    # the active gun model so the box sits where you tuned it for that gun.
    local Ref = magRefHolo()
    if(!Ref:isValid()){ Ref = Parent }
    P:setPos(Ref:toWorld(MaG_PoS))
    P:setAng(Ref:toWorld(Mag_Ang))
    P:propNotSolid(0)
    P:setModelScale(Mag_Scale)
    P:setPhysScale(Mag_phys_scale)
    
    MagEnt = P
    MagEntIndex = P:id()
    Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds)
    Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo
    Ammo_EjectedEnt = noentity()
    Last_Warning_Time = 0
    # Make the light-base holder (1050) ride with the seated box
    updateLightBaseParent()
    
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "seat_mag")) { stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "seat_mag") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "seat_mag", 0.05, function() {
        if(!P:isValid()) {
            MagSeated = 0
            MagEnt = noentity()
            MagEntIndex = 0
            return
        }
        local Par = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : (holoEntity(20):isValid() ? holoEntity(20) : entity())
        if(Par:isValid() && !isOwnedElsewhere(P)) {
            local Rf = magRefHolo()
            if(!Rf:isValid()){ Rf = Par }
            P:setPos(Rf:toWorld(MaG_PoS))
            local AmmoIndex = clamp(AmmoProfile, 1, max(AmmoModels:count(), 1))
            local ProfileAng = AmmoProfileAngles:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfileAngles[AmmoIndex, angle] : ang()
            P:setAng(Rf:toWorld(Mag_Ang + ProfileAng))
            P:parentTo(Par)
        }
        # NO propFreeze(0) here - unfreezing a box that's overlapping the gun
        # makes the engine delete it instantly ("vanishes"). Keep it frozen
        # while seated; ejectAmmoBox unfreezes it when it needs to fly off.
        MagSeated = 1
        Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
        stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
        hideAmmoDockMarker()
        printColor(vec(0, 255, 255), "[AMMO] Magazine seated! Capacity: " + Max_Ammo + " rounds")
        hint("Ammo prop seated", 3)
        entity():soundPlay(0, 1, "buttons/bell1.wav")
    })
}

function number isValidTarget(Ent:entity){
    if(!Ent:isValid() || !Ent:isAlive()){
        return 0
    }
    if(Ent:health() <= 0){
        return 0
    }
    if(isWhitelisted(Ent)){
        return 0
    }
    return 1
}

function void parentEGP(){
    if(!EGP:isValid()){
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[EGP] parentEGP: EGP not valid (not wired?)")
        return
    }
    if(!hiveAmIEGPOwner()){ return }
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "[EGP] parentEGP called, Auto_Parent_EGP=" + Auto_Parent_EGP)
    # Manual screen placement: don't snap/freeze/parent it, just build the HUD
    # wherever the prop currently is.
    if(!Auto_Parent_EGP){
        # Edge: just dropped 1->0 and screen was parented to our mount holo - release
        # it so it floats in place instead of staying welded on.
        if(Auto_Parent_EGP_Prev == 1 && holoEntity(9001):isValid() && EGP:parent() == holoEntity(9001)){
            EGP:deparent()
            EGP:propFreeze(0)
            printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[EGP] released EGP from mount (Auto_Parent_EGP 1->0)")
        }
        if(isMountedByOtherTurret(EGP)){
            printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[EGP] manual branch - blocked by isMountedByOtherTurret")
            Auto_Parent_EGP_Prev = Auto_Parent_EGP
            return
        }
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "egp_setup")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "egp_setup") }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "egp_setup", 0.1, function(){ setupEGPScreen() })
        printColor(vec(255,255,0), "[EGP] manual placement branch (Auto_Parent_EGP=0) - screen left in place")
        Auto_Parent_EGP_Prev = Auto_Parent_EGP
        return
    }
    if(!holoEntity(9001):isValid()){
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[EGP] holo 9001 invalid - cannot snap!")
        Auto_Parent_EGP_Prev = Auto_Parent_EGP
        return
    }
    if(isMountedByOtherTurret(EGP)){
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[EGP] auto branch - blocked by isMountedByOtherTurret")
        Auto_Parent_EGP_Prev = Auto_Parent_EGP
        return
    }
    printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[EGP] snapping + parenting EGP to holo 9001")
    EGP:setPos(holoEntity(9001):pos())
    EGP:setAng(holoEntity(9001):angles())
    EGP:propFreeze(1)
    Auto_Parent_EGP_Prev = Auto_Parent_EGP

    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "seatParent3")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent3") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent3", 0.05, function(){
        if(EGP:isValid()){
            EGP:parentTo(holoEntity(9001))
            hint("Parenting done", 5)
            BaseEnt:soundPlay(0, 1, "buttons/bell1.wav")
            EGP:propFreeze(0)
            # Build status UI once the screen is parented
            timer(TimerPrefix + "egp_setup", 0.1, function(){ setupEGPScreen() })
        }
    })
}

# ============================================================
# TARGET-LOCK HUD system (optional EGP emitter + HUD overlay)
# ============================================================
# Draws a "+" reticle at the target's MASS CENTER with the target name beside
# it and its world XYZ below.
#  - HUD overlay (gmod_wire_egp_hud on your screen): server-projected onto your
#    view as a lock-on bracket.
#  - EGP emitter (world surface): a 3D tracker parented to the mass center,
#    with the "+"/name/XYZ as 2D children of it, so the marker is anchored to
#    the target's true map position and follows it live.
# Gate: runs when either the screen HUD or emitter is enabled.
#
# worldToHUD(Cam, T) -> vec2
#   Converts a world position into 0..512 HUD screen coords (0,0 = top-left,
#   256,256 = center) relative to the given camera player's view.
function vector2 worldToHUD(Cam:entity, T:vector){
    if(!Cam:isValid()){ return vec2(-9999, -9999) }
    local E = Cam:pos()
    local A = Cam:eyeAngles()
    local F = A:forward()
    local R = A:right()
    local U = A:up()
    local D = (T - E):normalized()
    local Fwd   = D:dot(F)
    local Right = D:dot(R)
    local Up    = D:dot(U)
    if(Fwd <= 0.01){ return vec2(-9999, -9999) }
    local YawOff   = atan2(Right, Fwd) * 180 / _PI
    local PitchOff = atan2(Up, Fwd) * 180 / _PI
    if(abs(YawOff) > turretLRRange() || PitchOff < -UP_FOV || PitchOff > DOWN_FOV){ return vec2(-9999, -9999) }
    return vec2(TurretHUD_RetCenter:x() + YawOff * TurretHUD_PxPerDeg,
                TurretHUD_RetCenter:y() - PitchOff * TurretHUD_PxPerDeg)
}

# 1 if the target is inside the aim cone (L/R within the real sweep, U/D within UP_FOV/DOWN_FOV).
function number inHUDView(T:entity){
    if(!T:isValid()){ return 0 }
    local W = worldToHUD(owner(), T:massCenter())
    return (W:x() >= 0 && W:x() <= 512 && W:y() >= 0 && W:y() <= 512) ? 1 : 0
}

# Create the three fixed target-lock objects on one surface (HUD or emitter).
function void createHUDObjects(S:wirelink){
    if(!S:entity():isValid()){ return }
    S:egpClear()
    # Player-screen HUD: 3D tracker glued to the target
    TurretHUD_RetIdx  = 1   # 3D tracker
    TurretHUD_NameIdx = 2   # "+"
    TurretHUD_PosIdx  = 3   # name
    # index 4 = XYZ
    S:egp3DTracker(TurretHUD_RetIdx, BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:pos() : entity():pos())
    S:egpText(TurretHUD_NameIdx, "+", vec2())
    S:egpColor(TurretHUD_NameIdx, TurretHUD_RetLockCol)
    S:egpSize(TurretHUD_NameIdx, TurretHUD_Size + 6)
    S:egpFont(TurretHUD_NameIdx, TurretHUD_Font)
    S:egpAlign(TurretHUD_NameIdx, 1, 1)
    S:egpText(TurretHUD_PosIdx, "", TurretHUD_LabelOff)
    S:egpColor(TurretHUD_PosIdx, TurretHUD_NameCol)
    S:egpSize(TurretHUD_PosIdx, TurretHUD_Size)
    S:egpFont(TurretHUD_PosIdx, TurretHUD_Font)
    S:egpText(4, "", TurretHUD_LabelOff + vec2(0, 16))
    S:egpColor(4, TurretHUD_XYZCol)
    S:egpSize(4, TurretHUD_Size)
    S:egpFont(4, TurretHUD_Font)
    S:egpParent(TurretHUD_NameIdx, TurretHUD_RetIdx)
    S:egpParent(TurretHUD_PosIdx, TurretHUD_RetIdx)
    S:egpParent(4, TurretHUD_RetIdx)
    S:egpAlpha(TurretHUD_NameIdx, 0)
    S:egpAlpha(TurretHUD_PosIdx, 0)
    S:egpAlpha(4, 0)
}

# ============================================================
# EMITTER = read-only multi-turret STATUS panel (no touch, no +)
# ============================================================
function void createEmitterStatus(S:wirelink){
    if(!S:entity():isValid()){ return }
    S:egpClear()
    local W = 512
    local H = 512

    # ---- Fully opaque background (no world bleed-through) ----
    S:egpBox(1, vec2(W/2, H/2), vec2(W, H))
    S:egpColor(1, Theme_BG_Col)
    S:egpAlpha(1, 255)
    local ThemeBG = (Theme_BG_Index >= 1 && Theme_BG_Index <= Theme_Backgrounds:count()) ? Theme_Backgrounds[Theme_BG_Index, string] : ""
    if(Theme_Mode == 1){
        if(ThemeBG != ""){
            S:egpColor(1, vec(255))
            S:egpMaterial(1, ThemeBG)
            S:egpAlpha(1, 255)
        }else{
            S:egpColor(1, Theme_BG_Col)
            S:egpMaterial(1, "")
            S:egpAlpha(1, 255)
        }
    }

    # ---- Title bar ----
    S:egpBox(2, vec2(W/2, 30), vec2(W, 60))
    S:egpColor(2, Theme_Panel_Col)
    S:egpAlpha(2, 255)
    if(Theme_Mode == 1){
        S:egpBoxOutline(9, vec2(W/2, 30), vec2(W, 60))
        S:egpColor(9, Theme_Title_Col)
        S:egpSize(9, 1)
    }
    S:egpText(3, Theme_Title != "" ? Theme_Title : "SQUAD STATUS", vec2(W/2, 30))
    S:egpAlign(3, 1, 1)
    S:egpSize(3, Theme_Title_Size > 0 ? Theme_Title_Size : 20)
    S:egpColor(3, Theme_Title_Col)
    S:egpFont(3, Theme_Title_Font != "" ? Theme_Title_Font : "Trebuchet MS")

    # Column headers
    local Headers = array("NAME", "PWR", "AMMO", "HEAT", "DIST", "TARGET", "SWEEP", "BATT", "STATUS")
    local HX = array(45, 100, 150, 198, 246, 312, 368, 424, 480)
    for(I = 1, 9){
        local HID = 10 + I
        S:egpText(HID, Headers[I, string], vec2(HX[I, number], 68))
        S:egpAlign(HID, 1, 1)
        S:egpSize(HID, EGP_EMITTER_HEDDER_SCALE)
        S:egpColor(HID, vec(140, 160, 200))
    }

    # Value slots only (NO empty row stripe boxes  those were the "random bars")
    for(R = 1, 8){
        local RY = 98 + (R - 1) * 42
        for(C = 1, 9){
            local ID = 20 + (R - 1) * 9 + (C - 1)
            S:egpText(ID, "", vec2(HX[C, number], RY))
            S:egpAlign(ID, 1, 1)
            S:egpSize(ID, EGP_EMITTER_TURRET_INFO_SCALE)
            S:egpColor(ID, vec(180, 190, 210))
            S:egpFont(ID, "Trebuchet MS")
        }
    }

    S:egpText(100, "read-only  |  hive shared", vec2(W/2, 490))
    S:egpAlign(100, 1, 1)
    S:egpSize(100, 10)
    S:egpColor(100, vec(90, 100, 120))

    printColor(vec(0,255,180), "[HUD] Emitter panel ready (opaque bg, no empty row bars)")
}

function void updateEmitterStatus(S:wirelink){
    if(!S:entity():isValid()){ return }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    local RowMax = min(HiveSlots, 8)
    local LiveSlots = array()
    for(Slot = 1, RowMax){
        local Sig = GT["s" + Slot + "_sig", number]
        local Hbt = GT["s" + Slot + "_hb", number]
        if(Sig != 0 && curtime() <= Hbt + 5){
            LiveSlots:pushNumber(Slot)
        }
    }
    local LiveN = LiveSlots:count()
    local HX = array(45, 100, 150, 198, 246, 312, 368, 424, 480)

    for(R = 1, 8){
        local Base = 20 + (R - 1) * 9
        local RY = 98 + (R - 1) * 42
        if(R > LiveN){
            for(C = 0, 8){ S:egpText(Base + C, "", vec2(HX[C+1, number], RY)) }
            continue
        }
        local Slot = LiveSlots[R, number]
        local Name = GT["s" + Slot + "_name", string]
        if(Name == ""){ Name = "T" + Slot }
        local On    = GT["s" + Slot + "_on", number]
        local Batt  = floor(GT["s" + Slot + "_batt", number])
        local ACur  = GT["s" + Slot + "_acur", number]
        local AMax  = GT["s" + Slot + "_amax", number]
        local Heat  = floor(GT["s" + Slot + "_heat", number])
        local TDist = GT["s" + Slot + "_tdist", number]
        local Sweep = GT["s" + Slot + "_sw", number]
        local St    = GT["s" + Slot + "_status", string]
        if(St == ""){ St = On ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE" }
        local TEnt = entity(GT["s" + Slot + "_tgt", number])
        local TName = (TEnt:isValid() && TEnt:isAlive()) ? shortenTargetName(TEnt) : "--"

        local AmmoStr  = (AMax > 0) ? (floor(ACur) + "/" + floor(AMax)) : "--"
        local DistStr  = (TDist >= 0) ? (TDist + "ft") : "--"
        local SweepStr = round(Sweep):toString()
        local PwrStr   = On ? "ON" : "OFF"
        local PwrCol   = On ? vec(0, 220, 80) : vec(200, 60, 60)
        local BattCol  = (Batt > 50) ? vec(0, 255, 100) : ((Batt > 25) ? vec(255, 200, 0) : vec(255, 60, 60))
        local HeatCol  = (Heat > 80) ? vec(255, 80, 80) : ((Heat > 50) ? vec(255, 180, 60) : vec(180, 190, 210))
        local StatusCol = On ? vec(0, 255, 100) : vec(255, 80, 80)
        local SweepCol  = (Sweep < -0.5) ? vec(120, 200, 255) : ((Sweep > 0.5) ? vec(0, 255, 100) : vec(220, 220, 220))
        local TgtCol = (TEnt:isValid() && TEnt:isAlive()) ? vec(255, 120, 120) : vec(120, 130, 140)

        S:egpText(Base + 0, Name, vec2(45, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 0, vec(220, 230, 255))
        S:egpText(Base + 1, PwrStr, vec2(100, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 1, PwrCol)
        S:egpText(Base + 2, AmmoStr, vec2(150, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 2, vec(180, 190, 210))
        S:egpText(Base + 3, Heat + "%", vec2(198, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 3, HeatCol)
        S:egpText(Base + 4, DistStr, vec2(245, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 4, (TDist >= 0) ? vec(0, 220, 180) : vec(120, 130, 140))
        S:egpText(Base + 5, TName, vec2(312, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 5, TgtCol)
        S:egpText(Base + 6, SweepStr, vec2(368, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 6, SweepCol)
        S:egpText(Base + 7, Batt + "%", vec2(424, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 7, BattCol)
        S:egpText(Base + 8, St, vec2(480, RY))
        S:egpColor(Base + 8, StatusCol)
    }
}


function void drawHUDObjects(S:wirelink){
    if(!S:entity():isValid()){ return }
    if(Target:isValid()){
        local AimPoint = getAimPoint(Target)
        S:egpPos(TurretHUD_RetIdx, AimPoint)
        # Scale text up with distance, but never past TurretHUD_SizeMax.
        local Dist = owner():pos():distance(AimPoint)
        local Cap = max(TurretHUD_SizeMax, TurretHUD_Size)
        local Sz = clamp(floor(TurretHUD_Size + Dist / 50), TurretHUD_Size, Cap)
        S:egpSize(TurretHUD_NameIdx, Sz + 8)   # "+" a bit larger
        S:egpSize(TurretHUD_PosIdx, Sz)
        S:egpSize(4, Sz)
        S:egpText(TurretHUD_NameIdx, "+", vec2())
        S:egpColor(TurretHUD_NameIdx, TurretHUD_RetLockCol)
        S:egpText(TurretHUD_PosIdx, shortenTargetName(Target), TurretHUD_LabelOff)
        if(TurretHUD_ShowXYZ){
            local TPos = AimPoint
            S:egpText(4, "X:" + floor(TPos:x(),0) + " Y:" + floor(TPos:y(),0) + " Z:" + floor(TPos:z(),0), TurretHUD_LabelOff + vec2(0, 16))
        }else{
            S:egpText(4, "", TurretHUD_LabelOff + vec2(0, 16))
        }
        S:egpAlpha(TurretHUD_NameIdx, 255)
        S:egpAlpha(TurretHUD_PosIdx, 255)
        S:egpAlpha(4, TurretHUD_ShowXYZ ? 255 : 0)
    }else{
        S:egpAlpha(TurretHUD_NameIdx, 0)
        S:egpAlpha(TurretHUD_PosIdx, 0)
        S:egpAlpha(4, 0)
    }
}

# ============================================================
# EMITTER + HOLO = forced respawn until both exist and are seated
# ============================================================

function void resetEmitterTracked(){
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat") }
    if(TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid()){
        local W = TurretHUD_Emitter:wirelink()
        if(W:entity():isValid()){ W:egpHudEnable(0) }
        TurretHUD_Emitter:propDelete()
        TurretHUD_Emitter:admPropDelete()
    }
    if(holoEntity(90100):isValid()){ holoDelete(90100) }
    TurretHUD_Emitter = noentity()
    TurretHUD_EmitterCreated = 0
    TurretHUD_EmitterSeated = 0
}

# Re-sync the backing holo to the emitter's LIVE Position/Scale/Angle inputs so it always
# mirrors the 2D screen even when those change at runtime. The EGP screen renders at
# Em:LocalToWorld(DrawOffsetPos + vec(-64,0,64)), faces along up() of DrawOffsetAng, and its
# EGP content origin (0,0) sits at a CORNER of the 512x512 sheet (so we center by half-extents).
function void syncEmitterHolo(Em:entity, Sc:number){
    if(!Em:isValid()){ return }
    if(!holoEntity(90100):isValid()){ return }
    local EffScale1 = clamp(Sc * 0.25, 0.01, 0.5)
    local PosC1 = vec(64 - 256*EffScale1, 0, 7 + 256*EffScale1) + TurretHUD_EmitContentOffset
    local ScLocal = vec(-64, 0, 64) + PosC1   # DrawOffsetNoRT = Vector(-64,0,64) = the 3D2D plane origin
    local PlDim = 512 * EffScale1   # full 512-unit screen, scaled to world size
    local Half   = 256 * EffScale1  # half extent; EGP content origin (0,0) is a CORNER, not center
    local HoloA = Em:toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitAngOffset)   # world angle of the sheet plane
    holoPos(90100, Em:toWorld(ScLocal) + HoloA:right() * Half + HoloA:forward() * Half - HoloA:up() * 0.05)
    holoAng(90100, HoloA)
    holoScaleUnits(90100, vec(PlDim, PlDim, 0.02))
}

function void setupEmitterHolo(Em:entity, Sc:number){
    if(!Em:isValid()){ return }
    if(holoEntity(90100):isValid()){ holoDelete(90100) }
    holoCreate(90100)
    holoModel(90100, "cube")
    holoColor(90100, vec(255))
    holoParent(90100, Em)
    syncEmitterHolo(Em, Sc)
}

function void spawnEmitterFresh(){
    # Rate-limit spawn attempts so a persistent failure doesn't hammer every tick.
    if(curtime() <= TurretHUD_EmitterRetryT){ return }
    local Sc = clamp(TurretHUD_EmitScale, 0.04, 2)
    Emitter_Scale = Sc
    local EmPos = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitPos) : entity():toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitPos)
    local EmAng = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitAng) : entity():toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitAng)
    local Em = spawnEgpEmitter(EmPos, EmAng, 1)
    if(Em:isValid()){
        Em:noCollideAll(1)
        Em:propNotSolid(1)
        Em:propFreeze(1)
        TurretHUD_Emitter = Em
        TurretHUD_EmitterCreated = 1
        TurretHUD_EmitterSeated = 0
        Em:createWire(entity(), "Scale", "Emitter_Scale")
        local SE = Em:wirelink()
        if(SE:entity():isValid()){
            createEmitterStatus(SE)
        }
        setupEmitterHolo(Em, Sc)
        printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[HUD] Emitter (re)spawned Scale=" + Sc)
    }else{
        TurretHUD_EmitterRetryT = curtime() + 1
        printColor(vec(255,180,0), "[HUD] Emitter spawn failed - keep retrying")
    }
}

function void seatEmitterStep(){
    # Retry until the emitter is actually seated on the base.
    if(!(TurretHUD_EmitterCreated && TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid())){ return }
    if(!BaseEnt:isValid()){ return }   # base not up yet; keep timer alive to retry later
    TurretHUD_Emitter:parentTo(BaseEnt)
    TurretHUD_Emitter:propFreeze(1)
    if(TurretHUD_Emitter:pos():distance(BaseEnt:toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitPos)) <= 8){
        TurretHUD_EmitterSeated = 1
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat") }
        printColor(vec(0,255,100), "[HUD] Emitter seated")
    }
}

function void maintainEmitterAndHolo(){
    if(!TurretHUD_UseEmitter){ return }

    # ---- 1. If NOT yet seated and sitting in the wrong spot, force a fresh spawn.
    # Only acts pre-seating so a rotating base doesn't trigger constant respawns.
    if(TurretHUD_EmitterCreated && TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid() && TurretHUD_EmitterSeated == 0 && BaseEnt:isValid()){
        if(TurretHUD_Emitter:pos():distance(BaseEnt:toWorld(TurretHUD_EmitPos)) > 8
            && curtime() > TurretHUD_EmitterRetryT + 2){
            TurretHUD_EmitterRetryT = curtime()
            printColor(vec(255,180,0), "[HUD] Emitter mis-seated - forced respawn")
            resetEmitterTracked()
        }
    }

    # ---- 2. If missing/invalid, spawn it (and its backing holo).
    if(!(TurretHUD_EmitterCreated && TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid())){
        spawnEmitterFresh()
    }

    # ---- 3. Ensure the backing holo exists; recreate if lost (holos die on chip reset).
    if(TurretHUD_EmitterCreated && TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid() && !holoEntity(90100):isValid()){
        setupEmitterHolo(TurretHUD_Emitter, clamp(TurretHUD_EmitScale, 0.04, 2))
    }

    # ---- 4. Keep the seat loop running until the emitter is seated.
    if(TurretHUD_EmitterCreated && TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid() && TurretHUD_EmitterSeated == 0){
        if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat")){
            timer(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat", 0.5, 0, function(){ seatEmitterStep() })
        }
    }
}

function void drawTurretHUDTick(){
    if(!TurretHUD_Enabled && !TurretHUD_UseEmitter){ return }

    # ---- Self-healing Player HUD overlay ----
    # The HUD screen prop can die or never spawn on first boot. Recreate it here
    # every draw tick so the retry never "runs out" - the original one-shot setup
    # only got a handful of retries, so the HUD was flaky. This covers the HUD side.
    if(TurretHUD_Enabled && !(TurretHUD_ScreenCreated && TurretHUD_Screen:isValid())){
        local HPos = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:toWorld(TurretHUD_Offset) : entity():toWorld(TurretHUD_Offset)
        local HAng = BaseEnt:isValid() ? BaseEnt:toWorld(ang(0,0,0)) : entity():toWorld(ang(0,0,0))
        local H = spawnEgpHud(HPos, HAng, 1)
        if(H:isValid()){
            H:noCollideAll(1)
            H:propFreeze(1)
            H:propNotSolid(0)
            TurretHUD_Screen = H
            TurretHUD_ScreenCreated = 1
            local S = H:wirelink()
            if(S:entity():isValid()){
                S:egpHudEnable(1)
                createHUDObjects(S)
            }
            printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[HUD] Player HUD (re)spawned - press E within ~5s to connect")
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_parent_delay")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "hud_parent_delay") }
            timer(TimerPrefix + "hud_parent_delay", 0.5, function(){
                if(TurretHUD_Screen:isValid()){
                    TurretHUD_Screen:propNotSolid(1)
                    if(holoEntity(9001):isValid()){ TurretHUD_Screen:parentTo(holoEntity(9001)) }
                }
            })
        }
    }elseif(TurretHUD_Enabled && TurretHUD_ScreenCreated && TurretHUD_Screen:isValid()){
        drawHUDObjects(TurretHUD_Screen:wirelink())
    }

    if(TurretHUD_UseEmitter){
        # Forced maintain: respawn the emitter and backing holo until both are
        # present AND seated. Runs every draw tick, so a failed/missing emitter
        # never stays missing (no one-shot retry window).
        maintainEmitterAndHolo()

        if(TurretHUD_EmitterCreated && TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid()){
            local EW = TurretHUD_Emitter:wirelink()
            if(EW:entity():isValid()){
                updateEmitterStatus(EW)
                Emitter_Scale = clamp(TurretHUD_EmitScale, 0.04, 2)
                local EffScale = clamp(Emitter_Scale * 0.25, 0.01, 0.5)
                local EmitPosCorrect = vec(64 - 256*EffScale, 0, 7 + 256*EffScale) + TurretHUD_EmitContentOffset
                EW["Scale", number] = Emitter_Scale
                EW["Position", vector] = EmitPosCorrect
                EW["Angle", angle] = TurretHUD_EmitAngOffset
                syncEmitterHolo(TurretHUD_Emitter, TurretHUD_EmitScale)
            }
        }
    }
}

function void setupTurretHUD(){
    if(!TurretHUD_Enabled && !TurretHUD_UseEmitter){ return }
    if(!BaseEnt:isValid()){
        # Base not ready yet  retry shortly
        if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_retry")){
            timer(TimerPrefix + "hud_retry", 0.5, 20, function(){ setupTurretHUD() })
        }
        return
    }

    local NeedRetry = 0

    # ---- Player HUD ----
    if(TurretHUD_Enabled && !(TurretHUD_ScreenCreated && TurretHUD_Screen:isValid())){
        local H = spawnEgpHud(BaseEnt:toWorld(TurretHUD_Offset), BaseEnt:toWorld(ang(0,0,0)), 1)
        if(H:isValid()){
            H:noCollideAll(1)
            H:propFreeze(1)
            H:propNotSolid(0)
            TurretHUD_Screen = H
            TurretHUD_ScreenCreated = 1
            local S = H:wirelink()
            if(S:entity():isValid()){
                S:egpHudEnable(1)
                createHUDObjects(S)
            }
            printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[HUD] Player HUD spawned - press E within ~5s to connect")
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_parent_delay")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "hud_parent_delay") }
            timer(TimerPrefix + "hud_parent_delay", 0.5, function(){
                if(TurretHUD_Screen:isValid()){
                    TurretHUD_Screen:propNotSolid(1)
                    if(holoEntity(9001):isValid()){ TurretHUD_Screen:parentTo(holoEntity(9001)) }
                }
            })
        }else{
            NeedRetry = 1
            printColor(vec(255,180,0), "[HUD] spawnEgpHud failed - will retry")
        }
    }

    # ---- Emitter + backing holo ----
    # Single code path shared with the draw tick: spawn + holo + seat retry.
    if(TurretHUD_UseEmitter){
        maintainEmitterAndHolo()
    }

    if(NeedRetry){
        if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_retry")){
            timer(TimerPrefix + "hud_retry", 0.5, 20, function(){ setupTurretHUD() })
        }
    }else{
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_retry")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "hud_retry") }
    }

    if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_draw")){
        timer(TimerPrefix + "hud_draw", TurretHUD_DrawInterval, 0, function(){ drawTurretHUDTick() })
    }
}

function void disableTurretHUD(){
    if(TurretHUD_Screen:isValid()){
        TurretHUD_Screen:wirelink():egpHudEnable(0)
        TurretHUD_Screen:propDelete()
        TurretHUD_Screen:admPropDelete()
    }
    if(TurretHUD_Emitter:isValid()){
        TurretHUD_Emitter:wirelink():egpHudEnable(0)
        TurretHUD_Emitter:propDelete()
        TurretHUD_Emitter:admPropDelete()
    }
    if(holoEntity(9002):isValid()){ holoDelete(9002) }
    if(holoEntity(90100):isValid()){ holoDelete(90100) }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "hud_draw")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "hud_draw") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "emitter_seat") }
    TurretHUD_Screen = noentity()
    TurretHUD_ScreenCreated = 0
    TurretHUD_Emitter = noentity()
    TurretHUD_EmitterCreated = 0
    TurretHUD_EmitterSeated = 0
}

function void debugAutoReload() {
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "--- AUTO-RELOAD DEBUG INFO ---")
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Auto_Reload_Enabled: " + Auto_Reload_Enabled)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Ammo_Count: " + Ammo_Count + "/" + Max_Ammo)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Auto_Reload_Threshold: " + Auto_Reload_Threshold)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Auto_Reload_Resume_Threshold: " + Auto_Reload_Resume_Threshold)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Auto_Reload_Triggered: " + Auto_Reload_Triggered)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Auto_Reload_Paused: " + Auto_Reload_Paused)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Refilling: " + Refilling)
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Target: " + (Target:isValid() ? shortenTargetName(Target) : "none"))
    if(Reload_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        local Distance = getDistanceToReloadProp()
        local DistanceFeet = floor(Distance / 16)
        printColor(vec(0,255,255), "Reload Prop Distance: " + DistanceFeet + " feet")
    }
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "-------------------------------")
}

function void updateKinematics(){
    local CurrentTime = curtime()
    CurrentTargetPos = getAimPoint(Target)
    TargetLocation = CurrentTargetPos

    if(LastTargetUpdate > 0 && CurrentTime - LastTargetUpdate >= VelocityUpdateInterval){
        local TimeDelta = CurrentTime - LastTargetUpdate
        if(TimeDelta > 0){
            local RawVel = (CurrentTargetPos - PrevTargetPos) / TimeDelta
            TargetVel = smoothVelocity(RawVel)
            if(PrevTargetVel:length() > 0){
                TargetAccel = (TargetVel - PrevTargetVel) / TimeDelta
            }
            PrevTargetVel = TargetVel
            PrevTargetPos = CurrentTargetPos
            LastTargetUpdate = CurrentTime
        }
    }elseif(LastTargetUpdate == 0){
        PrevTargetPos = CurrentTargetPos
        LastTargetUpdate = CurrentTime
        TargetVel = Target:vel()
        PrevTargetVel = TargetVel
    }
    
if(UseIntercept){
    local InterceptPos = CurrentTargetPos

    if(WeaponType == "bolt" && BoltSpeed > 0){
        if(TargetVel:length() > 1){
            local ShootingPoint = Gun:pos()

            if(holoEntity(4583):isValid()){
                ShootingPoint = holoEntity(4583):pos()
            }

   InterceptPos = calculateBoltIntercept(
    ShootingPoint,
    CurrentTargetPos,
    TargetVel,
    BoltSpeed
)
            
            
        }
    }elseif(WeaponType == "hitscan"){
        InterceptPos = calculateHitscanIntercept(CurrentTargetPos, TargetVel, TargetAccel)
    }

    TargetLocation = InterceptPos
}





    if(DebugMode == 1){
        holoPos(2, TargetLocation)
        holoPos(3, CurrentTargetPos)
        holoPos(4, CurrentTargetPos + TargetVel:normalized() * min(TargetVel:length() * 3, 80))
        holoPos(6, holoEntity(8600):pos())
    }
}

# Name of the hive turret currently holding Tgt ("" = nobody). Self-contained
# (direct gTable reads) so it can live before the hive helper functions.
function string hiveNameOfTarget(Tgt:entity){
    if(!Tgt:isValid() || HiveName == ""){ return "" }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
        if(I == HiveSlot){ continue }
        if(GT["s" + I + "_sig", number] == 0){ continue }
        if(curtime() > GT["s" + I + "_hb", number] + 5){ continue }
        if(GT["s" + I + "_tgt", number] == Tgt:id()){
            return GT["s" + I + "_name", string]
        }
    }
    return ""
}

function void statusTick(){
    if(Battery_Shutdown){
        Battery_Status = "LOW POWER"
    }elseif(Battery_Fire_Paused){
        Battery_Status = "RECHARGING"
    }elseif(Battery_Percent <= Battery_LowThreshold){
        Battery_Status = "WARNING"
    }elseif(Battery_Charging){
        Battery_Status = "CHARGING"
    }else{
        Battery_Status = "NORMAL"
    }

    Display_Battery_Smooth += (Battery_Current - Display_Battery_Smooth) * clamp(Status_Rate * 7.5, 0.02, 1)
    Display_Battery_Percent = Display_Battery_Smooth

    if(TurretActive_Real){
        holoAlpha(10001, 0)
        holoAlpha(10002, 255)
    }else{
        holoAlpha(10001, 255)
        holoAlpha(10002, 0)
        RED = 0
        GREEN = 0
        BLUE = 0
    }

    if(BatteryEnt:isValid()){
        if(Battery_Percent > 50){
            holoColor(500, vec(0, 255, 0))
            BatteryEnt:setColor(vec(0, 255, 0))
            RED = 0
            GREEN = 255
        }elseif(Battery_Percent > Battery_LowThreshold){
            holoColor(500, vec(255, 255, 0))
            BatteryEnt:setColor(vec(255, 255, 0))
            RED = 255
            GREEN = 255
        }else{
            holoColor(500, vec(255, 0, 0))
            BatteryEnt:setColor(vec(255, 0, 0))
            RED = 255
            GREEN = 0
        }
    }

    # Use effective desired state so remote mode does not depend on the wired TurretActive input
    local DesiredForStatus = ((WeaponSender_Enabled == 1) || (RemoteToggleEnabled == 1)) ? RemoteState : TurretActive
    if(!DesiredForStatus){
        holoColor(500, vec(5))
        lightToggle(LightBase + 101, 1)
    }

    if(RechargeStation:isValid()){
        local State = ""
        local Col = vec(255)
        local TextCol = vec(200,200,200)
        if(!Recharge_In_Range){
            Col = vec(0,100,200)
            TextCol = vec(255,0,0)
            State = "CHARGER: RED (OUT OF RANGE)"
        }elseif(!TurretActive_Real){
            Col = vec(0,0,0)
            TextCol = vec(100,100,100)
            State = "CHARGER: BLACK (TURRET OFF)"
        }elseif(Battery_Percent >= 100){
            Col = vec(255)
            TextCol = vec(0,200,255)
            State = "CHARGER: WHITE (FULLY CHARGED)"
        }elseif(Battery_Fire_Paused){
            Col = vec(255,165,0)
            TextCol = vec(255,165,0)
            State = "CHARGER: ORANGE (PAUSED - CHARGING TO " + (HasValidTarget ? Fire_Resume_Threshold : 100) + "%)"
        }elseif(Fire){
            Col = vec(100,100,100)
            TextCol = vec(150,150,150)
            State = "CHARGER: GRAY (FIRING - CANNOT CHARGE)"
        }elseif(!HasValidTarget && Battery_Percent < 100){
            Col = vec(0,255,0)
            TextCol = vec(0,255,0)
            State = "CHARGER: GREEN (NO TARGETS - TOPPING TO 100%)"
        }elseif(Battery_Percent < Battery_Startup_Threshold){
            Col = vec(255,255,0)
            TextCol = vec(255,255,0)
            State = "CHARGER: YELLOW (CHARGING TO " + Battery_Startup_Threshold + "%)"
        }else{
            Col = vec(255)
            TextCol = vec(200,200,200)
            State = "CHARGER: WHITE (STANDBY)"
        }
        RechargeStation:setColor(Col)
        if(Recharge_prop_chat && State != LastChargeState && curtime() - LastChargeMsgTime >= ChargeMsgCooldown){
            printColor(TextCol, State)
            LastChargeState = State
            LastChargeMsgTime = curtime()
        }
    }

    if(BatteryEnt:isValid()){
        holoParent(500, BatteryEnt)
    }

    # Hive backup status: are we engaging a target another turret holds?
    local NewBackup = ""
    if(HasValidTarget && Target:isValid() && HiveName != ""){
        NewBackup = hiveNameOfTarget(Target)
    }
    BackingUp = NewBackup != "" ? 1 : 0
    BackupTurret = NewBackup
    if(NewBackup != PrevBackupTurret){
        if(NewBackup != ""){
            printColor(vec(255,170,0), " HELPING OUT TURRET " + NewBackup:upper())
        }
        PrevBackupTurret = NewBackup
    }

    # Built-in EGP status display (uses internal values, no extra wires)
    updateEGPScreen()
}

function void reloadTick(){
    if(Refilling && Reload_Prop:entity():isValid() && isReloadPropInRange()) {
        local AmmoToAdd = min(Reload_Rate, Max_Ammo - Ammo_Count)
        if(AmmoToAdd > 0) {
            Ammo_Count += AmmoToAdd
            if(Ammo_Count < Max_Ammo) {
                timer(TimerPrefix + "reload_tick", Reload_Tick_Delay, function(){ reloadTick() })
            } else {
                Refilling = 0
                Auto_Reload_Triggered = 0
                Gun:soundPlay(4, 0.7, "@weapons/357/357_reload4.wav")
                printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[AMMO] Reload complete! Ammo: " + Ammo_Count + "/" + Max_Ammo)
            }
        } else {
            Refilling = 0
            Auto_Reload_Triggered = 0
        }
    } else {
        Refilling = 0
        Auto_Reload_Triggered = 0
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[AMMO] Reload interrupted!")
    }
}

function void ammoDockScan(){
    if(MagSeated) {
        Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
        stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
        hideAmmoDockMarker()
    } elseif(curtime() <= JustReset) {
        if(Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop) { timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() }) }
    } elseif(Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop && holoEntity(56):isValid()) {
        if(!findCanQuery()) {
            timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
        } else {
            findClearBlackList()
            findClearWhiteList()
            findExcludeClass("gmod_wire_hologram")
            findExcludeClass("player")
            findExcludeEntity(entity())
            if(Gun:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Gun) }
            if(Base:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Base) }
            if(MagEnt:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(MagEnt) }
            if(Ammo_EjectedEnt:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Ammo_EjectedEnt) }

            findByModel("models/Items/BoxSRounds.mdl")
            findSortByDistance(holoEntity(56):pos())
            local P = find()

            if(P:isValid() && P != Ammo_EjectedEnt && !isOwnedElsewhere(P)) {
                local Dist = P:pos():distance(holoEntity(56):pos())
                if(MagEntIndex > 0 && P:id() == MagEntIndex) {
                    MagEnt = P
                    MagSeated = 1
                    Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
                    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
                    hideAmmoDockMarker()
                    if(Ammo_Count <= 0) { 
                        Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds)
                        Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo 
                    }
                    printColor(vec(0,255,100), "[AMMO] Scan re-bound existing mag (dist=" + floor(Dist) + ")")
                } elseif(Dist < Ammo_Dock_Radius) {
                    printColor(vec(0, 255, 0), "[AMMO] GRABBING")
                    seatAmmoProp(P)
                    Ammo_EjectedEnt = noentity()
                } else {
                    if(curtime() > Last_Warning_Time + 2) {
                        printColor(vec(0,255,255), "[SCAN] BoxSRounds dist=" + floor(Dist) + " (need < " + Ammo_Dock_Radius + ")")
                        Last_Warning_Time = curtime()
                    }
                    timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
                }
            } else {
                if(!findCanQuery()) {
                    timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
                } else {
                    findClearBlackList()
                    findClearWhiteList()
                    findExcludeClass("gmod_wire_hologram")
                    findExcludeClass("player")
                    findExcludeEntity(entity())
                    if(Gun:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Gun) }
                    if(Base:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Base) }
                    if(MagEnt:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(MagEnt) }
                    if(Ammo_EjectedEnt:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Ammo_EjectedEnt) }
                    findInSphere(holoEntity(56):pos(), Ammo_Dock_Radius)
                    findSortByDistance(holoEntity(56):pos())
                    P = find()

                    if(P:isValid() && P:model():lower():find("boxsrounds") && P != Ammo_EjectedEnt && !isOwnedElsewhere(P)) {
                        if(MagEntIndex > 0 && P:id() == MagEntIndex) {
                            MagEnt = P
                            MagSeated = 1
                            Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
                            stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
                            hideAmmoDockMarker()
                            if(Ammo_Count <= 0) { 
                                Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds)
                                Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo 
                            }
                            printColor(vec(0,255,100), "[AMMO] Sphere re-bound existing mag")
                        } else {
                            printColor(vec(0, 255, 0), "[AMMO] GRABBING (sphere)")
                            seatAmmoProp(P)
                            Ammo_EjectedEnt = noentity()
                        }
                    } elseif(P:isValid()) {
                        local Mdl = P:model():lower()
                        if(!Mdl:find("hologram") && !Mdl:find("hq_cylinder") && !Mdl:find("hq_sphere")) {
                            printColor(vec(255, 165, 0), "[SCAN DEBUG] Nearest prop in range: model='" + P:model() + "' dist=" + floor(P:pos():distance(holoEntity(56):pos())))
                        }
                        timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
                    } else {
                        timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } elseif(!getAmmoEntity():isValid()) {
        timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
    }
}

function void showAmmoDockMarker() {
    if(MagSeated) { return }
    if(holoEntity(5602):isValid()) {
        holoAlpha(5602, 255)
        holoScale(5602, vec(1.4, 1.4, 0.1))
    }
    Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 1
    if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")) {
        timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
    }
}

function void recoverAmmoBox() {
    if(MagSeated && MagEnt:isValid() && Gun:isValid()) {
        # If another turret stole the box, release our claim and go find another
        if(isOwnedElsewhere(MagEnt)){
            MagSeated = 0
            MagEnt = noentity()
            MagEntIndex = 0
            Ammo_Count = 0
            showAmmoDockMarker()
            printColor(vec(255,150,0), "[AMMO] Box claimed by another turret - releasing claim")
            return
        }
        # Already good  still ensure parent / pos / angle match the config
        if(magNeedsRepos()) { repositionMagOnly(MagEnt) }
        return
    }

    if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        seatAmmoProp(Ammo_Prop:entity())
        return
    }

    if(MagEntIndex > 0) {
        local E = entity(MagEntIndex)
        if(E:isValid() && !isOwnedElsewhere(E)) {
            MagEnt = E
            MagSeated = 1
            if(Max_Ammo <= 0) { Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
            if(Ammo_Count <= 0) { Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo }
            hideAmmoDockMarker()
            if(Gun:isValid()) { repositionMagOnly(E) }
            if(timerExists("retry_ammo_recovery")){ stoptimer("retry_ammo_recovery") }
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover") }
            return
        }
        # Box gone or stolen by another turret - clear the claim so we can find a fresh one
        MagEnt = noentity()
        MagEntIndex = 0
        MagSeated = 0
    }

    if(MagEnt:isValid()) {
        if(isOwnedElsewhere(MagEnt)){
            MagEnt = noentity()
            MagEntIndex = 0
            MagSeated = 0
        } else {
            MagSeated = 1
            MagEntIndex = MagEnt:id()
            if(Max_Ammo <= 0) { Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
            if(Ammo_Count <= 0) { Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo }
            hideAmmoDockMarker()
            if(Gun:isValid()) { repositionMagOnly(MagEnt) }
            if(timerExists("retry_ammo_recovery")){ stoptimer("retry_ammo_recovery") }
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover") }
            return
        }
    }

    local SearchFrom = Gun:isValid() ? Gun:pos() : entity():pos()
    if(findCanQuery()) {
        findClearBlackList()
        findClearWhiteList()
        findExcludeClass("player")
        findExcludeClass("gmod_wire_hologram")
        findExcludeEntity(entity())
        if(Gun:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Gun) }
        if(Base:isValid()) { findExcludeEntity(Base) }
        findInSphere(SearchFrom, 250)
        findSortByDistance(SearchFrom)
        local Arr = findToArray()
        local Box = noentity()
        for(I = 1, Arr:count()) {
            local C = Arr[I, entity]
            if(!C:isValid()) { continue }
            local M = C:model():lower()
            if(isOwnedElsewhere(C)){ continue }
            if(M:find("boxsrounds") || M:find("boxmrounds") || M:find("boxbuckshot") || M:find("item_ammo") || M:find("items/box")) {
                Box = C
                break
            }
        }
        if(!Box:isValid()) {
            # fallback: exact model path either casing
            findByModel("models/Items/BoxSRounds.mdl")
            findSortByDistance(SearchFrom)
            Box = find()
            if(Box:isValid() && isOwnedElsewhere(Box)) { Box = noentity() }
            if(!Box:isValid()) {
                findByModel("models/items/boxsrounds.mdl")
                findSortByDistance(SearchFrom)
                Box = find()
                if(Box:isValid() && isOwnedElsewhere(Box)) { Box = noentity() }
            }
        }
        if(Box:isValid()) {
            MagEnt = Box
            MagEntIndex = Box:id()
            MagSeated = 1
            if(Max_Ammo <= 0) { Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
            if(Ammo_Count <= 0) { Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo }
            hideAmmoDockMarker()
            repositionMagOnly(Box)
            if(timerExists("retry_ammo_recovery")){ stoptimer("retry_ammo_recovery") }
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover") }
            return
        }
    }

    # Keep retrying after dupe until Gun + find budget are ready (quiet)
    if(!MagSeated && !timerExists("retry_ammo_recovery")) {
        timer("retry_ammo_recovery", 0.4, function(){ recoverAmmoBox() })
    }
}

# ============================================================
# HIVEMIND TARGET COORDINATION
# ============================================================
# Shared "gTable" memory so nearby turrets running the same code
# don't all shoot the same target. ZERO wiring needed: every turret
# claims a slot in the global table "turret_hive" and writes its
# current target entity id every 0.5s. Every turret reads everyone
# else's claims and skips targets already held by a live turret,
# only falling back to a claimed target when nothing else is free
# (so a single lone attacker still gets engaged by multiple guns).

function number hiveGetSig(SlotT:number){
    return gTable(HiveName)["s" + SlotT + "_sig", number]
}

function number hiveGetHeartbeat(SlotT:number){
    return gTable(HiveName)["s" + SlotT + "_hb", number]
}

function number hiveGetTarget(SlotT:number){
    return gTable(HiveName)["s" + SlotT + "_tgt", number]
}

# Claim a free / abandoned slot (abandoned = no heartbeat for 5s)
function void hiveClaimSlot(){
    if(HiveSlots <= 0){ HiveSlots = 8 }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    HiveSlot = 0
    for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
        if(GT["s" + I + "_sig", number] == UniqueID){
            HiveSlot = I
            break
        }
    }
    if(HiveSlot == 0){
        for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
            local Sig = GT["s" + I + "_sig", number]
            local Hbt = GT["s" + I + "_hb", number]
            if(Sig == 0 || curtime() > Hbt + 5){
                HiveSlot = I
                break
            }
        }
    }
    if(HiveSlot > 0){
        GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_sig", number] = UniqueID
        GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_hb", number] = curtime()
        # Sync cmd sequence so a leftover ON from a previous chip/session is NOT
        # replayed the moment power comes back (that was auto-arming on battery insert).
        LastHiveCmdSeq = GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_cmdseq", number]
        GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_cmd", number] = 0
        GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_on", number] = 0
    }
}

# This turret writes its current target id (0 = none) + heartbeat
function void hiveBroadcast(){
    if(HiveName == ""){ return }
    if(!Setup_Done){ return }
    if(HiveSlot <= 0 || HiveSlot > HiveSlots){ hiveClaimSlot() }
    if(HiveSlot <= 0){ return }
    if(hiveGetSig(HiveSlot) != 0 && hiveGetSig(HiveSlot) != UniqueID){ hiveClaimSlot() }
    if(HiveSlot <= 0){ return }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_hb", number] = curtime()
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_tgt", number] = Target:isValid() ? Target:id() : 0
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_tdist", number] = (HasValidTarget && Target:isValid() && Target:isAlive() && BaseEnt:isValid()) ? floor(toUnit("ft", (Target:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length())) : -1
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_on", number] = TurretActive_Real
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_batt", number] = Battery_Percent
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_name", string] = TurretName == "" ? BaseName : TurretName
    local HeatVal = -1
    if(Heat_Max > 0){
        if(Heat_Max > Heat_Min && Heat_Min > 0){
            # Scale so idle heat (Heat_Min) reads exactly Heat_Min% and max reads 100%
            HeatVal = clamp(Heat_Min + (Heat - Heat_Min) * (100 - Heat_Min) / (Heat_Max - Heat_Min), 0, 100)
        }else{
            HeatVal = clamp(Heat / Heat_Max * 100, 0, 100)
        }
    }
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_heat", number] = HeatVal
    local AmmoCap = Max_Ammo > 0 ? Max_Ammo : (Max_rounds > 0 ? floor(Max_rounds) : -1)
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_acur", number] = AmmoCap > 0 ? max(0, Ammo_Count) : -1
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_amax", number] = AmmoCap
    local ChgCode = 0
    if(RechargeStation:isValid()){
        if(Recharge_In_Range){
            if(Battery_Charging && Battery_Percent < 100){
                ChgCode = 1
            }elseif(Battery_Percent >= 100){
                ChgCode = 2
            }else{
                ChgCode = 3
            }
        }else{
            ChgCode = 4
        }
    }
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_chrg", number] = ChgCode
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_e", number] = EGP:isValid() ? 1 : 0
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_sw", number] = round(swingOffsetScreen())
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_bk", number] = BackingUp ? 1 : 0
    GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_bkn", string] = BackingUp ? BackupTurret : ""
}

# Array of entity-ids currently claimed by OTHER live turrets
function array hiveClaimedByOthers(){
    local IDs = array()
    for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
        if(I == HiveSlot){ continue }
        if(hiveGetSig(I) == 0){ continue }
        if(curtime() > hiveGetHeartbeat(I) + 5){ continue }
        local Tgt = hiveGetTarget(I)
        if(Tgt > 0){ IDs:pushNumber(Tgt) }
    }
    return IDs
}

# Fallback: nearest valid target held by another live turret. Used only
# when every candidate in the queue is already claimed by someone else,
# so a lone target still gets engaged instead of ignored. Each turret
# still applies its OWN line-of-sight check here - a claimed target
# you cannot see is never aimed at, no matter what the other turret says.
function entity hiveNearestClaimed(){
    local Best = noentity()
    local BestDist = Dist
    for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
        if(I == HiveSlot){ continue }
        if(hiveGetSig(I) == 0){ continue }
        if(curtime() > hiveGetHeartbeat(I) + 5){ continue }
        local T = entity(hiveGetTarget(I))
        if(!T:isValid() || !T:isAlive() || T:health() <= 0){ continue }
        if(isWhitelisted(T)){ continue }
        if(!isInTurretRange(T)){ continue }
        # Don't track/fire at a target we cannot actually see ourselves
        if(!hasLineOfSight(T)){ continue }
        if((T:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length() > Dist){ continue }
        local D = (T:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length()
        if(D < BestDist){
            BestDist = D
            Best = T
        }
    }
    return Best
}

# returns 1 if Val is present in Lookup, 0 otherwise
function number hiveInArray(Val:number, Lookup:array){
    for(J = 1, Lookup:count()){
        if(Lookup[J, number] == Val){ return 1 }
    }
    return 0
}

function entity nextFromQueue(){
    local LosChecks = 0
    local Claimed = hiveClaimedByOthers()
    while(TargetQueueSize > 0){
        local Candidate = TargetQueue[1, entity]
        TargetQueue:removeEntity(1)
        TargetQueueScore:removeNumber(1)
        TargetQueueSize--
        TargetCount = TargetQueueSize
        # Cheap rejects first  avoid ranger (LOS) on dead bodies
        if(!Candidate:isValid() || !Candidate:isAlive() || Candidate:health() <= 0){ continue }
        if(isWhitelisted(Candidate)){ continue }
        if(!isInTurretRange(Candidate)){ continue }
        # Skip targets another live turret is already engaging
        if(hiveInArray(Candidate:id(), Claimed)){ continue }
        LosChecks++
        if(LosChecks > 5){ break }   # hard cap ranger spam when queue is full of blocked/dead
        if(hasLineOfSight(Candidate)){
            return Candidate
        }
    }
    return noentity()
}

function void refreshQueue(){
    local Ents = array()
    local Scores = array()
    local Size = 0
    local Claimed = hiveClaimedByOthers()

    for(I = 1, TargetQueueSize){
        local Ent = TargetQueue[I, entity]
        if(Ent == Target){ continue }
        if(!isValidTarget(Ent)){ continue }
        if(!isInTurretRange(Ent)){ continue }
        if((Ent:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length() > Dist){ continue }
        if(hiveInArray(Ent:id(), Claimed)){ continue }

        local Score = targetScore(Ent)
        local Slot = Size + 1
        for(J = 1, Size){
            if(Score < Scores[J, number]){
                Slot = J
                break
            }
        }
        Ents:insertEntity(Slot, Ent)
        Scores:insertNumber(Slot, Score)
        Size++
    }

    TargetQueue = Ents
    TargetQueueScore = Scores
    TargetQueueSize = Size
    TargetCount = Size
}

function void startAutoReload() {
    if(curtime() <= JustReset) { return }
    if(!Reload_Prop:entity():isValid()) { 
        Auto_Reload_Enabled = 0
        return 
    }
    if(!isReloadPropInRange()) {
        if(Debug_Mode) { printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[AUTO-RELOAD] Reload prop too far.") }
        return
    }
    Refilling = 1
    Auto_Reload_Triggered = 1
    Auto_Reload_Paused = 1
    Gun:soundPlay(3, 0.5, "@weapons/357/357_reload1.wav")
    printColor(vec(0,255,255), "[AUTO-RELOAD] Starting automatic reload...")
    holoColor(3000, vec(0, 255, 255))
    holoAlpha(3001, 0)
    holoAlpha(3002, 0)
    holoAlpha(3003, 0)
    timer(TimerPrefix + "reload_tick", Reload_Tick_Delay, function(){ reloadTick() })
    logAutoReloadState("Auto-reload started")
}

function void retryAmmoRecovery(){
    recoverAmmoBox()
}

# 1 = this turret currently has something to actually fire. Infinite ammo counts.
# With no box seated or 0 rounds there is no point scanning/searching for targets.
function number hasAmmoToFire(){
    if(Max_Ammo <= 0){ return 1 }
    if(!MagSeated || !MagEnt:isValid()){ return 0 }
    return (Ammo_Count > 0) ? 1 : 0
}

# Apply the configured halo (incl. per-NPC crisp overrides) to a single NPC.
function void applyNPCHalo(Ent:entity, D:number){
    Ent:setHaloEnabled(1)
    Ent:setHaloColor(HaloColor:x(), HaloColor:y(), HaloColor:z())
    Ent:setHaloAlpha(HaloAlpha)
    local UseSharp = SmallHaloEnabled == 1 && isSharpNPC(Ent)
    local BaseSize = UseSharp ? SmallHaloSize : HaloSize
    local BasePasses = UseSharp ? SmallHaloPasses : HaloPasses
    local HaloScale = 1
    local HaloPassScale = 1
    if(HaloDistanceScale == 1 && D > HaloDistFloor && HaloDistFloor >= 0){
        local BeyondFloor = (D - HaloDistFloor) / (Dist - HaloDistFloor)
        if(BeyondFloor > 1){ BeyondFloor = 1 }
        if(BeyondFloor < 0){ BeyondFloor = 0 }
        HaloScale = 1 + (HaloDistMax - 1) * BeyondFloor
        HaloPassScale = 1 + (HaloPassMax - 1) * BeyondFloor
    }
    Ent:setHaloSize(BaseSize * HaloScale)
    Ent:setHaloPasses(max(1, floor(BasePasses * HaloPassScale + 0.5)))
    Ent:setHaloAdd(HaloAdditive)
    Ent:setHaloZDepth(HaloIgnoreZ)
}

function void buildTargetQueue(){
    if(!Setup_Done){ return }

    if(!TurretActive_Real || !Power_Available || !BaseEnt:isValid() || !hasAmmoToFire()){
        TargetQueue = array()
        TargetQueueScore = array()
        TargetQueueSize = 0
        TargetCount = 0
        return
    }

    if(!canScan()){
        # out of find budget this moment - keep the queue we already have
        refreshQueue()
        return
    }

    TargetQueue = array()
    TargetQueueScore = array()
    TargetQueueSize = 0
    TargetCount = 0

    # Sphere search  old FOV/8 cone (~12) missed anything not almost dead-ahead of the base.
    # isInTurretRange still limits to the turret's real yaw/pitch envelope afterward.
    # Re-apply target filters each scan: recoverBattery()/recoverAmmoBox()/ammoDockScan()
    # clobber the chip's find include/exclude lists with battery/mag-only filters, which
    # made scans return nothing usable until the E2 was reset.
    applyTargetFindFilters()
    findInSphere(BaseEnt:pos(), Dist)
    findSortByDistance(BaseEnt:pos())
    local Candidates = findToArray()
    local Claimed = hiveClaimedByOthers()

    local Checked = 0
    foreach(I:number, Ent:entity = Candidates){
        I = I
        if(TargetQueueSize >= Max_Targets){ break }
        Checked++
        if(Checked > 25){ break }   # was 40  corpses inflate the list; don't burn ops on them

        if(Ent == Target){ continue }
        # Cheap dead/corpse reject BEFORE range math / scoring (big ops saver with many NPCs)
        if(!Ent:isValid() || !Ent:isAlive() || Ent:health() <= 0){ continue }
        if(isOwnProjectile(Ent)){ continue }
        if(isWhitelisted(Ent)){ continue }
        if(!isInTurretRange(Ent)){ continue }
        # Skip targets another live turret is already engaging
        if(hiveInArray(Ent:id(), Claimed)){ continue }

        local Score = targetScore(Ent)

        # insertion sort, lowest score first
        local Slot = TargetQueueSize + 1
        for(J = 1, TargetQueueSize){
            if(Score < TargetQueueScore[J, number]){
                Slot = J
                break
            }
        }
        TargetQueue:insertEntity(Slot, Ent)
        TargetQueueScore:insertNumber(Slot, Score)
        TargetQueueSize++
    }

    TargetCount = TargetQueueSize

    if(DebugMode == 1 && TargetQueueSize > 0){
        printColor(vec(0,200,255), "QUEUE (" + TargetQueueSize + "): nearest = " + shortenTargetName(TargetQueue[1, entity]) + " score " + round(TargetQueueScore[1, number]))
    }
}

function void ejectAmmoBox(Reason:string) {
    local E = getAmmoEntity()
    if(!E:isValid()) {
        Ammo_Count = 0
        Max_Ammo = 0
        MagEnt = noentity()
        MagEntIndex = 0
        MagSeated = 0
        showAmmoDockMarker()
        return
    }
    Ammo_EjectedEnt = E
    E:deparent()
    E:propFreeze(0)
    E:propNotSolid(0)
    local Dock = holoEntity(56)
    local Out = Dock:isValid() ? (Dock:right() + Dock:up() * 0.8 + Dock:forward() * 0.15) : vec(0, 1, 0.8)
    Out = Out:normalized()
    E:setPos(Dock:isValid() ? Dock:pos() + Out * 4 : E:pos())
    E:applyForce(Out * Ammo_Eject_Speed * E:mass() + vec(0, 0, Ammo_Eject_Up) * E:mass())
    Ammo_Count = 0
    Max_Ammo = 0
    Active = 0
    MagEnt = noentity()
    MagEntIndex = 0
    MagSeated = 0
    printColor(vec(255, 180, 0), "[AMMO] Magazine ejected (" + Reason + "). Drop a BoxSRounds on the green circle.")
    hint("Ammo ejected - drop a new box on the dock.", 5)
    Gun:soundPlay(12, 0.6, "@weapons/357/357_reload3.wav")
    showAmmoDockMarker()
}


# Decide whether to abandon a free target and stack on a teammate's claim.
# Mode 0 never prefers help over free. Mode 1/2 compare range + force band.
function number shouldHelpOverFree(FreeT:entity, ClaimedT:entity){
    if(Help_Aggressiveness <= 0){ return 0 }
    if(!ClaimedT:isValid() || !ClaimedT:isAlive()){ return 0 }
    if(!BaseEnt:isValid()){ return 0 }
    local Cd = (ClaimedT:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length()
    local Cap = Help_Max_Dist > 0 ? Help_Max_Dist : Dist
    if(Cd > Cap){ return 0 }
    if(!hasLineOfSight(ClaimedT)){ return 0 }

    # Always help if the claimed fight is right on top of us
    if(Help_Force_Dist > 0 && Cd <= Help_Force_Dist){ return 1 }

    if(!FreeT:isValid()){ return 1 }

    local Fd = (FreeT:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length()
    if(Help_Aggressiveness >= 2){
        # Aggressive: stack whenever claimed is not much farther than free
        if(Cd <= Fd * max(Help_Prefer_Ratio, 1.5)){ return 1 }
        return 0
    }
    # Mode 1: help when claimed is closer or only a bit farther than free
    if(Cd <= Fd * max(Help_Prefer_Ratio, 1.0)){ return 1 }
    return 0
}

function void engageAsHelp(New:entity){
    Target = New
    HasValidTarget = 1
    resetTracking()
    BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/blip2.wav", 100, 80, 0.95)
    local HelpName = hiveNameOfTarget(New)
    BackingUp = HelpName != "" ? 1 : 0
    BackupTurret = HelpName
    PrevBackupTurret = HelpName
    if(HelpName != ""){
        printColor(vec(255,170,0), " HELPING OUT TURRET " + HelpName:upper() + " on " + shortenTargetName(New))
    }else{
        printColor(vec(255,170,0), " FOCUS FIRE on " + shortenTargetName(New) + " (shared claim)")
    }
}

function void acquireTarget(){
    if(!TurretActive_Real || !Power_Available || !hasAmmoToFire()){
        Target = noentity()
        HasValidTarget = 0
        return
    }

    local New = nextFromQueue()
    if(!New:isValid()){
        buildTargetQueue()
        New = nextFromQueue()
    }

    local Claimed = hiveNearestClaimed()

    # Prefer helping a teammate when aggressiveness says the shared fight is better
    if(Claimed:isValid() && shouldHelpOverFree(New, Claimed)){
        engageAsHelp(Claimed)
    }elseif(New:isValid()){
        Target = New
        HasValidTarget = 1
        BackingUp = 0
        BackupTurret = ""
        resetTracking()
        BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/blip2.wav", 100, 80, 0.95)
        printColor(vec(50,150,200), " TARGET " + (Kill_Count + 1) + ": " + shortenTargetName(New) + " (" + floor(toUnit("ft", (New:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length())) + " ft, " + TargetQueueSize + " more queued)")
    }elseif(Claimed:isValid()){
        # No free targets - always fall back to help (any aggressiveness)
        engageAsHelp(Claimed)
    }else{
        Target = noentity()
        HasValidTarget = 0
        BackingUp = 0
        BackupTurret = ""
    }
    # Ping the hive RIGHT NOW so the other turret(s) see our claim instantly
    # instead of waiting up to 0.5s for the next heartbeat - closes the race
    # where two turrets grab the same target even when 2+ are available.
    hiveBroadcast()
}


function void applyTurretState(Desired:number){
    if(TurretActive_Real == Desired){ return }
    if(Desired){
        if(Power_Available){
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Turret is ARMED! (Battery: " + round(Battery_Percent) + "%)")
            BaseEnt:emitSound("@npc/turret_floor/turret_deploy_4.wav", 80, 100, 0.25)
            AimDown = 0
            ReturnToCenter = 1
            ReturnToCenterTimer = curtime() + TurnOn_Delay
            SweepDelayTimer = curtime() + TurnOn_Delay + Sweep_Delay
            Sweep_Dir = 1
            Sweep_Pausing = 0
            Sweep_PauseUntil = 0
            Sweep_Reenter = 0
            TurretActive_Real = 1
            # snap-scan immediately instead of waiting for the next scan tick
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "arm_scan")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "arm_scan") }
            timer(TimerPrefix + "arm_scan", 0.05, function(){
                buildTargetQueue()
                if(!Target:isValid()){ acquireTarget() }
            })
        }else{
            if(!BatteryEnt:isValid()){
                printColor(vec(255,0,0), "Turret ACTIVATION BLOCKED - No battery connected!")
            }else{
                printColor(vec(255,0,0), "Turret ACTIVATION BLOCKED - Insufficient Power! (" + round(Battery_Percent) + "%, need " + Battery_MinStartThreshold + "%)")
            }
            BaseEnt:emitSound("@hl1/fvox/power_level_is_unsafe.wav", 80, 80, 0.8)
        }
    }else{
        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "Turret is OFFLINE!")
        BaseEnt:emitSound("@npc/turret_floor/turret_disabled_4.wav", 80, 100, 0.25)
        AimDown = 1
        AimDownTimer = curtime() + TurnOff_Delay
        ReturnToCenter = 0
        TurretActive_Real = 0
        fireStop()
        Target = noentity()
        HasValidTarget = 0
        Retargeting = 0
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "retarget")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "retarget") }
        Battery_Fire_Paused = 0
        RED = 0
        GREEN = 0
        BLUE = 0
    }
}

function void gunTick(){
    disableGunAssemblyCollision()
    # Keep the debug shoot marker (holo 4580) in sync with Debug_Shoot config
    # so toggling it and resetting shows/hides the muzzle origin without re-test.
    if(Setup_Done && holoEntity(4580):isValid()){
        holoEntity(4580):propDraw(Debug_Shoot)
    }
    # Keep the muzzle flash origin (holo 4581) synced to the active mode's
    # per-gun position/angle arrays so tuning them takes effect live.
    syncMuzzleFlash()
    # Keep the dedicated bolt muzzle (holo 4583) synced the same way.
    syncBoltMuzzle()
    if(!MagEnt:isValid() && MagEntIndex > 0) {
        local E = entity(MagEntIndex)
        if(E:isValid() && !isOwnedElsewhere(E)) {
            MagEnt = E
            MagSeated = 1
            if(Max_Ammo <= 0) { Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
            if(Ammo_Count <= 0) {
                Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo
            }
            if(Gun:isValid()) {
                repositionMagOnly(E)
            }
        } elseif(E:isValid()) {
            # Box was claimed by another turret - release our claim
            MagEnt = noentity()
            MagEntIndex = 0
            MagSeated = 0
        }
    }

    if(MagSeated && MagEnt:isValid()) {
        Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")) { stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan") }
        hideAmmoDockMarker()
        # After reset/dupe, MagSeated can be true while Ammo_Count is still 0
        # (first() set it to 0 before recovery). Refill during the grace window only
        # so a genuinely empty mag later still reads empty.
        if(Max_Ammo <= 0) { Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
        if(Ammo_Count <= 0 && curtime() <= JustReset) {
            Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo
        }
        # If the box got claimed by another turret, release without re-parenting it
        if(isOwnedElsewhere(MagEnt)){
            MagSeated = 0
            MagEnt = noentity()
            MagEntIndex = 0
            Ammo_Count = 0
            showAmmoDockMarker()
        } elseif(Gun:isValid()) {
            # If the box is out of place vs config (dupe, physgun, constraint break), snap it back
            if(magNeedsRepos()) { repositionMagOnly(MagEnt) }
        }
    } elseif(MagSeated && !MagEnt:isValid()) {
        Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")) { stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan") }
        hideAmmoDockMarker()
        if(curtime() > JustReset) {
            local Still = (MagEntIndex > 0) ? entity(MagEntIndex) : noentity()
            if(Still:isValid() && isOwnedElsewhere(Still)) {
                MagSeated = 0
                MagEntIndex = 0
                MagEnt = noentity()
                Ammo_Count = 0
                Max_Ammo = 0
                showAmmoDockMarker()
            } elseif(!Still:isValid()) {
                MagSeated = 0
                MagEntIndex = 0
                Ammo_Count = 0
                Max_Ammo = 0
                showAmmoDockMarker()
