                printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[AMMO] Mag gone - dock re-armed.")
            }
        }
    } else {
        if(curtime() > JustReset) {
            if(!Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop) { showAmmoDockMarker() }
            if(Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop && !timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")) {
                timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan", 0.05, function(){ ammoDockScan() })
            }
        } else {
            if(!Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop) { hideAmmoDockMarker() }
        }
    }

    Gun:noCollideAll(1)
    RPM = floor((C / Gun_Delay) * 6000)
    RPM_Speed = RPM
    Low_Ammo = (Ammo_Count <= Low_Ammo_Threshold)

    # Machine gun (40mm) mode: no minigun spool - fire at the set rate instantly
    if(MachineGunMode == 1 && Fire){ C = Gun_Delay }

    if(~Debug_Button & Debug_Button) { debugAutoReload() }

    if(Reload_Button && curtime() > JustReset) {
        if(!Refilling) {
            if(!Reload_Prop:entity():isValid()) {

            } elseif(!isReloadPropInRange()) {
                displayAmmoError("Reload prop too far away! Must be within " + Reload_Distance_Feet + " feet of ammo prop!")   
                holoColor(1440055,vec(255,0,0))
                if(curtime() > Last_Distance_Error_Time + 2000) {
                    Gun:soundPlay(17, 0.8, "@weapons/pistol/pistol_empty.wav")
                    Last_Distance_Error_Time = curtime()
                }
            } elseif(Ammo_Count >= Max_Ammo) {
                if(curtime() > Last_Warning_Time + 2000) {
                    printColor(vec(255,255,0), "[AMMO] Already at maximum ammo capacity!")
                    Last_Warning_Time = curtime()
                }
            } else {
                Refilling = 1
                Auto_Reload_Triggered = 0
                Auto_Reload_Paused = 0
                Gun:soundPlay(3, 0.5, "@weapons/357/357_reload1.wav")
                printColor(vec(255,255,0), "[AMMO] Manual reloading started...")
                timer(TimerPrefix + "reload_tick", Reload_Tick_Delay, function(){ reloadTick() })
                holoAlpha(3001, 0)
                holoAlpha(3002, 0)
                holoAlpha(3003, 0)
            }
        }
    }

    if(Auto_Reload_Enabled && !Refilling && curtime() > JustReset) {
        if(shouldTriggerAutoReload()) { startAutoReload() }
    }

    if(Auto_Reload_Paused && Ammo_Count >= Auto_Reload_Resume_Threshold) {
        Auto_Reload_Paused = 0
        printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[AUTO-RELOAD] Ammo level reached " + Auto_Reload_Resume_Threshold + " - Firing RESUMED")
        holoAlpha(3001, 0)
        holoAlpha(3002, 0)
        holoAlpha(3003, 0)
        Gun:soundPlay(25, 0.7, "@weapons/357/357_reload4.wav")
        logAutoReloadState("Auto-reload resumed firing")
    }





    if(Auto_Reload_Triggered == 1){ holoColor(30001, vec(0,200,250)) }
    if(Auto_Reload_Triggered == 0){ holoColor(30001, vec(0,0,0)) }

    if(Fire){
        if(!MagSeated) {
            displayAmmoError("No magazine! Drop a BoxSRounds on the green circle")
            Active = 0
             stoptimer("Fire_Bolt")  # removed
            stoptimer("Fire_PewPew")
            stoptimer("Fire_Particle")
            stoptimer("Fire_Grenade")
            PewPewChainActive = 0
            ParticleChainActive = 0
            GrenadeChainActive = 0
            stoptimer("Fire_Bolt")
            BoltChainActive = 0
            
            soundStop(5)
            if(MachineGunMode == 0){
                if(C > 0) {
                    C = clamp(C - Spool_down_delay*Spooling_delay, 0, Gun_Delay)
                    SpinAdditive += C*Spooling_delay
                    holoAng(55, holoEntity(10):toWorld(ang(0,0,SpinAdditive)))
                    holoAng(5240008, holoEntity(95000):toWorld(ang(SpinAdditive,94,0)))
                    soundPitch(2, C*Gun_Delay*2)
                    soundLevel(9,0)
                } else { 
                    soundLevel(9,0) 
                }
            }
        } elseif(Ammo_Count <= 0) {
            displayAmmoError("Out of ammo! Reload required!")
            Active = 0
            if(MachineGunMode == 0){
                if(C > 0) {
                    C = clamp(C - Spool_down_delay*Spooling_delay, 0, Gun_Delay)
                    SpinAdditive += C*Spooling_delay 
                    holoAng(55, holoEntity(10):toWorld(ang(0,0,SpinAdditive)))
                    holoAng(5240008, holoEntity(95000):toWorld(ang(SpinAdditive,94,0)))
                    soundPitch(2, C*Gun_Delay*2)
                    soundLevel(9,0)
                    Active = 1
                } else { 
                    soundLevel(9,0)
                    Active = 0 
                }
            }
        } elseif(Overheating_Enabled && Overheated) {
            displayAmmoError("Turret overheated! Wait for cooling!")
            Active = 0
            if(MachineGunMode == 0){
                if(C > 0) {
                    C = clamp(C - Spool_down_delay*Spooling_delay, 0, Gun_Delay)
                    SpinAdditive += C*Spooling_delay
                    holoAng(55, holoEntity(10):toWorld(ang(0,0,SpinAdditive)))
                    holoAng(5240008, holoEntity(95000):toWorld(ang(SpinAdditive,94,0)))
                    soundPitch(2, C*Gun_Delay*2)
                    soundLevel(9,0)
                } else { 
                    soundLevel(9,0)
                }
            }
        } elseif(Auto_Reload_Paused) {
            displayAmmoError("Auto-reload in progress! Waiting for ammo to reach " + Auto_Reload_Resume_Threshold)
            Active = 0
            if(MachineGunMode == 0){
                if(C > 0) {
                    C = clamp(C - Spool_down_delay*Spooling_delay, 0, Gun_Delay)
                    SpinAdditive += C*Spooling_delay
                    holoAng(55, holoEntity(10):toWorld(ang(0,0,SpinAdditive)))
                    holoAng(5240008, holoEntity(95000):toWorld(ang(SpinAdditive,94,0)))
                    soundPitch(2, C*Gun_Delay*2)
                    soundLevel(9,0)
                } else { 
                    soundLevel(9,0) 
                }
            }
        } else {
            if(MachineGunMode == 0){
                if(C==0){
                    entity():soundPlay(2,0,"@acf_extra/vehiclefx/engines/gnomefather/gt101.wav")
                    soundVolume(2,0.54)
                }
                C = clamp(C + Spool_up_delay, 0, Gun_Delay)
                SpinAdditive += C*Spooling_delay
                holoAng(55, holoEntity(10):toWorld(ang(0,0,SpinAdditive)))
                holoAng(5240008, holoEntity(95000):toWorld(ang(SpinAdditive,94,0)))
                soundPitch(2, C*Gun_Delay*2)
            }
            if(RPM >= Min_RPM){
                if(Fire & (Ammo_Count > 0) & !Auto_Reload_Paused){
                    # Xbolt / shootBolt system removed (high ops from ranger + bolt spawn)
                    if(MuzzleModeA[MachineGunMode + 1, number] == 4 & MuzzleFlash_Enabled & !ParticleChainActive){
                        ParticleChainActive = 1
                        timer("Fire_Particle", max(ParticleDelay, 0.01), 0, function(){
                            if(Fire & (Ammo_Count > 0) & !Auto_Reload_Paused & (!Overheating_Enabled | !Overheated) & holoEntity(4581):isValid()){
                                local ParticleName = MuzzleEffNameA[MachineGunMode + 1, string]
                                local ParticleScale = MuzzleScaleA[MachineGunMode + 1, number]
                                local ParticleSpawned = pcfSpawnAutoScale(ParticleName, ParticleScale, holoEntity(4581):pos(), holoEntity(4581):angles(), holoEntity(4581))
                                if(!ParticleSpawned){
                                    # The PCF may not be mounted/ready yet. Stop this
                                    # cycle so the firing loop retries on the next tick.
                                    stoptimer("Fire_Particle")
                                    ParticleChainActive = 0
                                }
                            }else{
                                stoptimer("Fire_Particle")
                                ParticleChainActive = 0
                            }
                        })
                    }
                    if((PewPew==1) & !PewPewChainActive){
                        PewPewChainActive = 1
                        timer("Fire_PewPew", PewPewDelay, 0, function(){
                            if(( PewPew==1) & (Ammo_Count > 0) & !Auto_Reload_Paused & (!Overheating_Enabled | !Overheated)){
                                if(ShakeEnabled == 1){ shake(entity():pos(), ShakeAmp_PewPew, ShakeFreq, ShakeDuration, ShakeRadius) }
                                holoEntity(4580):shootTo(holoEntity(4580):forward(),Accuracy,Force,Bullet_damage,Bullet_per_Shot,Bullet)
                                pulseMuzzleLight()
                                ejectBrass()
                                Ammo_Count = max(0, Ammo_Count - 1)
                                # Per-shot pew sound, gated to a minimum gap so it stays in sync
                                # with bullets but never spams/rapid-stacks beyond the sound delay.
                                if(curtime() >= LastPewSound + PewPewSoundDelay){
                                    Gun:emitSound(Pew_sound, 5000, Shoot_Pitch, Shoot_Volume)
                                    LastPewSound = curtime()
                                }
                            } else { 
                                stoptimer("Fire_PewPew")
                                stoptimer("Fire_Particle")
                                
                                PewPewChainActive = 0 
                                ParticleChainActive = 0
                            }
                        })
                    }
                    
                    
                    # ---- Real bolt chain (mirrors the PewPew timing structure, but uses BoltMode) ----
                    # Use the same repeat-loop pattern as the regular pew-pew fire timer: start once,
                    # then keep firing while conditions remain valid. This avoids the stale one-shot
                    # behavior where a newly acquired target never re-enters the firing cycle.
if((BoltMode==1) & !BoltChainActive){
    BoltChainActive = 1
    timer("Fire_Bolt", BoltDelay, 0, function(){
        if((BoltMode==1) & Fire & (Ammo_Count > 0) & !Auto_Reload_Paused & (!Overheating_Enabled | !Overheated)){
            # Fire from the dedicated bolt muzzle holo 4583. That holo is ALWAYS
            # created and synced every tick to the per-mode BoltMuzzlePosA/
            # BoltMuzzleAngA arrays (index = MachineGunMode + 1), so you tune
            # position + angle per weapon and bolts always come out of the gun.
            local BoltOrigin = holoEntity(4583)
            if(BoltOrigin:isValid()){
                # Spawn facing the muzzle, then configure AntCore's controller.
                # This starts immediately and keeps the real bolt speed aligned
                # with the turret's lead-prediction setting.
                local Bolt = shootBolt(BoltOrigin:pos(), BoltOrigin:angles(), BoltDamage)
                if(!Bolt:isValid()){ return }
                Bolt:boltSetSpeed(BoltSpeed)
                Bolt:boltSetGravity(BoltGravity)
                if(BoltTrailMat != ""){ Bolt:boltSetTrail(BoltTrailMat, BoltTrailCol, BoltTrailWidth, BoltTrailLife) }
                if(BoltModel != ""){ Bolt:boltSetModel(BoltModel) }
                Bolt:boltSetModelScale(BoltModelScale)
                Bolt:boltSetModelAngle(BoltModelAngle)
                Bolt:boltSetGlow(BoltGlow)
            }else{
                printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[BOLT] Bolt holo invalid - cannot fire!")
                stoptimer("Fire_Bolt")
                BoltChainActive = 0
                return
            }

            if(ShakeEnabled == 1){ 
                shake(entity():pos(), ShakeAmp_PewPew, ShakeFreq, ShakeDuration, ShakeRadius) 
            }
            pulseMuzzleLight()
            ejectBrass()
            Ammo_Count = max(0, Ammo_Count - 1)

            if(curtime() >= LastBoltSound + BoltSoundDelay){
                Gun:emitSound(BoltSound, 5000, Shoot_Pitch, Shoot_Volume)
                LastBoltSound = curtime()
            }
        }else{
            stoptimer("Fire_Bolt")
            BoltChainActive = 0
        }
    })
}
                    
            
                    if((GrenadeMode==1) & !GrenadeChainActive){
                        GrenadeChainActive = 1
                        timer("Fire_Grenade", Grenade_Delay, 0, function(){
                            if((GrenadeMode==1) & (Ammo_Count > 0) & !Auto_Reload_Paused & Fire){
                                if(ShakeEnabled == 1){ shake(entity():pos(), ShakeAmp_Grenade, ShakeFreq, ShakeDuration, ShakeRadius) }
                                local GrenadeSource = holoEntity(4580)
                                if(GrenadeSource:isValid()){
                                    local FuseTime = Grenade_Fuse
                                    if(Auto_Grenade_Fuse && Target:isValid() && Target:isAlive() && Grenade_Force > 0){
                                        local TargetPoint = Target:massCenter()
                                        local FlightTime = (TargetPoint - GrenadeSource:pos()):length() / Grenade_Force
                                        FuseTime = clamp(FlightTime + Grenade_LandingExtra, 0.1, 10)
                                    }
                                    local Frag = entity():shootGrenade(GrenadeSource:pos(), GrenadeSource:forward() * Grenade_Force, Grenade_Damage, Grenade_Radius, FuseTime)
                                    if(Frag != noentity()){
                                        Frag:setAngles(GrenadeSource:forward():toAngle() + ang(0, 90, 90))
                                        Frag:setTrails(5, 0, 5, "trails/laser", vec(255, 100, 0), 100)
                                        Frag:setModelScale(0.35)
                                        N++
                                        lightCreate(N, Frag:pos(), vec(255,255,0), 1000, 2)
                                        lightParent(N, Frag)
                                        lightToggle(N, 1)
                                    }
                                }
                                Ammo_Count = max(0, Ammo_Count - 1)
                                # Per-grenade sound, gated to a minimum gap so it stays in sync
                                # with launches but never spams/rapid-stacks beyond the sound delay.
                                if(curtime() >= LastGrenadeSound + GrenadeSoundDelay){
                                    Gun:emitSound(Pew_sound, 5000, Shoot_Pitch, Shoot_Volume)
                                    LastGrenadeSound = curtime()
                                }
                            } else { 
                                stoptimer("Fire_Grenade")
                                GrenadeChainActive = 0 
                            }
                        })
                    }
                }
                
                
                
                
                
                Active = !Auto_Reload_Paused
                if(Overheating_Enabled) {
                    Heat = min(Heat_Max, Heat + Heat_Rate)
                    if(Heat >= Heat_Max && !Overheated) {
                        Overheated = 1
                        Gun:soundPlay(19, 1.0, "@weapons/357/357_reload1.wav")
                        printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[HEAT] CRITICAL OVERHEATING! Turret disabled until cooled!")
                        Gun_RED = 255
                        Gun_GREEN = 0
                    }
                }
                if(Ammo_Count <= 0) {
                    displayAmmoError("Out of ammo! Reload required!")
                    Active = 0
                }
                    if(!Fire | Auto_Reload_Paused | Ammo_Count <= 0 | Overheated) {
                    stoptimer("Fire_PewPew")
                    stoptimer("Fire_Particle")
                    stoptimer("Fire_Grenade")
                    stoptimer("Fire_Bolt")
                    PewPewChainActive = 0
                    ParticleChainActive = 0
                    GrenadeChainActive = 0
                    BoltChainActive = 0
                }
                
                
                try{
                    if(Ammo_Count == Low_Ammo_Threshold) {
                        Gun:emitSound(select(randint(1,2),"@buttons/weapon_cant_buy.wav","buttons/weapon_cant_buy.wav"),80,200,200)
                    }
                    if(Ammo_Count == 50) { Gun:soundPlay(11, 0.9, "@weapons/357/357_reload1.wav") }
                    if(Ammo_Count == 10) { Gun:soundPlay(12, 1.0, "@weapons/clipempty_rifle.wav") }
                    if(Overheating_Enabled && Heat >= Heat_Max * 0.50 && Heat < Heat_Max) {
                        if(curtime() > Last_Warning_Time + 2000) {
                            Gun:soundPlay(18, 0.9, "@weapons/pistol/pistol_empty.wav")
                            printColor(vec(255,165,0), "[HEAT] WARNING! Turret running hot: " + floor(Heat) + "/" + Heat_Max + "F")
                            Gun_RED = 255
                            Gun_GREEN = 0
                            Last_Warning_Time = curtime()
                        }
                    }
                }catch(Err:string){ 
                    print(Err)
                    assert(0,Err) 
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        stoptimer("Fire_PewPew")
        stoptimer("Fire_Particle")
        stoptimer("Fire_Grenade")
        stoptimer("Fire_Bolt")
        PewPewChainActive = 0
        ParticleChainActive = 0
        GrenadeChainActive = 0
        soundStop(5)
        if(MachineGunMode == 0){
            C = clamp(C - Spool_down_delay, 0, Gun_Delay)
            SpinAdditive += C*Spooling_delay
            holoAng(55, holoEntity(10):toWorld(ang(0,0,SpinAdditive)))
            holoAng(5240008, holoEntity(95000):toWorld(ang(SpinAdditive,94,0)))
            soundPitch(2, C*Gun_Delay*2)
        }
        lightToggle(LightBase + 50,Fire)
        if(MachineGunMode == 0){
            local CurrentClickRPM = (C > 0) ? floor((C / Gun_Delay) * 6000) : 0
            if(C > 0 && CurrentClickRPM < Click_RPM_Max){
                local ClickInterval = clamp(1 / (C * 3), 0.05, 1.0)
                if(curtime() > LastClickTime + ClickInterval){
                    Gun:emitSound(Click_sound, Click_SoundLevel, Click_Pitch, Click_Volume)
                    LastClickTime = curtime()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if(!Fire){ Active = 0 }

    if(Overheating_Enabled) {
        if(!Fire || Overheated) {
            local CoolingPerTick = Cooling_Rate / 50
            Heat = Heat - CoolingPerTick
            local TargetHeat = Heat_Min
            if(Overheated && Target:isValid() && Heat <= Heat_Combat) { TargetHeat = Heat_Combat }
            if(Heat < TargetHeat) { Heat = TargetHeat }
            if(Overheated && Heat <= Heat_Combat) {
                Overheated = 0
                if(Target) {
                    Gun:soundPlay(20, 0.7, "@weapons/357/357_reload4.wav")
                    printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[HEAT] Combat-ready! Gun cooled to " + floor(Heat) + "/" + Heat_Max + " (Targets present)")
                } else {
                    Gun:soundPlay(20, 0.7, "@weapons/357/357_reload4.wav")
                    printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[HEAT] Fully cooled and ready! " + floor(Heat) + "/" + Heat_Max)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if(Turret_online == 0) { 
        Gun_RED = 0
        Gun_GREEN = 0 
    } elseif(Overheating_Enabled && Overheated) { 
        Gun_RED = 255
        Gun_GREEN = 0 
    } elseif(Overheating_Enabled && Heat >= Heat_Max * 0.75) { 
        Gun_RED = 255
        Gun_GREEN = 128 
    } elseif(Overheating_Enabled && Target:isValid() && Heat > Heat_Min && Heat <= Heat_Combat) { 
        Gun_RED = 255
        Gun_GREEN = 255 
    } else { 
        Gun_RED = 0
        Gun_GREEN = 255 
    }

    if(Fire==0){ soundLevel(9,0) }

    if(changed(Target)){
        stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "Tick")
        if(Flash_Mode == 1){
            timer(TimerPrefix + "Tick", 0.5, 0, function(){ 
                Flash += 1
                if(Flash > 1){ Flash = 0 } 
            })
        } else {
            if(Target){ 
                timer(TimerPrefix + "Tick", 0.5, 0, function(){ 
                    Flash += 1
                    if(Flash > 1){ Flash = 0 } 
                }) 
            } else { 
                stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "Tick")
                Flash = 1 
            }
        }
        if(Laser_Color_Mode == 1){ Col = Target ? Col_Target : Col_NoTarget } else { Col = Col_Default }
        beamColor(Col)
    }

    if(Target:isValid()){
        Pos = holoEntity(524000):toWorld(vec(0,0,0.5))
        spriteSpawn(LightBase + 10,"sprites/flare1.vmt")
        spriteSetPos(LightBase + 10,Pos)
        spriteSetScale(LightBase + 10,0.02*Flash)
        spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 10,9)
        spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 10,255)
        spriteSetColor(LightBase + 10,vec(0,255,0))
        spriteEnable(LightBase + 10,Flash!=0)
        spriteSetParent(LightBase + 10,holoEntity(524000))
    } 

    if(!Target:isValid()){
        Pos = holoEntity(524000):toWorld(vec(0,0,0.5))
        spriteSpawn(LightBase + 10,"sprites/flare1.vmt")
        spriteSetPos(LightBase + 10,Pos)
        spriteSetScale(LightBase + 10,0.02*Flash)
        spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 10,9)
        spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 10,255)
        spriteSetColor(LightBase + 10,vec(255,0,0))
        spriteEnable(LightBase + 10,Flash!=0)
        spriteSetParent(LightBase + 10,holoEntity(524000))
    } 

    if(Turret_online==0){ 
        spriteSetColor(LightBase + 10,vec(0,0,0))
        spriteSetScale(LightBase + 10,0)
        spriteEnable(LightBase + 10,0) 
    }

    if(!Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) { Ammo_Prop:entity():setColor(vec(255, 0, 255)) }
    elseif(Ammo_Count == 0) { Ammo_Prop:entity():setColor(vec(255, 0, 0)) }
    elseif(Low_Ammo) { Ammo_Prop:entity():setColor(vec(255, 255, 0)) }
    elseif(Overheating_Enabled && Overheated) { holoColor(1440055,vec(255,165,0)) }
    elseif(Auto_Reload_Paused) {
        local BlinkRate = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(curtime() * 10)
        holoColor(1440055, vec(BlinkRate * 255, 0, (1-BlinkRate) * 255))
    } else { holoColor(1440055,vec(0,255,0)) }

    if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) { Ammo_Prop:entity():setColor(vec(255)) }

    Ammo_Count = max(0, Ammo_Count)
    Low_Ammo = (Ammo_Count <= Low_Ammo_Threshold)
    IsReloading = Refilling

    if(changed(Ammo_Count)) {
        local Status = ""
        if(!MagSeated) { Status = " (NO PROP)" }
        elseif(Ammo_Count <= 0) { Status = " (EMPTY)" }
        elseif(Low_Ammo) { Status = " (LOW)" }
        else { Status = " (OK)" }
        if(Print_ammo_on_hud == 1){    
            printColor(vec(100,255,100), "Ammo: " + Ammo_Count + "/" + Max_Ammo + Status + 
                       (Refilling ? " (RELOADING)" : "") +
                       (Auto_Reload_Paused ? " (AUTO-RELOAD PAUSED - Need " + (Auto_Reload_Resume_Threshold - Ammo_Count) + " more to resume)" : ""))
        }
    }

    if(!first() && changed(Ammo_Count <= 0)){
        if(Ammo_Count <= 0 && MagSeated){
            printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[AMMO] DEPLETED - 0/" + Max_Ammo + " - Reload required!")
            hint("Ammo depleted! Reload required.", 5)
            entity():emitSound("@hl1/fvox/ammo_depleted.wav", 80, 80, 0.8)
            Gun:soundPlay(10, 0.8, "@weapons/pistol/pistol_empty.wav")
            if(!Auto_Reload_Enabled && Ammo_Eject_On_Empty && getAmmoEntity():isValid() && !Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop){
                ejectAmmoBox("empty")
            }elseif(!Auto_Reload_Enabled && Ammo_Eject_On_Empty){
                showAmmoDockMarker()
            }
        }
    }

    if(changed(Overheated)) {
        if(Overheated) {
            printColor(vec(255,0,0), "[HEAT] CRITICAL OVERHEATING! Turret disabled until cooled!")
            Gun_RED = 255
            Gun_GREEN = 0
            entity():emitSound("@hl1/fvox/heat_damage.wav", 80, 80, 0.8)
        } else {
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[HEAT] Turret cooled down and ready to fire!")
            entity():emitSound("@hl1/fvox/inoperable.wav", 80, 80, 0.8)
            Gun_RED = 0
            Gun_GREEN = 255
        }
    }

    if (changed(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) & !Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        if(!MagEnt:isValid()) { showAmmoDockMarker() }
    }

    if(changed(C)&C){ soundStop("C") }
    if(changed(!C)&!C){ 
        Gun:soundPlay("C", 0, "@buttons/weapon_confirm.wav")
        soundVolume("C",1) 
    }

    if(changed(Target)){
        if(Laser_Color_Mode == 1){ Col = Target ? Col_Target : Col_NoTarget } else { Col = Col_Default }
        beamColor(Col)
    }

    # Ranger_Visual / bolt pullback ranger REMOVED (was high ops). Xbolt system disabled.

    # ---- Laser / beam / can-sprite ----
    # Uses TurretActive_Real so remote toggle works without wiring Turret_online.
    # Beam_On = 1  full beam + impact sprite + can sprite
    # Beam_On = 0  no beam, only the can/near-light sprite (red/green by target)
    if(Laser_Color_Mode == 1){
        Col = (Target:isValid() && HasValidTarget) ? Col_Target : Col_NoTarget
    }else{
        Col = Col_Default
    }

    if(TurretActive_Real){
        if(Beam_On){
            # Full laser beam mode
   rangerFilter(array(owner(), holoEntity(99999285), holoEntity(999992851)))
        
            local Holo = holoEntity(54)
            if(Holo:isValid()){
                local LaserDistance = Dist_laser ? Dist_laser : 10
                local Ranger = rangerOffset(LaserDistance, Holo:pos(), Holo:forward())

                local TargetPos = vec()
                if(Ranger:entity() == owner()){
                    TargetPos = Holo:pos() + Holo:forward() * LaserDistance
                }elseif(Ranger:distance() < 10){
                    TargetPos = Holo:pos() + Holo:forward() * 10
                }else{
                    TargetPos = Holo:pos() + Holo:forward() * Ranger:distance()
                }

                        if(holoEntity(2099):isValid()){
                    holoPos(2099, TargetPos)
                }


                beamVisible(owner(), 1)
                beamPoint(1, holoEntity(54))
                beamPoint(2, holoEntity(2099))
                beamColor(Col)

                lightCreate(LightBase + 10, holoEntity(2099):pos(), Col, Light_brightness_Dot, Light_dis_Dot)
                lightParent(LightBase + 10, holoEntity(2099))
                lightToggle(LightBase + 10, 1)

                # Impact / far sprite
                Pos = holoEntity(2099):toWorld(vec(0,0,0))
                spriteSpawn(LightBase + 100, "sprites/flare1.vmt")
                spriteSetPos(LightBase + 100, Pos)
                spriteSetScale(LightBase + 100, Dot_scale)
                spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 100, 9)
                spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 100, 255)
                spriteSetColor(LightBase + 100, Col)
                spriteEnable(LightBase + 100, 1)
                spriteSetParent(LightBase + 100, holoEntity(2099))

                # Can / near-light sprite
                Pos = holoEntity(54):toWorld(vec(0,0,0))
                spriteSpawn(LightBase + 101, "sprites/flare1.vmt")
                spriteSetPos(LightBase + 101, Pos)
                spriteSetScale(LightBase + 101, Dot_scale_Cam)
                spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 101, 9)
                spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 101, 255)
                spriteSetColor(LightBase + 101, Col)
                spriteEnable(LightBase + 101, 1)
                spriteSetParent(LightBase + 101, holoEntity(54))
            }
        }else{
            # Beam off: only the can/near-light sprite, colored by target state
            beamVisible(owner(), 0)
            lightToggle(LightBase + 10, 0)

            # Hide impact sprite
            Pos = holoEntity(2099):isValid() ? holoEntity(2099):toWorld(vec(0,0,0)) : vec()
            spriteSpawn(LightBase + 100, "sprites/flare1.vmt")
            spriteSetPos(LightBase + 100, Pos)
            spriteSetScale(LightBase + 100, 0)
            spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 100, 9)
            spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 100, 0)
            spriteSetColor(LightBase + 100, Col)
            spriteEnable(LightBase + 100, 0)
            if(holoEntity(2099):isValid()){ spriteSetParent(LightBase + 100, holoEntity(2099)) }

            # Show only can sprite (red = no target, green = has target when Laser_Color_Mode)
            if(holoEntity(54):isValid()){
                Pos = holoEntity(54):toWorld(vec(0,0,0))
                spriteSpawn(LightBase + 101, "sprites/flare1.vmt")
                spriteSetPos(LightBase + 101, Pos)
                spriteSetScale(LightBase + 101, Dot_scale_Cam)
                spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 101, 9)
                spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 101, 255)
                spriteSetColor(LightBase + 101, Col)
                spriteEnable(LightBase + 101, 1)
                spriteSetParent(LightBase + 101, holoEntity(54))
            }
        }
    }else{
        # Turret offline: hide beam + both sprites
        beamVisible(owner(), 0)
        lightToggle(LightBase + 10, 0)

        Pos = holoEntity(2099):isValid() ? holoEntity(2099):toWorld(vec(0,0,0)) : vec()
        spriteSpawn(LightBase + 100, "sprites/flare1.vmt")
        spriteSetPos(LightBase + 100, Pos)
        spriteSetScale(LightBase + 100, 0)
        spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 100, 9)
        spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 100, 0)
        spriteSetColor(LightBase + 100, Col)
        spriteEnable(LightBase + 100, 0)
        if(holoEntity(2099):isValid()){ spriteSetParent(LightBase + 100, holoEntity(2099)) }

        Pos = holoEntity(54):isValid() ? holoEntity(54):toWorld(vec(0,0,0)) : vec()
        spriteSpawn(LightBase + 101, "sprites/flare1.vmt")
        spriteSetPos(LightBase + 101, Pos)
        spriteSetScale(LightBase + 101, 0)
        spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 101, 9)
        spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 101, 0)
        spriteSetColor(LightBase + 101, Col)
        spriteEnable(LightBase + 101, 0)
        if(holoEntity(54):isValid()){ spriteSetParent(LightBase + 101, holoEntity(54)) }
    }

    # --- Lamp: mount, seat & recover (mirrors the ammo-prop system) ---
    # When the Lamp input arrives/changes, seat it to the mount (holo 42).
    if(~Lamp & Lamp:isValid()) { seatLampProp(Lamp) }
    # Keep retrying until the lamp is seated (covers late wire links / resets).
    if(!LampParented & (Lamp:isValid() | LampIndex > 0)) { recoverLamp() }
    # Auto-wire the lamp (gmod_wire_lamp) by pushing its lighting inputs through
    # its wirelink. Uses the configurable FOV_lamp / Brightness_lamp / Col_lamp /
    # Distance_lamp / Texture_lamp from first().
    if(LampEnt != noentity() & LampEnt:isValid() & LampWirelink){
        LampWirelink["On",number] = TurretActive_Real
        LampWirelink["RGB",vector] = Col_lamp
        LampWirelink["Red",number] = Col_lamp:x()
        LampWirelink["Green",number] = Col_lamp:y()
        LampWirelink["Blue",number] = Col_lamp:z()
        LampWirelink["Brightness",number] = Brightness_lamp
        LampWirelink["Distance",number] = Distance_lamp
        LampWirelink["FOV",number] = FOV_lamp
        if(Texture_lamp != ""){ LampWirelink["Texture",string] = Texture_lamp }
    }
    # ---- Lamp visibility: when no lamp prop is seated (LampParented == 0) the
    # lamp holograms (mount 42, holder 44, light 43) are completely hidden.
    # When a lamp is seated they are shown. (Holo 1050 is NOT gated here - it
    # is the ammo-box mount and stays visible regardless of the lamp.)
    if(LampParented){
        if(holoEntity(42):isValid()){
            holoEntity(42):propDraw(1)
            holoAlpha(42,255)
        }
        if(holoEntity(44):isValid()){
            holoEntity(44):propDraw(1)
            holoAlpha(44,255)
        }
        if(holoEntity(43):isValid()){
            holoEntity(43):propDraw(1)
            holoAlpha(43,255)
        }
    }else{
        if(holoEntity(42):isValid()){
            holoEntity(42):propDraw(0)
            holoAlpha(42,0)
        }
        if(holoEntity(44):isValid()){
            holoEntity(44):propDraw(0)
            holoAlpha(44,0)
        }
        if(holoEntity(43):isValid()){
            holoEntity(43):propDraw(0)
            holoAlpha(43,0)
        }
    }
    # Glow sprite only shows when a lamp is seated (light being used) AND the
    # turret is active.
    if(holoEntity(43):isValid() & holoEntity(42):isValid()){
        if(LampParented & TurretActive_Real){
            holoColor(43, Col_lamp)
            spriteSpawn(LightBase + 45, LampSpriteMat)
            spriteSetPos(LightBase + 45, holoEntity(43):toWorld(LampSpritePos))
            spriteSetScale(LightBase + 45, LampSpriteScale)
            spriteSetRendermode(LightBase + 45, LampSpriteRM)
            spriteSetAlpha(LightBase + 45, LampSpriteAlpha)
            spriteSetColor(LightBase + 45, LampSpriteCol)
            spriteEnable(LightBase + 45, 1)
            spriteSetParent(LightBase + 45, holoEntity(43))
        }else{
            spriteEnable(LightBase + 45, 0)
        }
    }

    if(changed(Ammo_Prop:entity()) & (Ammo_Prop:entity() == noentity())) {
        if(!MagEnt:isValid()) { showAmmoDockMarker() }
    }

    if(changed(Ammo_Prop:entity()) & (Ammo_Prop:entity() != noentity())) {
        seatAmmoProp(Ammo_Prop:entity())
        Ammo_EjectedEnt = noentity()
    }



    if(~Gun & Gun:isValid()) {
        if(MagSeated && MagEnt:isValid()) {
            local RLink = magRefHolo()
            if(!RLink:isValid()){ RLink = Gun }
            MagEnt:propFreeze(1)
            MagEnt:deparent()
            MagEnt:setPos(RLink:toWorld(MaG_PoS))
            local AmmoIndex = clamp(AmmoProfile, 1, max(AmmoModels:count(), 1))
            local ProfileAng = AmmoProfileAngles:count() >= AmmoIndex ? AmmoProfileAngles[AmmoIndex, angle] : ang()
            MagEnt:setAng(RLink:toWorld(Mag_Ang + ProfileAng))
            timer(TimerPrefix + "relink_mag_gun", 0.05, function() {
                if(MagEnt:isValid() && Gun:isValid() && !isOwnedElsewhere(MagEnt)) {
                    MagEnt:parentTo(Gun)
                    printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[AMMO] Mag re-linked to Gun")
                }
            })
        }
        if(holoEntity(10):isValid()) { holoParent(10, Gun) }
        if(holoEntity(20):isValid()) { holoParent(20, Gun) }
        if(holoEntity(16):isValid()) { holoParent(16, Gun) }

        # Gun's wired value just changed (arrived, or moved) - if we've been
        # waiting on the box, this is exactly the moment a delayed wire link
        # would finally let recovery succeed. Give it another shot right away
        # instead of waiting for the next scheduled retry.
        if(!MagSeated){
            if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()){
                seatAmmoProp(Ammo_Prop:entity())
            }else{
    recoverAmmoBox()
    recoverLamp()
            }
        }
    }



    if(changed(Reload_Prop:entity())&&(Reload_Prop:entity() != noentity())) {
        Reload_Prop:entity():setPos(holoEntity(30000):pos())
        Reload_Prop:entity():setAng(holoEntity(30000):angles())
        Reload_Prop:entity():propNotSolid(0)
        Reload_Prop:entity():propFreeze(1)
        timer(TimerPrefix + "Reparent_reloading", 0.1, function(){
            Reload_Prop:entity():parentTo(holoEntity(30000))
            Reload_Prop:entity():propFreeze(0)
            Reload_Prop:entity():setModelScale(vec(0.45))
            Reload_Prop:entity():setPhysScale(0.36)
            hint("reloading prop parented",3)
            entity():soundPlay(0,1,"buttons/bell1.wav")
        })
    }

}

# When Gun is newly wired: ALWAYS full-chip reset so first() rebuilds ALL gun
# holos (20/40/accessories/lamp mounts/ejectors/etc.) while Gun is valid, then
# the boot seat timers parent gun + ammo box + battery. User should never need
# a manual reset - wiring the Gun input is the trigger.
function void seatGunAssembly(Reason:string){
    if(!Gun:isValid()){ return }

    # Cooldown stops reset loops (watcher / input edge / post-reset first()).
    if(curtime() < LastGunResetTime + 2.5){
        # Soft re-seat only (same gun re-fired or reset just happened)
        MaG_PoS = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagPos1 : MagPos0
        Mag_Ang = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagAng1 : MagAng0
        parentGun()
        if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()){
            local AE = Ammo_Prop:entity()
            if(!MagSeated){ seatAmmoProp(AE) }
            else{ repositionMagOnly(AE) }
        }elseif(MagEnt:isValid()){
            repositionMagOnly(MagEnt)
        }else{
            recoverAmmoBox()
            if(MagEnt:isValid()){ repositionMagOnly(MagEnt) }
        }
        parentBattery()
        if(MachineGunMode == 1){
            if(holoEntity(40):isValid()){ holoEntity(40):propDraw(1) holoAlpha(40, 255) }
            if(holoEntity(20):isValid()){ holoEntity(20):propDraw(0) }
        }else{
            if(holoEntity(20):isValid()){ holoEntity(20):propDraw(1) holoAlpha(20, 255) }
        }
        if(holoEntity(20):isValid() && holoEntity(20):parent() != Gun){
            holoParent(20, Gun)
        }
        printColor(vec(0,255,200), "[GUN] " + Reason + " - soft seat (cooldown)")
        return
    }

    LastGunResetTime = curtime()
    printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[GUN] " + Reason + " - full rebuild so gun holos enable + ammo/battery seat")
    # reset() restarts the chip. first() rebuilds holos with Gun already wired,
    # then gun_boot_seat / setup timers parent gun, mag, and battery.
    reset()
}

function void requestGunRebuild(Reason:string){
    seatGunAssembly(Reason)
}

function void gunLinkWatcher(){
    local GID = Gun:isValid() ? Gun:id() : -1
    if(GID == GunMarkedID){ return }
    GunMarkedID = GID
    if(GID <= 0){ return }
    if(!Setup_Done){
        # Chip still booting - wait until setup finishes, then rebuild once
        if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun_rebuild_pending")){
            timer(TimerPrefix + "gun_rebuild_pending", 0.1, 0, function(){
                if(Setup_Done && Gun:isValid() && Gun:id() == GunMarkedID){
                    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "gun_rebuild_pending")
                    seatGunAssembly("Gun was wired during boot")
                }elseif(Setup_Done){
                    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "gun_rebuild_pending")
                }
            })
        }
        return
    }
    seatGunAssembly("Watcher detected new gun ID " + GID)
}

function void batteryLinkWatcher(){
    if(!Gun:isValid()){ return }
    recoverBattery()
    local BID = BatteryEnt:isValid() ? BatteryEnt:id() : -1
    # Reseat if the mount is wrong even when the entity ID did not change
    # (common after chip reset: same battery, no longer on holo 8888).
    if(BID == BatteryMarkedID){
        if(BID > 0 && batteryNeedsRepos()){
            parentBattery()
        }
        return
    }
    BatteryMarkedID = BID
    if(BID > 0){
        printColor(vec(0,200,255), "[BATTERY] Watcher detected new battery ID: " + BID)
        parentBattery()
    }
}


# Remote screen / wireless toggle: read our slot's command from the shared
# hive gTable (written by the portable EGP control screen) and apply it
# through applyTurretState() so the SAME power checks still gate turning ON.
# Edge-triggered by the screen's sequence counter, so pressing the same
# button again (even when power was blocked) re-applies the intent.
function void hiveHandleCommand(){
    if(HiveName == ""){ return }
    if(HiveSlot <= 0 || HiveSlot > HiveSlots){ return }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    if(hiveGetSig(HiveSlot) != 0 && hiveGetSig(HiveSlot) != UniqueID){ return }
    local Seq = GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_cmdseq", number]
    if(Seq == LastHiveCmdSeq){ return }
    LastHiveCmdSeq = Seq
    local Cmd = GT["s" + HiveSlot + "_cmd", number]
    if(Cmd == 0 || Cmd == 1){
        # Only write the shared remote state when the EGP remote is the active
        # control source (weapon sender has priority when it is enabled)
        if(!WeaponSender_Enabled && RemoteToggleEnabled){ RemoteState = Cmd }
        applyTurretState(Cmd)
    }
}

# POWER button / per-turret button helpers. Commands are written into each
# slot's shared hive gTable; the owning turret's hive poll applies its own
# slot's command ~0.1s later.
function void egpSendPowerCommand(Slot:number, State:number){
    if(HiveName == ""){ return }
    if(Slot <= 0 || Slot > HiveSlots){ return }
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    GT["s" + Slot + "_cmd", number] = State
    GT["s" + Slot + "_cmdseq", number] = GT["s" + Slot + "_cmdseq", number] + 1
}

# Per-turret button: toggles one specific turret (by its roster slot) on/off.
function void egpTouchToggleSlot(Slot:number){
    local GT = gTable(HiveName)
    local CurOn = GT["s" + Slot + "_on", number]
    egpSendPowerCommand(Slot, CurOn ? 0 : 1)
}

# MASTER POWER button: the original single switch. In single-switch mode
# (MasterPower_Power = 1) it toggles the WHOLE live squad at once by sending
# the same on/off command to every turret slot. It is hidden in multi-switch
# mode (MasterPower_Mode = 0) where per-turret buttons are used instead.
function void egpTouchToggleMaster(){
    if(RemoteToggleEnabled != 1){ return }
    local NewState = EGP_PowerOn ? 0 : 1
    EGP_PowerOn = NewState
    if(!WeaponSender_Enabled && RemoteToggleEnabled){ RemoteState = NewState }
    # Squad-wide: command every live turret slot to the same state at once.
    if(HiveName != ""){
        local GT = gTable(HiveName)
        for(I = 1, HiveSlots){
            local Sig = GT["s" + I + "_sig", number]
            if(Sig != 0 && curtime() <= GT["s" + I + "_hb", number] + 2){
                egpSendPowerCommand(I, NewState)
            }
        }
    }
    applyTurretState(NewState)
}

# playerUse handling for the HUD screen's POWER button + per-turret row buttons.
function void egpTouchHandlePress(Ply:entity){
    if(!Ply:isValid() || !EGP_ScreenCreated || !EGP:isValid()){ return }
    if(curtime() < EGP_IgnorePressUntil){ return }
    if(curtime() - EGP_LastPressTime < 0.18){ return }
    EGP_LastPressTime = curtime()
    local Cursor = EGP_Screen:egpCursor(Ply)
    if(Cursor:x() < -10){ return }
    EGP_LastUser = Ply
    # Per-turret row buttons (only shown/usable in multi-switch mode, for live turrets)
    if(MasterPower_Mode == 0){
        for(I = 1, min(HiveSlots, 8)){
            local Obj = EGP_SlotPwr[I, number]
            if(EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(Obj) && EGP_Screen:egpObjectContainsPoint(Obj, Cursor)){
                EGP_IgnorePressUntil = curtime() + 0.35
                local Slot = EGP_RowSlot[I, number]
                if(Slot > 0 && hiveGetSig(Slot) != 0 && curtime() <= hiveGetHeartbeat(Slot) + 5){
                    egpTouchToggleSlot(Slot)
                }
                return
            }
        }
    }
    # Master POWER button (only in single-switch mode, MasterPower_Mode = 1)
    if(MasterPower_Mode == 1 && EGP_Screen:egpHasObject(EGP_PowerBtnIndex)){
        if(EGP_PowerBtn:containsPoint(Cursor)){
            EGP_IgnorePressUntil = curtime() + 0.35
            egpTouchToggleMaster()
        }
    }
}

function void releaseTarget(Killed:number){
    local Old = Target
    fireStop()
    Target = noentity()
    HasValidTarget = 0
    # Free our hive claim immediately so another turret can take this target
    hiveBroadcast()
    resetTracking()
    # Resume side-to-side from current aim  one-shot nearer-side pick on next patrol tick
    Sweep_Pausing = 0
    Sweep_PauseUntil = 0
    Sweep_Reenter = 1
   if(Old:isValid()){ dropFromQueue(Old) }

    # Detect actual kill regardless of caller  dead/removed targets always use retarget delay
    if(!Old:isValid() || !Old:isAlive() || Old:health() <= 0){
        Killed = 1
    }

    if(Killed){
        Kill_Count++
    }

    local Wait = Killed ? Retarget_Delay : Target_Acquisition_Delay
    if(Wait > 0){
        Retargeting = 1
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "retarget")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "retarget") }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "retarget", Wait, function(){
            Retargeting = 0
            acquireTarget()
        })
    }else{
        Retargeting = 0
        acquireTarget()
    }
}

# Dedicated halo refresh - re-applies to the current target on its own interval so
# it reliably follows when the turret retargets (independent of scanTick early-returns).
function void haloTick(){
    if(HaloEnabled == 1){
        if(Target:isValid() && Target:isAlive()){
            if(HaloTarget != Target){
                if(HaloTarget != noentity()){ HaloTarget:setHaloEnabled(0) }
                HaloTarget = Target
            }
            applyNPCHalo(Target, (Target:pos() - entity():pos()):length())
        }else{
            if(HaloTarget != noentity()){
                HaloTarget:setHaloEnabled(0)
                HaloTarget = noentity()
            }
        }
    }
}

function void scanTick(){
    if(!Setup_Done){ return }
    if(!TurretActive_Real || !Power_Available){
        if(TargetQueueSize > 0){
            TargetQueue = array()
            TargetQueueScore = array()
            TargetQueueSize = 0
            TargetCount = 0
        }
        return
    }

    # No ammo to fire: don't scan/search for targets at all, just go idle + warn.
    # (Load a magazine and it resumes on its own.)
    if(!hasAmmoToFire()){
        if(TargetQueueSize > 0){
            TargetQueue = array()
            TargetQueueScore = array()
            TargetQueueSize = 0
            TargetCount = 0
        }
        if(Target:isValid() || HasValidTarget || Fire || Fire_Pending){
            fireStop()
            Target = noentity()
            HasValidTarget = 0
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "retarget")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "retarget") }
            Retargeting = 0
        }
        if(curtime() - Last_Warning_Time >= 3){
            Last_Warning_Time = curtime()
            printColor(vec(255,150,0), "[AMMO] No ammo - scanning paused. Load a magazine to engage targets.")
            if(BaseEnt:isValid()){ BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/button10.wav", 70, 100, 0.6) }
        }
        return
    }

    buildTargetQueue()

    if(!Target:isValid() && !Retargeting){
        # nothing engaged: take the nearest queued candidate right away
        acquireTarget()
        return
    }

    # If a closer / higher priority threat shows up, switch to it right away.
    if(Target:isValid() && TargetQueueSize > 0){
        local Best = TargetQueue[1, entity]
        if(Best != Target && Best:isValid()){
            local CurrentScore = targetScore(Target)
            if(TargetQueueScore[1, number] < CurrentScore - 100 && hasLineOfSight(Best)){
                if(DebugMode == 1){
                    printColor(vec(255,200,0), "SWITCHING to closer threat: " + shortenTargetName(Best))
                }
                fireStop()
                Target = Best
                HasValidTarget = 1
                resetTracking()
                dropFromQueue(Best)
            }
        }
    }
}

function void applyAim(){
    if(!holoEntity(8600):isValid() || !BaseEnt:isValid() || !holoEntity(8500):isValid()){ return }

    # rate-independent smoothing  mode 0 uses Sensitivity_aim / Sensitivity_sweep
    # mode 1 uses Sensitivity_aim_Side / Sensitivity_sweep_Side for track blend
    local AimAlpha   = clamp(Sensitivity_aim   * (Aim_Rate / 0.02), 0.01, 1)
    local SweepAlpha = clamp(Sensitivity_sweep * (Aim_Rate / 0.02), 0.005, 1)
    # SweepAlphaSide reserved for future mode-1 ease
    local SweepAlphaSide = clamp(Sensitivity_sweep_Side * (Aim_Rate / 0.02), 0.005, 1)
    if(0){ SweepAlphaSide = SweepAlphaSide }

    if(TurretActive_Real && Target:isValid() && TargetLocation:length() > 0){
        # Muzzle from elevation holo (BaseEnt parent chain  correct when inverted)
        local MuzzlePos = holoEntity(8600):pos()
        if(Gun:isValid()){ MuzzlePos = Gun:pos() }
        local WorldAimDir = (TargetLocation - MuzzlePos)
        if(WorldAimDir:length() < 1){ WorldAimDir = BaseEnt:forward() }
        WorldAimDir = WorldAimDir:normalized()

        # Base-local direction: +X forward, +Y right, +Z up (into room when inverted)
        local LD = BaseEnt:toLocalAxis(WorldAimDir)
        if(LD:length() < 0.001){ LD = vec(1, 0, 0) }
        LD = LD:normalized()

        # atan2 returns radians  convert to degrees for ang()
        local Yaw = atan2(LD:y(), LD:x()) * 180 / _PI
        local Horiz = sqrt(LD:x() * LD:x() + LD:y() * LD:y())
        local Pitch = atan2(-LD:z(), max(Horiz, 0.0001)) * 180 / _PI

        local TempDesiredAngle = BaseEnt:toWorld(ang(Pitch, Yaw, 0))


        
                # Same orientation-independent cone as isInTurretRange
        local YawDiff = Yaw
        while(YawDiff > 180){ YawDiff -= 360 }
        while(YawDiff < -180){ YawDiff += 360 }
        local PitchDiff = Pitch
        while(PitchDiff > 180){ PitchDiff -= 360 }
        while(PitchDiff < -180){ PitchDiff += 360 }
        local InRange = (abs(YawDiff) <= turretLRRange()) && (PitchDiff >= -UP_FOV) && (PitchDiff <= DOWN_FOV)
        #local InRange = (abs(YawDiff) <= 90) && (abs(PitchDiff) <= 85)
        

          if(InRange){
            DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(TempDesiredAngle), 0.85):toAngle()
            Sweep_Pausing = 0
        }else{
            if(DebugMode == 1){
                printColor(vec(255,150,0), "Target outside rotation range - releasing")
            }
            releaseTarget(0)
        }
    }

    if(!Target:isValid()){
        if(!TurretActive_Real){
            if(AimDown && curtime() >= AimDownTimer){
                local IsUpsideDown = BaseEnt:up():z() < 0
                local PitchOffset = IsUpsideDown ? -Turret_off_ang : Turret_off_ang
                local DownAngle = BaseEnt:angles() + ang(PitchOffset, 0, 0)
                DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(DownAngle), SweepAlpha):toAngle()
            }else{
                DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(BaseEnt:angles()), SweepAlpha):toAngle()
            }
        }else{
            if(ReturnToCenter){
                DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(BaseEnt:angles()), SweepAlpha):toAngle()
                if(curtime() >= ReturnToCenterTimer){
                    ReturnToCenter = 0
                }
            }elseif(!HasValidTarget){
                if(Hold_Between_Kills && Retargeting && TargetQueueSize > 0){
                    # hold position while waiting for next queued target
                }elseif(curtime() < SweepDelayTimer){
                    DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(BaseEnt:angles()), SweepAlpha):toAngle()
                }elseif(Sweep_speed == 0 || TargetQueueSize > 0){
                    # Sweep disabled (Sweep_speed == 0) or targets waiting  hold center
                    DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(BaseEnt:angles()), SweepAlpha):toAngle()
                }elseif(Sweep_Mode == 0){
                    # MODE 0  classic continuous sine sweep (original)
                    local SweepRange = (LEFT_RIGHT_FOV - Sweep_Margin) / 2
                    local SweepTargetAngle = BaseEnt:angles() + ang(0, SweepRange * sin(Sweep_speed * curtime() % 360), 0)
                    DesiredAngle = slerp(quat(DesiredAngle), quat(SweepTargetAngle), SweepAlpha):toAngle()
                }else{
                    # MODE 1  left/right patrol with end pause (constant yaw rate)
                    local SweepRange = (LEFT_RIGHT_FOV - Sweep_Margin) / 2
                    local BaseYaw = BaseEnt:angles():yaw()
                    local BasePitch = BaseEnt:angles():pitch()
                    local BaseRoll = BaseEnt:angles():roll()
                    local CurYaw = DesiredAngle:yaw()

                    # One-shot after leaving a target: aim toward the nearer side so we
                    # ease back into patrol instead of crossing the whole sweep envelope. Must NOT
                    # run every tick or it flips back to the side you're already on.
                    if(Sweep_Reenter){
                        local ErrRight = (BaseYaw + SweepRange) - CurYaw
                        local ErrLeft  = (BaseYaw - SweepRange) - CurYaw
                        while(ErrRight > 180){ ErrRight -= 360 }
                        while(ErrRight < -180){ ErrRight += 360 }
                        while(ErrLeft > 180){ ErrLeft -= 360 }
                        while(ErrLeft < -180){ ErrLeft += 360 }
                        Sweep_Dir = (abs(ErrRight) <= abs(ErrLeft)) ? 1 : -1
                        Sweep_Pausing = 0
                        Sweep_Reenter = 0
                    }

                    local SideYaw = BaseYaw + Sweep_Dir * SweepRange

                    if(Sweep_Pausing){
                        DesiredAngle = ang(BasePitch, SideYaw, BaseRoll)
                        if(curtime() >= Sweep_PauseUntil){
                            Sweep_Pausing = 0
                            Sweep_Dir = -Sweep_Dir
                        }
                    }else{
                        local YawErr = SideYaw - CurYaw
                        while(YawErr > 180){ YawErr -= 360 }
                        while(YawErr < -180){ YawErr += 360 }

                        local MaxStep = max(Sweep_Speed_Side, 1) * Aim_Rate
                        if(abs(YawErr) <= MaxStep){
                            DesiredAngle = ang(BasePitch, SideYaw, BaseRoll)
                            # Pause only when the physical gun has actually arrived
                            local RealErr = SideYaw - Angle:yaw()
                            while(RealErr > 180){ RealErr -= 360 }
                            while(RealErr < -180){ RealErr += 360 }
                            if(abs(RealErr) < 2.5){
                                Sweep_Pausing = 1
                                Sweep_PauseUntil = curtime() + Sweep_End_Pause
                            }
                        }else{
                            local Step = (YawErr > 0) ? MaxStep : -MaxStep
                            DesiredAngle = ang(BasePitch, CurYaw + Step, BaseRoll)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Angle = slerp(quat(Angle), quat(DesiredAngle), AimAlpha):toAngle()

    # Drive yaw plate + elevation from BaseEnt-local pitch/yaw (atan2 -> degrees)
    local WorldFwd = Angle:forward()
    local LD2 = BaseEnt:toLocalAxis(WorldFwd)
    if(LD2:length() < 0.001){ LD2 = vec(1, 0, 0) }
    LD2 = LD2:normalized()
    local DriveYaw = atan2(LD2:y(), LD2:x()) * 180 / _PI
    local DriveHoriz = sqrt(LD2:x() * LD2:x() + LD2:y() * LD2:y())
    local DrivePitch = atan2(-LD2:z(), max(DriveHoriz, 0.0001)) * 180 /_PI

    holoEntity(8500):setAng(BaseEnt:toWorld(ang(0, DriveYaw, 0)))
    holoEntity(8600):setAng(holoEntity(8500):toWorld(ang(DrivePitch, 0, 0)))
    if(Gun:isValid()){
        Gun:setAng(holoEntity(8600):angles())
    }

    # Accumulate in BASE-LOCAL heading (DriveYaw), not world yaw, so the
    # counter cog keeps counter-rotating no matter how the base is mounted
    # (floor, wall, or ceiling/flipped). World yaw flips sign on an upside
    # down mount, which made the counter cog spin WITH the main cog.
    local YawDelta = DriveYaw - CogPrevYaw
    if(CogPrevYaw != 9999){
        while(YawDelta > 180){ YawDelta -= 360 }
        while(YawDelta < -180){ YawDelta += 360 }
        CogAccumAngle = CogAccumAngle + YawDelta
    }
    CogPrevYaw = DriveYaw
    holoAng(999887, holoEntity(9895):toWorld(ang(0, -CogAccumAngle+6 * 1.2, 0)))
}

function void losTick(){
    if(!Target:isValid() || !Gun:isValid()){ return }
    if(!hasLineOfSight(Target)){
        if(DebugMode == 1){ print("Target blocked - releasing") }
        releaseTarget(0)
        return
    }
    if(!isInTurretRange(Target) || (Target:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length() > Dist){
        releaseTarget(0)
    }
}

function void aimTick(){
    if(!BaseEnt:isValid() || !holoEntity(8600):isValid()){ return }

    GunHoloForward = holoEntity(8600):forward()
    Gun_Pos = Gun:isValid() ? Gun:pos() : holoEntity(8600):pos()

    if(Target:isValid()){
        if(!Target:isAlive() || Target:health() <= 0){
            if(DebugMode == 1){ print("Target eliminated - pacing " + Retarget_Delay + "s before next") }
            releaseTarget(1)
        }elseif(!isValidTarget(Target)){
            releaseTarget(0)
        }
    }elseif(HasValidTarget){
        # Target entity removed from world  treat as kill, apply retarget delay
        releaseTarget(1)
    }

    if(Target:isValid()){
        HasValidTarget = 1
        updateKinematics()
        # Stop searching beep when we have a target
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep") }
    }else{
        HasValidTarget = 0
        TargetLocation = vec()
        if(Fire || Fire_Pending){ fireStopDelayed() }

        # Start searching beep if armed and no target
        if(IdleSearchBeep_Enabled && TurretActive_Real && Power_Available){
            if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep")){
                timer(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep", IdleSearchBeep_Interval, 0, function(){ idleSearchBeepTick() })
            }
        }else{
            if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep") }
        }

        if(DebugMode == 1){
            holoPos(2, vec())
            holoPos(3, vec())
            holoPos(4, vec())
            holoPos(6, vec())
        }
    }

    applyAim()

    if(Target:isValid() && TargetLocation:length() > 0){
        local GunToTarget = (TargetLocation - Gun:pos()):normalized()
        if(GunToTarget:dot(GunHoloForward) > Accuracy_Scale){
            fireStart()
        }
    }

    X = TargetLocation:x()
    Y = TargetLocation:y()
    Z = TargetLocation:z()
    TargetNumber = Fire

    servoSound()
}

function void startTimers(){
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "aim")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "aim") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "scan")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "scan") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "los")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "los") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "power")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "power") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "status")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "status") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "halo")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "halo") }

    timer(TimerPrefix + "aim",    Aim_Rate,    0, function(){ aimTick() })
    timer(TimerPrefix + "scan",   Scan_Rate,   0, function(){ scanTick() })
    timer(TimerPrefix + "los",    LOS_Rate,    0, function(){ losTick() })
    timer(TimerPrefix + "power",  Power_Rate,  0, function(){ powerTick() })
    timer(TimerPrefix + "status", Status_Rate, 0, function(){ statusTick() })
    timer(TimerPrefix + "halo",   max(Halo_Refresh_Delay, 0.02), 0, function(){ haloTick() })
}

# ============================================================
# UTILITY / DISPLAY
# ============================================================

# Chained target-list print, now fully nested lambda timers (no clk timers)

# Line of sight from the gun to a candidate

# ============================================================
# TARGET QUEUE  (sorted: nearest to base first, optional threat weighting)
# ============================================================

# Lower score = engaged first.
# Priority_Mode 0 -> pure distance from base
# Priority_Mode 1 -> distance minus how far the target closes on the base in Threat_Lead seconds

# Re-score and prune the existing queue without spending find quota.
# Keeps "nearest to base first" accurate between actual find calls.

# findInCone costs 4 of the chip's find credits and credits regenerate at
# 1 per wire_expression2_find_rate (0.05s default, max 10). Querying without
# credit throws a runtime error and kills the chip, so gate every find here.


# ============================================================
# INIT
# ============================================================

    # Gun systems timer is started later in if(first()) after gunTick() is defined
    # (early registration caused: No such function: gunTick())
    # if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "gun") }
    # timer(TimerPrefix + "gun", 0.05, 0, function(){ gunTick() })

 #   BaseEnt:emitSound("@buttons/bell1.wav", 80, 100, 0.8)
    printColor(vec(0,255,200), "[BOOT] Turret system initializing...")

    # whitelist/find filters can wait a tick for props to settle
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "setup")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "setup") }
    Setup_Done = 0
    timer(TimerPrefix + "setup", 0.35, function(){
        setupWhitelist()

        # ---- Boot finalize (replaces the old "reset a second time" habit) ----
        # Props/wires often land a tick late on first paste. Re-seat everything
        # now that holos exist, restore ammo if a mag is present, and restart
        # the lambda timers so scan/aim actually run against a live target list.
        if(Gun:isValid()){ parentGun() }
        recoverAmmoBox()
        if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid() && !MagSeated){
            seatAmmoProp(Ammo_Prop:entity())
        }
        if(MagEnt:isValid() && MagSeated){
            if(Max_Ammo <= 0){ Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds) }
            if(Ammo_Count <= 0){ Ammo_Count = Max_Ammo }
        }
        if(Gun:isValid()){ parentBattery() }
        if(Prop:isValid()){ parentProp() }

        Setup_Done = 1
        startTimers()
        setupTurretHUD()

        # Default is always OFF after boot. Do not arm just because battery/power
        # came online - user must press EGP POWER / toggle the wired input.
        RemoteState = 0
        EGP_PowerOn = 0
        TurretActive_Real = 0

        printColor(vec(0,255,255), "=== TURRET ONLINE (lambda timer core) ===")
        printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Weapon Type: " + WeaponType:upper())
        printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Aim loop: " + round(1 / Aim_Rate) + " Hz | Scan loop: " + round(1 / Scan_Rate) + " Hz")
        printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Retarget delay after a kill: " + Retarget_Delay + "s  (!setretarget <n>)")
        printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Priority: NEAREST TO BASE  (!priority 1 for threat mode)")
        printColor(vec(255,170,0), "Help mode: " + Help_Aggressiveness + "  (!helpmode 0/1/2)  force=" + Help_Force_Dist + "u  ratio=" + Help_Prefer_Ratio)
        printColor(WeaponSender_Enabled ? vec(0,255,0) : vec(255,150,0), "Weapon Sender 1-click remote: " + (WeaponSender_Enabled ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED"))
        printColor(RemoteToggleEnabled ? vec(0,255,0) : vec(255,150,0), "EGP remote toggle: " + (RemoteToggleEnabled ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED"))
        printColor(vec(0,200,255), "Name: " + BaseName + "  |  ID: " + UniqueID + "  (multi-turret safe)")
        printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[BOOT] Battery: " + (BatteryEnt:isValid() ? "OK" : "MISSING") + " | Mag: " + (MagSeated ? (Ammo_Count + "/" + Max_Ammo) : "none") + " | Gun: " + (Gun:isValid() ? "OK" : "MISSING") + " | Power: " + (Power_Available ? "YES" : "NO"))
        printColor(vec(0,255,255), "=========================================")
        if(!BatteryEnt:isValid()){
            printColor(vec(255,50,50), "[WARN] No battery found yet - will keep searching. Targeting starts once battery + ammo are ready.")
        }
    })

    # re-parent on reset, exactly like the original did with timer(TimerPrefix + "Reparent_",200)
    # and timer(TimerPrefix + "seatParent3",200) - Prop/Gun/Battery come through changed() below
    if(RechargeStation:isValid()){
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "reparent_boot")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "reparent_boot") }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "reparent_boot", 0.2, function(){ parentCharger() })
    }

    if(EGP:isValid()){
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "egp_boot")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "egp_boot") }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "egp_boot", 0.2, function(){ parentEGP() })
    }

    # The input event only fires on a change, so anything already wired at
    # reset is re-parented here (the old code got this from changed() firing
    # on the first execution).
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "boot_parent")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "boot_parent") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "boot_parent", 0.05, function(){
        if(Prop != noentity()){ parentProp() }
        if(Gun != noentity()){ parentGun() }
        # Battery only after gun is present
        if(Gun:isValid()){ parentBattery() }
    })
}

# ============================================================
# INPUT EDGES - native input event, no changed() polling
# ============================================================

# Desired = 1  try arm, Desired = 0  force offline
# Caller is responsible for power checks when Desired == 1 (remote path does this)

event input(InputName:string){
    if(InputName == "TurretActive"){
        # The wired TurretActive input controls the turret when no remote source
        # is enabled (both toggles off). It is not held by the reset-arm lock.
        if(WeaponSender_Enabled != 1 && RemoteToggleEnabled != 1){
            applyTurretState(TurretActive)
        }
    }elseif(InputName == "Prop"){
        if(Prop != noentity()){ parentProp() }
    }elseif(InputName == "Gun"){
        if(Gun:isValid()){
            local GID = Gun:id()
            if(GID != GunMarkedID){
                GunMarkedID = GID
                if(Setup_Done){
                    seatGunAssembly("Gun input wired (id " + GID + ")")
                }
                # If still booting, gunLinkWatcher pending timer will rebuild after Setup_Done
            }else{
                # Same gun re-fired - soft seat only
                parentGun()
                MaG_PoS = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagPos1 : MagPos0
                Mag_Ang = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagAng1 : MagAng0
                recoverAmmoBox()
                if(MagEnt:isValid()){ repositionMagOnly(MagEnt) }
                parentBattery()
            }
        }
    }elseif(InputName == "Battery"){
        if(Battery != noentity() && Gun:isValid()){ parentBattery() }
        elseif(Battery != noentity() && !Gun:isValid()){
            printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[BATTERY] Wire the Gun first - battery will seat after the gun is linked")
        }
    }elseif(InputName == "EGP"){
        if(EGP != noentity()){ parentEGP() }
    }elseif(InputName == "RechargeStation"){
        if(RechargeStation != noentity()){ parentCharger() }
    }
}

# ============================================================
# CHAT COMMANDS
# ============================================================
event chat(Player:entity, Message_Raw:string, Team:number){
    if(Player == Owner){
        local Message = Message_Raw:lower()
        local Args = Message:explode(" ")

        if(Message == "!battery" || Message == "!power"){
            hideChat(1)
            printColor(vec(0,255,255), "========== BATTERY STATUS ==========")

            printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Battery Voltage: " + round(Battery_Voltage, 2) + "V")
            printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Status: " + Battery_Status)

            printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Charger In Range: " + (Recharge_In_Range ? "YES" : "NO"))

        }

        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setdelay" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Target_Acquisition_Delay = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 0, 10)
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Acquisition delay set to " + Target_Acquisition_Delay + "s")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setturnon" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            TurnOn_Delay = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 0, 10)

        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setturnoff" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            TurnOff_Delay = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 0, 10)
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Sleep droop delay set to " + TurnOff_Delay + "s")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setsweepdelay" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Sweep_Delay = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 0, 10)
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Pre-sweep delay set to " + Sweep_Delay + "s")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setsweep" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)

        }

        }
