        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setaimrate" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            setAimRate(Args[2,string]:toNumber())
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Aim loop set to " + Aim_Rate + "s (" + round(1 / Aim_Rate) + " Hz)")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setscanrate" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            setScanRate(Args[2,string]:toNumber())
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Scan loop set to " + Scan_Rate + "s (" + round(1 / Scan_Rate) + " Hz)")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!halo" && Args:count() >= 2){
                    hideChat(1)
                    Halo_Refresh_Delay = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 0.02, 2)
                    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "halo")){ timerSetDelay(TimerPrefix + "halo", max(Halo_Refresh_Delay, 0.02)) }
                    printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Halo refresh set to " + Halo_Refresh_Delay + "s (" + round(1 / Halo_Refresh_Delay) + " Hz)")
                }
                elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setspeed" && Args:count() >= 2){
                    hideChat(1)
                    BoltSpeedServerConfig = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 100, 20000)
                    BoltSpeed = BoltSpeedServerConfig
                   # ProjectileSpeed = BoltSpeedServerConfig
                    printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Bolt speed set to " + BoltSpeedServerConfig)
                }
                elseif(Args[1,string] == "!priority" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Priority_Mode = Args[2,string]:toNumber() ? 1 : 0
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Priority: " + (Priority_Mode ? "THREAT (closing speed weighted)" : "NEAREST TO BASE"))
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!setmaxtargets" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Max_Targets = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 1, 30)
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Max queued targets: " + Max_Targets)
        }
        elseif(Message == "!w" || Message == "!whitelist"){
            hideChat(1)
            displayWhitelist()
        }
        elseif(Message == "!targets" || Message == "!targetlist"){
            hideChat(1)
            displayTargetList()
        }
        elseif(Message == "!debug"){
            hideChat(1)
            DebugMode = !DebugMode
            printColor(vec(255,255,0), "Debug mode: " + (DebugMode ? "ON" : "OFF"))
        }
        elseif(Message == "!scan"){
            hideChat(1)
            if(!canScan()){
                printColor(vec(255,150,0), "Find budget busy - showing last queue")
            }
            buildTargetQueue()
            printColor(vec(0,255,255), "========== SCAN (" + TargetQueueSize + " targets) ==========")
            for(I = 1, TargetQueueSize){
                local Q = TargetQueue[I, entity]
                printColor(vec(255,100,100), "[TARGET] " + shortenTargetName(Q) + " (" + floor(toUnit("ft", (Q:pos() - BaseEnt:pos()):length())) + " ft)")
            }
            printColor(vec(0,255,255), "====================================")
        }
        elseif(Message == "!restart"){
            hideChat(1)
            startTimers()
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Lambda timers restarted")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!helpmode" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Help_Aggressiveness = clamp(floor(Args[2,string]:toNumber()), 0, 2)
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Help mode: " + Help_Aggressiveness + "  (0=only if idle, 1=prefer nearby shared, 2=aggressive focus-fire)")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!helpforce" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Help_Force_Dist = max(0, Args[2,string]:toNumber())
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Help force-distance set to " + Help_Force_Dist + " units (always consider help this close)")
        }
        elseif(Args[1,string] == "!helpratio" && Args:count() >= 2){
            hideChat(1)
            Help_Prefer_Ratio = clamp(Args[2,string]:toNumber(), 0.5, 3.0)
            printColor(vec(0,255,0), "Help prefer ratio set to " + Help_Prefer_Ratio + " (claimed <= free * ratio)")
        }
        elseif(Message == "*delete"){
            hideChat(1)
            printColor(vec(255,100,0), "* Dissolving turret E2 chip...")
            # Dissolves the E2 itself (electrical dissolve fx, 1 = ELECTRICAL). Undone by nothing lol
            entity():propDissolve(1)
        }
        elseif(Message == "!help"){
            hideChat(1)
            printColor(vec(100,200,255), "========== TURRET COMMANDS ==========")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !battery            - battery status")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !targeting          - targeting settings")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !queue              - live target queue")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !scan               - rescan and list")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !rates              - lambda timer rates")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setretarget <n>    - delay between kill and next target")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setdelay <n>       - delay after losing a target")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setturnon <n>      - wake center delay (s)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setturnoff <n>     - sleep droop delay (s)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setsweepdelay <n>  - pre-sweep delay after wake (s)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setsweep <n>       - master on/off + sine speed (0 = off)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setsweepside <n>   - side-to-side travel speed (mode 1)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setsweepmode <0|1> - 0=sine, 1=side-to-side + end pause")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setsweeppause <n>  - hold time at each side (mode 1)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setaimrate <n>     - aim loop period (s)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setscanrate <n>    - scan loop period (s)")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !halo <n>           - halo re-apply refresh period (s)")
                        printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setspeed <n>       - bolt speed (100-20000)")
                        printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !priority <0|1>     - nearest-to-base / threat mode")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !setmaxtargets <n>  - queue depth")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !helpmode <0|1|2>  - how often to stack on teammate targets")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !helpforce <n>     - always help if claimed target within n units")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !helpratio <n>     - help if claimed dist <= free dist * n")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  !debug, !restart, !whitelist, !targets")
            printColor(vec(200,200,200), "  *delete             - dissolve this E2 chip (irreversible, no undo)")
            printColor(vec(100,200,255), "====================================")
        }
    }

}
event playerUse(Ply:entity, Ent:entity){
    if(Ent != EGP && Ent != entity()){ exit() }
    if(!EGP:isValid()){ exit() }
    if(Ply:shootPos():distance(EGP:pos()) > EGP_UseRange){ exit() }
    egpTouchHandlePress(Ply)
}

event removed(Resetting:number){
    # Tear down the target-lock HUD (HUD overlay + emitter).
    disableTurretHUD()
    # De-parent the battery immediately so the engine does not delete it with the holos
    if(BatteryEnt:isValid()){
        BatteryEnt:deparent()
        BatteryEnt:propFreeze(1)

        # Namespaced timer so multi-turret setups don't stomp each other
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "reparent_battery_delay")){
            stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "reparent_battery_delay")
        }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "reparent_battery_delay", 1.0, function(){
            if(BatteryEnt:isValid() && !isOwnedElsewhere(BatteryEnt) && !isMountedByOtherTurret(BatteryEnt)){
                # Holos will have been rebuilt by the time this fires on a normal reset
                parentBattery()
                # Leave frozen while seated (matches lamp/mag behaviour)
            }
        })
    }
    # Existing cleanup code
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "gun") }
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "idle_search_beep") }
    stopAllTimers()
    if(EGP_ScreenCreated && EGP:isValid()){
        EGP_Screen:egpClear()
    }
    Prop:deparent()
    Gun:deparent()
    # Battery already handled above - do NOT deparent again here
    EGP:deparent()
    RechargeStation:deparent()

    # ---- merged from AIMGUNSIMPLE's event removed() ----
    if(MagEnt:isValid()){
        MagEntIndex = MagEnt:id()
        MagEnt:propFreeze(1)
    }
}  


if(duped() | dupefinished()) { 
    WasDupeReset = 1
    reset()
}

if(first()){
LampParented = 0
    
}




# ============================================================
# GUN / AMMO SYSTEM
# (ordered so every function is defined before lambdas that call it)
# ============================================================



if(first()) {
    if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun")){ timer(TimerPrefix + "gun", 0.01, 0, function(){ gunTick() }) }
    # Auto re-init if the wired Gun prop is detected after the chip already
    # booted (previously: manual reset needed after wiring the gun). Re-arms
    # whenever the gun is removed and a new one takes its place.
    # Pre-arm to the gun already wired at boot so gunLinkWatcher does NOT
    # immediately reset() in a loop. Wiring a gun LATER still changes the ID
    # and triggers requestGunRebuild() -> reset().
    GunMarkedID = Gun:isValid() ? Gun:id() : -1
    if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun_link")){ timer(TimerPrefix + "gun_link", 0.1, 0, function(){ gunLinkWatcher() }) }
    # Gun already present at boot: make sure ammo + battery still seat (holos
    # were built in this same first() pass, so parent is enough - no reset needed)
    if(Gun:isValid()){
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun_boot_seat")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "gun_boot_seat") }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "gun_boot_seat", 0.05, function(){
            parentGun()
            # Re-resolve mag offsets for the active gun mode (set in this first() pass)
            MaG_PoS = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagPos1 : MagPos0
            Mag_Ang = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagAng1 : MagAng0
            recoverAmmoBox()
            if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()){
                local AE = Ammo_Prop:entity()
                if(!MagSeated || magNeedsRepos()){
                    seatAmmoProp(AE)
                }elseif(MagEnt:isValid()){
                    repositionMagOnly(MagEnt)
                }
            }elseif(MagEnt:isValid()){
                repositionMagOnly(MagEnt)
            }
            parentBattery()
            # Force gun model visibility for the active mode
            if(MachineGunMode == 1){
                if(holoEntity(40):isValid()){ holoEntity(40):propDraw(1) holoAlpha(40, 255) }
                if(holoEntity(20):isValid()){ holoEntity(20):propDraw(0) }
            }else{
                if(holoEntity(20):isValid()){ holoEntity(20):propDraw(1) holoAlpha(20, 255) }
            }
            printColor(vec(0,255,150), "[GUN] Boot seat done - gun/mag/battery")
        })
        # Second pass a bit later - AdvDupe/wire often lands the mag one tick late
        if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "gun_boot_seat2")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "gun_boot_seat2") }
        timer(TimerPrefix + "gun_boot_seat2", 0.2, function(){
            if(!Gun:isValid()){ return }
            MaG_PoS = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagPos1 : MagPos0
            Mag_Ang = MachineGunMode == 1 ? MagAng1 : MagAng0
            recoverAmmoBox()
            if(MagEnt:isValid()){
                repositionMagOnly(MagEnt)
            }elseif(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()){
                seatAmmoProp(Ammo_Prop:entity())
            }
            parentBattery()
        })
    }
    # Same for Battery: auto-parent when wired (or found nearby) after boot.
    # Left at -1 (not pre-armed to whatever's already wired) so batteryLinkWatcher()
    # is guaranteed to fire once on the very first resolve, whether that's a
    # pre-existing wire connection or a proximity find.
    BatteryMarkedID = -1
    if(!timerExists(TimerPrefix + "battery_link")){ timer(TimerPrefix + "battery_link", 0.1, 0, function(){ batteryLinkWatcher() }) }
    JustReset = curtime() + 3.0
    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")
    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "reload_tick")
    Auto_Reload_Triggered = 0
    Auto_Reload_Paused = 0
    Refilling = 0

    Max_rounds = 4500
    Ammo_Dock_Radius = 300
    Ammo_Dock_Marker_Scale = 0.0047
    recoverAmmoBox()

    # ---- Robust wired-box recovery (mirrors what the simple standalone
    # AIMGUNSIMPLE does on boot, but retried instead of checked once) ----
    # After an Adv Dupe 2 paste, wire connections across a big multi-entity
    # dupe (Base/Prop/Gun/Battery/RechargeStation/EGP/Ammo_Prop/Reload_Prop
    # all wired at once) don't all re-establish on the exact same tick. A
    # single check right here in first() can run before Ammo_Prop's wire
    # has actually reconnected, so it silently finds nothing and never
    # gets another chance. This timer instead keeps checking every 0.1s
    # until the box is seated, then stops itself.
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover") }
    # Repeating recovery for ~8s after spawn/dupe  covers late wire + entity ID remap
    timer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover", 0.15, 0, function(){
        if(MagSeated && MagEnt:isValid() && Gun:isValid()){
            if(isOwnedElsewhere(MagEnt)){
                MagSeated = 0
                MagEnt = noentity()
                MagEntIndex = 0
                Ammo_Count = 0
                showAmmoDockMarker()
            }elseif(magNeedsRepos()) {
                repositionMagOnly(MagEnt)
            }
            if(curtime() > JustReset) {
                stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_prop_recover")
            }
        }else{
            recoverAmmoBox()
            if(Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid() && !MagSeated){
                seatAmmoProp(Ammo_Prop:entity())
            }
        }
    })



    # ---- Robust lamp recovery (mirrors the ammo box) ----
    # After a reset/dupe the lamp's parent link to the mount holo (42) can be
    # broken or late to re-establish. Keep re-seating it for a while.
    if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "lamp_recover")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "lamp_recover") }
    timer(TimerPrefix + "lamp_recover", 0.15, 0, function(){
        if(LampParented && LampCache:isValid() && holoEntity(42):isValid()){
            local Ppar = Gun:isValid() ? Gun : (holoEntity(20):isValid() ? holoEntity(20) : entity())
            if(LampCache:parent() != Ppar || LampCache:pos():distance(holoEntity(42):pos()) > 25) {
                repositionLampOnly(LampCache)
            }
            if(curtime() > JustReset) {
                stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "lamp_recover")
            }
        }else{
            recoverLamp()
            if(Lamp:isValid() && !LampParented){
                seatLampProp(Lamp)
            }
        }
    })

    if(Reload_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        timer(TimerPrefix + "Reparent_reloading", 0.05, function() {
            if(Reload_Prop:entity():isValid() && holoEntity(30000):isValid()) {
                Reload_Prop:entity():propFreeze(1)
                Reload_Prop:entity():setPos(holoEntity(30000):pos())
                Reload_Prop:entity():setAng(holoEntity(30000):angles())
                Reload_Prop:entity():propNotSolid(0)
                Reload_Prop:entity():parentTo(holoEntity(30000))
                Reload_Prop:entity():propFreeze(0)
                Reload_Prop:entity():setModelScale(vec(0.45))
                Reload_Prop:entity():setPhysScale(0.36)
                hint("reloading prop re-parented after reset", 2)
            }
        })
    }

    print(BaseName + " E2 joined " + DS_ResetName + " (multi-turret safe)")

    Scale = 0.2

    EnableBrass = 1
    BrassEjectHolo = 9002   
    Reload_Distance_Feet = 10
    Reload_Speed = 5
    Reload_Tick_Delay = 70
    Auto_Reload_Enabled = 0

        if(E:isValid()) { MagEnt = E }

    if(!IsDupeRecovery && !MagEnt:isValid() && Ammo_Prop:entity():isValid()) {
        MagEnt = Ammo_Prop:entity()
        MagEntIndex = MagEnt:id()
    }

        MagSeated = 0
    } elseif(MagEnt:isValid() && MagEnt:model():lower():find("boxsrounds")) {
        HadMagOnReset = 1
    } elseif(PreResetMagSeated && PreResetMagIndex > 0) {

if(!HadMagOnReset && !MagEnt:isValid()) {

    printColor(vec(255,200,0), "[AMMO] No valid mag after reset - dock scan will start after grace") 
} else { 
    MagSeated = 1 
    Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0 
    stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "ammo_dock_scan")

    if(MagEnt:isValid()) { MagEntIndex = MagEnt:id() } 
    if(PreResetAmmoCount > 0) {  
        Ammo_Count = PreResetAmmoCount 
        Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds)  
    } else {  
        Ammo_Count = floor(Max_rounds) 
        Max_Ammo = floor(Max_rounds)  
    } 
    printColor(vec(0,255,100), "[AMMO] Mag recovered on reset (index " + MagEntIndex + ", ammo " + Ammo_Count + ")") 
}

    if(Lamp:isValid()) {
        LampCache = Lamp
        LampEnt = Lamp

        LampParented = 0

            LampEnt = LE

            LampParented = 0

    if(HadLampOnReset) {
        # Seated flag cleared so first()/gunTick reseat hooks re-run and re-parent

        # actual repositioning once the mount holo exists.

    # Target halo ([Wiremod]Expression2 halos addon) - size/passes control thickness
    # Crisp L4D2-style: thin solid outline. Higher passes = solid edge (not fuzzy glow).
    HaloEnabled = 0
    Halo_Refresh_Delay = 0.01   # how often the halo re-applies to the current target
    HaloSize = 1.0       # outline spread per pass (higher = thicker)
    HaloPasses = 8       # stacked passes (higher = denser/solid edge, lower = thinner)
    HaloAlpha = 255      # opacity (lower = more see-through; 255 = solid)
    HaloAdditive = 1
    HaloIgnoreZ = 1   # 1 = render halo through walls
    HaloColor = vec(255, 255, 0)   # bright green
    # Distance handling - the KEY to crispness at range:
    #   HaloSize = BLUR radius. Growing it at distance = fat fuzzy glow (the bug).
    #   So keep HaloSize thin (crisp) at all ranges and instead boost HaloPasses
    #   at distance to keep the thin line SOLID/BRIGHT so far targets don't fade out.
    #   Up to HaloDistFloor it stays at base HaloSize/HaloPasses (uniform).
    #   Beyond that passes ramp to HaloPasses*HaloPassMax at max range (Dist).
    #   HaloDistanceScale = 1 -> use floor logic; 0 = fixed everywhere
    #   HaloDistFloor = distance (units) where scaling starts
    #   HaloDistMax = size multiplier at range (keep = 1 so blur stays thin/crisp)
    #   HaloPassMax = passes multiplier at range (higher = stays solid when far)
    HaloDistanceScale = 1
    HaloDistMax = 1
    HaloPassMax = 3
    HaloDistFloor = 1000

    # Per-NPC crispness overrides - for small/fast NPCs that look fuzzy at their size.
    # Same HaloColor/alpha as normal; only Size and Passes change (crisper/denser edge).
    SmallHaloEnabled = 0
    SmallHaloSize = 0.6     # thinner blur so tiny targets keep a crisp outline
    SmallHaloPasses = 4     # more passes so the thin line stays solid on small bodies

    holoCreate(44, holoEntity(42):toWorld(AccLampHolderP*Scale), LampHolderScale*Scale, holoEntity(42):toWorld(AccLampHolderA), vec(150), LampHolderModel)

        holoEntity(600003):propDraw(0)
        holoEntity(95000):propDraw(0)
        # 30mm rotary barrels (Barrel_type 2) are only created in that branch
        if(Barrel_type == 2){
            holoEntity(6000):propDraw(0)
            holoEntity(6002):propDraw(0)
        }
        # Barrel_type 3 tubes/barrels are only created in that branch
        if(Barrel_type == 3){
            holoEntity(20001):propDraw(0)
            holoEntity(20002):propDraw(0)
            holoEntity(20003):propDraw(0)
            holoEntity(200021):propDraw(0)
            holoEntity(200022):propDraw(0)
            holoEntity(200023):propDraw(0)
            for(I=0,Barrel_Count-1){
                holoEntity(99 + I):propDraw(0)
            }
        }
        # Machine-gun body must stay visible (chain-gun set is hidden above)
        if(holoEntity(40):isValid()){
            holoEntity(40):propDraw(1)
            holoAlpha(40, 255)
        }
    }else{
        if(holoEntity(20):isValid()){
            holoEntity(20):propDraw(1)
            holoAlpha(20, 255)
        }
    }

    # Light-base holder (holo 1050) - the light_spotlight02_base model that sits
    # with the ammo box. It parents to the STABLE Gun / gun-model hologram (NOT
    # the box) so deleting the box never destroys the hologram - it always stays
    # visible and just tracks the box's base position/angle via the per-gun
    # offsets AmmoBase_Pos0/1 + AmmoBase_Ang0/1.
    holoCreate(1050)
    holoModel(1050, "models/props_wasteland/light_spotlight02_base.mdl")
    holoScale(1050, vec(2, 5, 2)*Scale)
    holoMaterial(1050, "models/props_pipes/Pipesystem01a_skin2")
    updateLightBaseParent()
    holoEntity(1050):propDraw(1)

    local E2Bottom = -entity():boxSize():z() / 2
    local DockOffset = vec(0, -28, E2Bottom + 0.5)

    holoCreate(56)
    holoModel(56, "cube")
    holoMaterial(56, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoColor(56, vec(255, 0, 255))
    holoAlpha(56, 0)
    holoScale(56, vec(1.5, 1.5, 1.5))
    holoPos(56, entity():toWorld(DockOffset))
    holoAng(56, entity():angles())
    holoParent(56, entity())
    holoDisableShading(56, 1)

    holoCreate(5601)
    holoModel(5601, "cube")
    holoMaterial(5601, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoColor(5601, vec(0, 255, 255))
    holoAlpha(5601, 0)
    holoScale(5601, vec(1.0, 1.25, 1.0))
    holoPos(5601, holoEntity(56):pos())
    holoAng(5601, holoEntity(56):angles())
    holoParent(5601, holoEntity(56))
    holoDisableShading(5601, 1)

    holoCreate(5602)
    holoModel(5602, "hq_cylinder")
    holoMaterial(5602, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoColor(5602, vec(0, 255, 0))
    holoAlpha(5602, 0)
    holoScale(5602, vec(Ammo_Dock_Radius * Ammo_Dock_Marker_Scale, Ammo_Dock_Radius * Ammo_Dock_Marker_Scale, 0.1))
    holoPos(5602, holoEntity(56):pos())
    holoAng(5602, holoEntity(56):angles())
    holoParent(5602, holoEntity(56))
    holoDisableShading(5602, 1)


    timer(TimerPrefix + "force_visible", 0.4, 0, function() {
        if(Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop) {
            if(holoEntity(5601):isValid()) { 
                holoAlpha(5601, 0)
                holoColor(5601, vec(0, 255, 255))
                holoScale(5602, vec(1.4, 1.4, 0.1))
            }
            if(holoEntity(5602):isValid()) { 
                holoAlpha(5602, 255)
                holoColor(5602, vec(0, 255, 0))
                holoScale(5602, vec(Ammo_Dock_Radius * Ammo_Dock_Marker_Scale, Ammo_Dock_Radius * Ammo_Dock_Marker_Scale, 0.1))
            }
        } else {
            if(holoEntity(5601):isValid()) { holoAlpha(5601, 0) }
            if(holoEntity(5602):isValid()) { holoAlpha(5602, 0) }
        }
    })

    if(HadMagOnReset || MagEnt:isValid()) { 
        hideAmmoDockMarker()
        Ammo_Waiting_For_Prop = 0 
    } else { 
        showAmmoDockMarker() 
    }
    printColor(vec(0,255,0), "[AMMO] Dock created with official find method - look for CYAN + GREEN")

    holoCreate( 524000 )
    holoModel( 524000 ,"models/meem/parts/nixie_tube.mdl" )
    holoAng( 524000 , holoEntity(20):toWorld( ang( -90 ,0,0)))
    holoPos(524000, holoEntity(20):toWorld( vec( -30 ,0, 0)*Scale))
    holoScale( 524000 , vec( 4,4,2 )*Scale )
    holoParent(524000, holoEntity(20))

    holoCreate( 95000) ## no clue what the fuckj this is lol 
    holoModel( 95000,"cube" ) 
    holoScale( 95000 , vec(0  ) )
    holoAng( 95000 , holoEntity(20):toWorld( ang( 0 , 0, 0)))
    holoPos(95000,holoEntity(20):toWorld(vec(11.2,-3.55,-2)))
    holoDisableShading(95000,0)
    holoParent(95000,holoEntity(20))
    holoColor(95000,vec(255))

    holoCreate(3000)
    holoModel(3000, "models/hunter/blocks/cube1x1x1.mdl")
    holoPos(3000, Gun:toWorld(vec(-25, 10, 25)*Scale))
    holoAng(3000, Gun:toWorld(ang(0, 0, 0)))
    holoScale(3000, vec(0)*Scale)
    holoColor(3000, vec(0, 0, 0))
    holoMaterial(3000, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoParent(3000, Gun)
    holoAlpha(3000, 200)

    holoCreate(3001)
    holoModel(3001, "models/hunter/blocks/cube1x1x1.mdl")
    holoPos(3001, Gun:toWorld(vec(-25, 10, 20)*Scale))
    holoAng(3001, Gun:toWorld(ang(0, 0, 0)))
    holoScale(3001, vec(0)*Scale)
    holoColor(3001, vec(255, 0, 0))
    holoMaterial(3001, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoParent(3001, Gun)
    holoAlpha(3001, 0)

    holoCreate(3002)
    holoModel(3002, "models/hunter/blocks/cube1x1x1.mdl")
    holoPos(3002, Gun:toWorld(vec(-25, 10, 15)*Scale))
    holoAng(3002, Gun:toWorld(ang(0, 0, 0)))
    holoScale(3002, vec(0)*Scale)
    holoColor(3002, vec(0, 255, 0))
    holoMaterial(3002, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoParent(3002, Gun)
    holoAlpha(3002, 0)

    holoCreate(3003)
    holoModel(3003, "models/hunter/blocks/cube1x1x1.mdl")
    holoPos(3003, Gun:toWorld(vec(-25, 10, 30)*Scale))
    holoAng(3003, Gun:toWorld(ang(0, 0, 0)))
    holoScale(3003, vec(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)*Scale)
    holoColor(3003, vec(0, 0, 255))
    holoMaterial(3003, "models/debug/debugwhite")
    holoParent(3003, Gun)
    holoAlpha(3003, 0)
    
    # Deliberately no extra bolt muzzle offset cube here. The real firing origin is
    # holo 4580 itself; anything spawned in front of that point is a debug artifact.


    # Ranger holos removed with Xbolt system

    if(Auto_Reload_Enabled) { 
        holoColor(3000, vec(0, 255, 0))
        printColor(vec(0,255,255), "[AUTO-RELOAD] System ENABLED") 
    } else { 
        holoColor(3000, vec(50, 50, 50))
        printColor(vec(255,100,0), "[AUTO-RELOAD] System DISABLED") 
    }
}

event tick() {
    # ---- Remote 1-click toggle (weapon_wiresender) ----
    # Only active when WeaponSender_Enabled == 1 (set in first()).
    if(WeaponSender_Enabled == 1 && owner():isValid() && owner():weapon():isValid()){
        if(owner():weapon():getClass() == "weapon_wiresender"){
            local Fire = owner():keyAttack1()
            if(Fire & !LastFire){
                ClickCount++
                if(timerExists(TimerPrefix + "clickwindow")){ stoptimer(TimerPrefix + "clickwindow") }
                timer(TimerPrefix + "clickwindow", Delay_between_Clicks, function(){
                    if(ClickCount == 1){
                        local NewDesired = !RemoteState
                        if(NewDesired){
                            # Trying to turn ON  power must be available
                            if(!Power_Available){
                                if(!BatteryEnt:isValid()){
                                    printColor(vec(255,0,0), "Turret ACTIVATION BLOCKED - No battery connected!")
                                }else{
                                    printColor(vec(255,0,0), "Turret ACTIVATION BLOCKED - Insufficient Power! (" + round(Battery_Percent) + "%, need " + Battery_MinStartThreshold + "%)")
                                }
                                BaseEnt:emitSound("@hl1/fvox/power_level_is_unsafe.wav", 80, 80, 0.8)
                            }else{
                                RemoteState = 1
                                applyTurretState(1)
                            }
                        }else{
                            # Turning OFF  always allowed
                            RemoteState = 0
                            applyTurretState(0)
                        }
                    }
                    ClickCount = 0
                })
            }
            LastFire = Fire
        }
    }

    local ActiveEdge = changed(Active)
    local MzMode = MuzzleModeA[MachineGunMode + 1, number]
    if(Fire & MuzzleFlash_Enabled){
        if(MzMode == 0){
            # Retry SENT creation while firing if the previous spawn failed.
            if(ActiveEdge | !Entity:isValid()){
                Entity = sentSpawn(Model,holoEntity(4581):pos(),holoEntity(4581):angles(),1)
                if(Entity:isValid()){
                    Entity:parentTo(holoEntity(40))
                    Entity:noCollideAll(1)
                    Entity:propNotSolid(1)
                }
            } 
} elseif(MzMode == 3) {
    doMuzzleFlashEffect()

    local FlashPos = holoEntity(4581):pos()
    local FlashFwd = holoEntity(4581):forward()
    local FlashRight = holoEntity(4581):right()

    makeParticleSimple("effects/yellowflare", Sprite_col1, 255, 6, 0.05, FlashPos, FlashFwd * 40)
    makeParticleSimple("effects/yellowflare", Sprite_col2, 200, 10, 0.08, FlashPos + FlashRight * 1.5, FlashFwd * 25 + FlashRight * 8)
    makeParticleSimple("effects/yellowflare", Sprite_col2, 200, 10, 0.08, FlashPos - FlashRight * 1.5, FlashFwd * 25 - FlashRight * 8)

} elseif(MzMode == 4) {
    # The light is pulsed once per actual shot in Fire_PewPew.
}

    }elseif(!Active & MuzzleFlash_Enabled){
        if(MzMode == 0){
            if(!Fire & (Fire==0)){
                if(Entity:isValid()){
                    Entity:propDelete()
                    Entity:admPropDelete()
                    Entity:noCollideAll(1)
                    timer("KILL", 0.01, function(){
                        if(Entity:isValid()){ 
                            Entity:propDelete()
                            Entity:admPropDelete()
                            Entity:noCollideAll(1) 
                        }
                    })
                }
            }
        } elseif(MzMode == 2) { 
            lightToggle(LightBase + 1,0) 
        } elseif(MzMode == 3) {
            lightToggle(LightBase + 1,0)
        } elseif(MzMode == 4) {
            lightToggle(LightBase + 1,0)
        }
    }
}
if(EGP:isValid()){
EGP:noCollideAll(1)    
    
}
